### [1] [SCALE: Sensitivity-Aware Federated Unlearning with Information Freshness Optimization for Mobile Edge Computing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22589)
*Zihao Ding,Beining Wu,Jun Huang*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 提出SCALE框架，一种结合历史贡献分析和信息新鲜度感知自适应稀疏化的双层联邦遗忘方法，用于移动边缘计算中高效、精准的客户端数据选择性移除。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有联邦遗忘方法存在遗忘精度低、缺乏时间信息反映的问题，导致遗忘性能次优，无法满足移动边缘计算中对数据污染治理和隐私规约的要求。

Method: SCALE框架采用双层机制：首先通过基于历史贡献的层敏感性分析识别受目标客户端影响最大的层；然后在权重子组层面通过自适应稀疏化进行细粒度遗忘，平衡信息新鲜度与遗忘效果。

Result: 理论分析证明了收敛性和加速优势；实验与测试平台结果相比现有基线方法取得了更优的遗忘效果，遗忘性能显著提升。

Conclusion: SCALE通过融合历史贡献与信息新鲜度感知的自适应稀疏化，在联邦遗忘任务中实现了更高的遗忘精度和更好的时间信息反映能力，为MEC系统提供了一种有效的隐私保护方案。

Abstract: Federated Unlearning (FU) is emerging as a powerful tool that enables the selective removal of client data to effectively address data contamination and meet strict privacy regulations in mobile edge computing (MEC) systems. Although FU has recently drawn attention in the AI community, existing approaches suffer from low unlearning precision and lack temporal information reflection, which results in suboptimal forgetting performance. To address these issues, we propose SCALE, a dual-level unlearning framework combining historical contribution analysis with information freshness-aware adaptive sparsification. Our framework first employs a historical contribution-based layer sensitivity analysis to identify layers most influenced by target clients, then performs fine-grained unlearning through adaptive sparsification at the weight sub-group level to balance information freshness with forgetting effectiveness. Through theoretical analysis, the proposed framework demonstrates the convergence properties and acceleration advantages. Our experiments and testbed results demonstrate superior unlearning effectiveness compared to state-of-the-art baselines, with significantly improved forgetting performance.

</details>


### [2] [An intensive vRAN deployment with OpenAirInterface](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22638)
*Romain Beurdouche,Raymond Knopp*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文探讨了如何在5G虚拟化无线接入网（vRAN）中利用不同的计算机架构（通用处理器与硬件加速器）进行大规模部署，基于OpenAirInterface软件栈，通过适配和优化软件以充分利用硬件能力，并实现多个vRAN实例共享服务器的扩展部署，最后分享了性能观察结果和局限性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 5G vRAN对计算机架构提出了新挑战，需要选择或设计既能提供足够性能又能最大化灵活性和效率的计算技术。

Method: 基于OpenAirInterface 5G软件栈，适配软件以利用硬件加速能力，探索多个vRAN实例共享服务器的扩展部署方案。

Result: 改进了软件栈并观察了不同计算机架构对部署性能的影响。

Conclusion: 讨论了当前部署的局限性以及未来实现更好vRAN部署的进一步工作方向。

Abstract: The advent of 5G virtualized Radio Access Networks (vRANs) brings a new challenge with regards to computer architectures. It requires to select or design computing technologies that provide a sufficient level of performance while maximizing the flexibility and efficiency of the implemented networks. Several solutions addressing this challenge were proposed, relying on general purpose processors as well as hardware accelerators. This work describes our effort to enable an intensive vRAN deployment using the 5G software stack OpenAirInterface on top of these computer architectures. We had to adapt the software stack to leverage the capabilities of hardware and to find how to scale up the vRAN deployment with several vRAN instances sharing a server. We describe in this work our improvements to the stack and their effect on performance. We also share our observations on the behavior of the computer architectures and how they affect our deployment. We finally discuss the limitations of our deployment and further efforts to implement better vRAN deployments.

</details>

### [3] [Quantum Genetic Optimization for Negative Selection Algorithms in Anomaly Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22527)
*Giancarlo P. Gamberi,Calebe P. Bianchini*

Main category: cs.NE

TL;DR: 本文提出量子遗传负选择算法(QGNSA)，通过将量子遗传算法集成到EvoSeedRNSA中，利用量子叠加态和概率幅调整增强探测器生成效率，在元宇宙金融交易数据集上实现了比经典算法更优的异常检测性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统负选择算法在异常检测中受限于探测器生成的效率，亟需一种能够增强搜索空间探索能力和收敛效率的方法来突破这一瓶颈。

Method: 将量子遗传算法(QGA)集成到EvoSeedRNSA算法中，取代其经典进化优化过程，利用量子叠加态和概率幅调整来增强搜索空间探索和收敛效率。

Result: 在元宇宙金融交易数据集上，QGNSA相比经典算法实现了更优的异常检测准确率，并在不同超参数配置下保持鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 量子计算在人工免疫系统中具有潜在优势，尤其在高维异常检测任务中。未来工作将优化量子电路设计、在真实量子硬件上部署以及探索混合量子-经典方法。

Abstract: Negative Selection Algorithms (NSAs), inspired by the self/non-self discrimination mechanism of the human immune system, have been widely employed in anomaly detection. However, their effectiveness is often constrained by the efficiency of detector generation. This paper presents the Quantum Genetic Negative Selection Algorithm (QGNSA), a novel approach that integrates a Quantum Genetic Algorithm (QGA) into the EvoSeedRNSA algorithm, replacing its classical evolutionary optimization process. The proposed method exploits quantum superposition and probabilistic amplitude adjustment to enhance search space exploration and convergence efficiency in the detector generation process. Empirical evaluations using the Metaverse Financial Transactions Dataset demonstrate that QGNSA achieves superior anomaly detection accuracy compared to its classical counterpart while maintaining robustness under varying hyperparameter configurations. The experimental results highlight the potential advantages of quantum computing in artificial immune systems, particularly in high-dimensional anomaly detection tasks. Future research will focus on further optimizing quantum circuit design, deploying the algorithm on real quantum hardware, and exploring hybrid quantum-classical approaches for improved computational efficiency.

</details>

### [4] [Autonomous LLM Agents & CTFs: A Second Look](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21497)
*Youness Bouchari,Matteo Boffa,Marco Mellia,Idilio Drago,Thanh Minh Bui,Dario Rossi*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文重新审视了LLM智能体在自动化攻防安全任务中的表现，通过设计多种架构并在30个Web CTF挑战上进行测试，发现通用智能体Claude-code可作为强基线，当前智能体仍低于人类水平，且结构化编排优于单体设计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 近期研究声称LLM智能体在CTF挑战中达到了接近人类的成功率，本文旨在重新审视这些结论，通过更系统的实验设计验证其真实能力水平。

Method: 在覆盖14种漏洞类别的30个Web CTF挑战上，构建了逐步增加复杂度和模块化的多种智能体架构，使用多个LLM骨干进行实例化，并与通用智能体Claude-code进行对比评估。

Result: Claude-code解决了19/30的任务，与人工设计架构性能相当；所有架构在相同挑战类别上表现不佳，存在持久性障碍；结构化专业化角色编排优于单体设计，能提高运行一致性和降低执行成本。

Conclusion: 通用智能体是攻防安全任务的强基线；当前LLM智能体尚未达到人类水平；结构化多角色编排架构是提升性能的有效方向。

Abstract: Large Language Model (LLM) agents are increasingly proposed to automate offensive security tasks, with recent studies reporting near human-level success rates in Capture-the-Flag (CTF) challenges. We here revisit these results, providing a second look at these claims. We engineer different agent architectures of increasing complexity and modularity on 30 web-based CTFs challenges spanning 14 vulnerability classes. We instantiate these agents with multiple LLM backbones, and compare them with claude-code, a general-purpose agent that automatically determines its internal architecture. Our evaluation yields three main findings. First, claude-code achieves performance comparable to the engineered architectures (19/30 solved tasks), suggesting that general-purpose agents are strong baselines for offensive security tasks. Second, both our architectures and claude-code struggle in the same challenge categories, revealing persistent barriers that keep current agents below human-level capability. Third, by leveraging our manually designed architectures we can systematically measure the impact of additional components, finding that structured orchestration of specialized roles outperforms monolithic designs, improving run-to-run consistency, and reducing execution costs.

</details>


### [5] [ASSEMBLAGE-DEEPHISTORY: A Cross-Build Binary Dataset with Temporal Coverage](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21615)
*Chang Liu,Noah Fleischmann,Nicolò Altamura,Edward Raff,James Holt,Kristopher Micinski*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: ASSEMBLAGE-DEEPHISTORY 是一个包含73,610个二进制文件的统一框架，覆盖248个开源项目，集成了跨编译器、跨优化级别、跨平台和多版本历史构建，并将每个二进制文件的编译上下文、源代码、漏洞函数和包版本作为一等元数据存储，支持复杂的安全分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有二进制语料库仅捕获一两个维度的变异（如仅跨编译器构建无时间轴，或仅单构建的CVE标签），缺乏将跨构建多样性、跨版本历史和CVE标签整合为可查询结构的统一框架。

Method: 构建了包含73,610个二进制文件的语料库，涵盖248个开源项目，使用GCC、Clang和MSVC编译器在Linux和Windows上以多种优化级别进行跨多年历史构建。每个二进制文件与源代码、函数、调试信息、变体构建、历史版本和漏洞函数建立索引关联。

Result: 通过三项分析验证了该结构的价值：(1)三阶段LLM基准测试，测试LLM是推理二进制漏洞还是模式匹配构建特定伪影；(2)比较MalConv嵌入、jTrans函数嵌入和TLSH模糊哈希，量化同版本包在各空间的聚类效果；(3)贝叶斯回归分解二进制相似性中时间距离、文件变更和提交的贡献。

Conclusion: ASSEMBLAGE-DEEPHISTORY成功将跨构建多样性、跨版本历史和CVE标签整合为统一可查询框架，为二进制安全分析提供了丰富的数据基础。

Abstract: Existing binary corpora typically capture only one or two axes of binary variation: they either provide cross-compiler builds without a temporal axis, or CVE labels for single-build binaries. None combine cross-build diversity, cross-version history, and CVE labels into a queryable structure. We present ASSEMBLAGE-DEEPHISTORY, which consolidates these dimensions into a unified framework where every binary's compilation context, source code, vulnerable functions, and package version are stored as first-class metadata.
  ASSEMBLAGE-DEEPHISTORY comprises 73,610 binaries spanning 248 open-source projects, compiled across GCC, Clang, and MSVC at multiple optimization levels on Linux and Windows, with multi-year historical builds. Each binary is indexed in a database that links it to its source code, functions, debug info, variant builds, historical versions, and vulnerable functions. Three analyses demonstrate this structure's value: (1) a three-stage LLM benchmark (recognition, strategy-guided detection, and cross-build transfer) to test whether LLMs reason about binary vulnerabilities or pattern-match on build-specific artifacts; (2) a comparison of MalConv embeddings, jTrans function embeddings, and TLSH fuzzy hashes quantifying how same-package versions cluster in each space; and (3) a Bayesian regression decomposing binary similarity into contributions from temporal distance, file changes, and commits.

</details>


### [6] [Adversarial Reframing: A Framework for Targeted Generation in Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21674)
*Shahnewaz Karim Sakib,Swati Kar,Anindya Bijoy Das*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: THREAT是一种基于推理的多LLM协作框架，通过将提示发现形式化为非凸优化问题，高效生成越狱提示，以更高成功率和更低计算成本揭示对齐LLM中未被检测到的漏洞。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM在现实场景中广泛部署，但仍易受到越狱攻击（基于提示的攻击绕过安全过滤器），现有方法效率低且成本高。

Method: 提出THREAT框架，协调多个LLM在迭代搜索循环中生成文本越狱提示；将提示发现形式化为非凸优化问题，并提供高效求解方案以降低运行时间并提高攻击效果。

Result: THREAT在多种数据集和模型架构上以更低计算成本实现了更高的攻击成功率；生成的提示在不到1%的情况下被标记为有害，而对应的未修改提示约有50%被拒绝。

Conclusion: 研究发现揭示了已对齐LLM中先前未被检测到的漏洞，THREAT可作为主动增强基础模型安全性的实用工具。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) are widely deployed in diverse real-world settings, yet remain vulnerable to jailbreaking, where prompt-based attacks bypass safety filters. We present THREAT (Targeted Harmful generation via Reframing and Exploitation of Adversarial Tactics), a reasoning-driven framework that coordinates multiple LLMs in an iterative search loop to find textual jailbreak prompts. We formulate prompt discovery as a nonconvex optimization problem and provide an efficient solution that lowers runtime and improves attack effectiveness. Across diverse datasets and model architectures, THREAT delivers higher attack success rates with lower computational cost than prior methods. The crafted prompts were flagged as harmful in fewer than 1% of cases, compared with about 50% refusals for the corresponding unmodified prompts. These findings reveal previously undetected vulnerabilities in aligned LLMs and position THREAT as a practical tool for proactively strengthening the safety of foundation models.

</details>


### [7] [TriSweep: A Four-Drone Swarm Framework for Electromagnetic Side-Channel Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22709)
*Eric Yocam,Varghese Vaidyan*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: TriSweep提出了一种四无人机编队架构，用于对嵌入式微控制器进行远距离（0.25-1.5米）电磁侧信道分析。通过三个专用采集无人机（Anchor全频谱、Mask Probe掩码寄存器泄漏、Cipher Probe掩码SubBytes输出泄漏）和一个汇聚无人机（相干合并+4.8 dB SNR增益、二阶掩码消除），在ANSSI ASCAD数据集上实现了模拟密钥排名18±1.7（0.25米）。采用互相关对齐补偿无人机悬停振动，双通道CNN提升了去同步数据的密钥排名。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统电磁侧信道分析假设探头静止且紧贴芯片，低估了空中 adversaries 的威胁。本文旨在探索无人机编队能否在远距离（0.25-1.5米）实现有效的自主电磁侧信道攻击。

Method: 提出TriSweep仿真框架：四无人机编队包括三个专用采集无人机（Anchor全频谱采集、Mask Probe捕获掩码寄存器泄漏、Cipher Probe捕获掩码SubBytes输出泄漏）和一个汇聚无人机（执行相干合并以获得+4.8 dB SNR增益，并通过两个空间分离泄漏流的中心化乘积实现二阶掩码消除）。采用互相关对齐补偿无人机悬停振动，使用双通道CNN提升去同步数据的密钥恢复性能。

Result: 在ANSSI ASCAD三个数据集（ATmega8515掩码AES-128及50/100样本去同步变体）上，框架在0.25米距离实现模拟密钥排名18±1.7（五种子）。单无人机在100样本抖动变体上的密钥排名从89降至21（互相关对齐后）。双通道CNN损失降至0.454（随机基线5.545），提升了去同步数据的排名。

Conclusion: TriSweep仿真验证了多无人机编队实现远距离电磁侧信道分析的可行性，展示了相干合并、空间分离掩码消除和振动补偿的有效性。尚未制造物理硬件，原型构建是下一步计划。

Abstract: Electromagnetic (EM) side-channel analysis traditionally assumes a stationary, close-proximity probe - a threat model that underestimates aerial adversaries. TriSweep is a simulation framework that designs and evaluates a four-drone swarm architecture for autonomous standoff EM-SCA of embedded microcontrollers at 0.25-1.5 m. Three spatially specialized collector drones - Anchor (full-spectrum), Mask Probe (mask-register loading leakage), and Cipher Probe (masked SubBytes output leakage) - feed a stationary Accumulator drone that performs coherent combining (+4.8 dB SNR gain) and second-order mask cancellation via a centered product of the two spatially separated leakage streams. Evaluated against three real ANSSI ASCAD datasets (ATmega8515 masked AES-128 and 50/100-sample desynchronized variants), the framework achieves a simulated key rank of 18 +/- 1.7 (five-seed) at 0.25 m on the primary masked dataset. Profiling-trace cross-correlation alignment reduces single-drone rank from 89 to 21 on the 100-sample-jitter variant, demonstrating compensation for drone hover vibration. A two-channel CNN in the Accumulator converges to a loss of 0.454 (vs. random baseline 5.545) and improves rank on desynchronized datasets. No physical hardware has been fabricated; prototype construction is the planned next step.

</details>


### [8] [FuzzingBrain V2: A Multi-Agent LLM System for Automated Vulnerability Discovery and Reproduction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21779)
*Ze Sheng,Zhicheng Chen,Qingxiao Xu,Kewen Zhu,Jeff Huang*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: FuzzingBrain V2是一个基于多智能体系统的自动化漏洞检测框架，结合OSS-Fuzz确保漏洞可复现，提出Suspicious Point抽象实现最优粒度定位，采用逻辑驱动的分层函数分析和MCP上下文工程增强复杂漏洞推理，在AIxCC 2025竞赛中达到90%检测率，并在真实部署中发现29个零日漏洞。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM-based漏洞检测面临三大挑战：高误报率且缺乏可复现验证、漏洞定位粒度不优（函数级粒度丢失上下文、行级粒度缺乏上下文）、难以推理跨函数依赖和复杂触发条件的漏洞。

Method: (1) 基于Google OSS-Fuzz的全自动化漏洞分析，确保所有报告漏洞可通过模糊测试复现；(2) 提出Suspicious Point控制流抽象，实现最优粒度的精确漏洞定位；(3) 逻辑驱动的分层函数分析结合双层模糊测试，在资源约束下增强函数覆盖；(4) 基于MCP的静态和动态分析工具结合上下文工程，增强复杂漏洞推理能力。

Result: 在AIxCC 2025决赛C/C++数据集上达到90%检测率（40个漏洞中检测到36个）；在真实世界部署中发现29个零日漏洞，涉及12个开源项目，已全部确认修复，其中2个获得CVE编号。

Conclusion: FuzzingBrain V2通过多智能体协作、模糊测试保障可复现性、Suspicious Point最优粒度定位及上下文增强的复杂推理，有效解决了LLM漏洞检测中的关键挑战，在高检测率和低误报率上取得了显著成果。

Abstract: Software vulnerabilities pose critical security threats, with nearly 50,000 CVEs reported in 2025. While Large Language Models (LLMs) show promise for automated vulnerability detection, three key challenges remain. First, LLM-generated vulnerability reports suffer from high false positive rates and lack
  reproducible verification. Second, existing LLM-based approaches use suboptimal granularities for vulnerability localization: function-level analysis overlooks bugs when context becomes extensive, while line-level analysis lacks sufficient context. Third, existing approaches have difficulty reasoning about
  vulnerabilities with complex cross-function dependencies and triggering conditions.
  We present FuzzingBrain V2, a multi-agent system that addresses these gaps through four key contributions: (1) fully automated vulnerability analysis built on Google's OSS-Fuzz, ensuring all reported vulnerabilities are fuzzer-reproducible; (2) Suspicious Point, a novel control-flow-based abstraction for precise
  vulnerability localization at the optimal granularity; (3) logic-driven hierarchical function analysis with dual-layer fuzzing enhancing function coverage under resource constraints; (4) MCP-based static and dynamic analysis tools with context engineering enhancing complex vulnerability reasoning.
  On the AIxCC 2025 Final Competition C/C++ dataset, FuzzingBrain V2 achieved 90% detection rate (36 of 40 vulnerabilities). In real-world deployment, FuzzingBrain V2 discovered 29 zero-day vulnerabilities across 12 open-source projects, all confirmed and fixed by maintainers, with 2 assigned CVE IDs.

</details>


### [9] [Polars inside Intel SGX2 Enclaves: An Empirical Study of Confidential Analytical Query Processing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21797)
*Wei Wang,Burns Smith,Kenny Leftin*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文研究了基于Arrow-native的DataFrame引擎Polars在Intel SGX2安全飞地中的性能表现，发现端到端开销约为1.49-1.56倍，但表加载开销从2.27倍增至4.07倍，而查询开销则从1.51倍降至1.43倍；同时发现惰性执行比急切执行快2.25-2.27倍，且急切执行在41GB及以上时会出现内存溢出错误。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有针对TEE的机密分析研究多集中在SQL数据库引擎或早期SGX版本，缺乏对Arrow-native DataFrame引擎在SGX2环境下的系统性性能评估。

Method: 在Intel SGX2飞地中通过Gramine运行Polars引擎，使用TPC-H SF30基准测试和Azure Blob Storage，分别测量标准TPC-H功率分数和仅查询变体以分离计算开销与数据加载开销，并对比不同数据集宽度（22-73 GB）和API执行模式（惰性与急切）。

Result: 端到端开销恒定在1.49-1.56倍；查询开销从1.51-1.52倍降至1.43-1.44倍；表加载开销从2.27倍升至4.07倍；中位每查询SGX减速1.45倍，最大2.57倍；惰性执行比急切执行快2.25-2.27倍；急切执行在41GB及以上内存溢出。

Conclusion: SGX2能够支持Arrow-native分析处理且安全开销与DuckDB-SGX2研究相近，但加载路径放大和API级优化是端到端性能的主要决定因素。

Abstract: Trusted Execution Environments (TEEs) have renewed interest in confidential analytics, but most prior evaluations focus on SQL database engines or earlier SGX generations. This paper studies an Arrow-native DataFrame engine, Polars, running inside Intel SGX2 enclaves via Gramine on TPC-H SF30 with Azure Blob Storage. We report both the standard TPC-H power score and a query-only variant that removes table-loading time in order to separate compute overhead from data-ingestion overhead. Across four dataset-width configurations (approximately 22-73 GB), end-to-end overhead remains nearly constant at 1.49-1.56$\times$, but this composite metric obscures two distinct behaviors: query-only overhead declines from 1.51-1.52$\times$ to 1.43-1.44$\times$, whereas table-loading overhead rises from 2.27$\times$ to 4.07$\times$. We further show that overhead is not uniform across queries: for the len130 configuration, the median per-query SGX slowdown is 1.45$\times$ with a maximum of 2.57$\times$, and a small set of queries exhibits pronounced run-to-run spikes consistent with stateful EPC pressure. Finally, we compare Polars' lazy and eager APIs under the same TEE setting. Lazy execution is 2.25-2.27$\times$ faster overall, while eager execution fails with out-of-memory errors at 41 GB and above. Relative to the recent DuckDB-SGX2 study, our results suggest that SGX2 can support Arrow-native analytical processing with a similar order of security overhead, but that load-path amplification and API-level optimization are first-order determinants of end-to-end performance.

</details>


### [10] [Graph Structure of Chebyshev Permutation Polynomials over Binary and Ternary Adic Rings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21819)
*Xiaoxiong Lu,Yuling Dai,Chengqing Li*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文研究了环 $\mathbb{Z}_{2^{k_1}3^{k_2}}$ 上Chebyshev置换多项式的函数图结构，利用模2和模3幂的Chebyshev多项式新性质，给出了路径长度和环结构的显式刻画，证明了图具有强正则性，包括恒定数量的给定长度环和随 $k_1, k_2$ 增长的可预测分支模式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解有限域上非线性映射的函数图结构对于分析其动力学复杂性及其在密码学和伪随机生成中的潜在应用至关重要。

Method: 将环中每个元素视为顶点，多项式定义的映射关系视为有向边；利用Chebyshev多项式模2和模3幂的新性质进行分析。

Result: 尽管二元和三元分量引入了复杂性，但函数图表现出强正则性：给定长度的环数量恒定，且随着 $k_1$ 和 $k_2$ 增长，分支模式可预测。

Conclusion: 结果扩展了之前关于素幂环的研究，为理解数字非线性映射中复杂性的涌现提供了见解，并支持其密码学应用的安全性分析。

Abstract: Understanding the functional graph of a nonlinear map over a finite domain is crucial for analyzing its dynamical complexity and potential applications in cryptography and pseudorandom generation. In this paper, we investigate the graph structure of Chebyshev permutation polynomials over the ring $\mathbb{Z}_{2^{k_1}3^{k_2}}$, where $k_1$ and $k_2$ are positive integers and $0\in\{k_1, k_2\}$. Each element of the ring is regarded as a vertex, and the mapping relation defined by the polynomial corresponds to a directed edge. Building on new properties of Chebyshev polynomials modulo powers of $2$ and $3$, we provide an explicit characterization of path lengths and cycle structures in the functional graph. We show that, despite the complexities introduced by the binary and ternary components, the graph exhibits strong regularities, including a constant number of cycles of a given length and predictable branching patterns as $k_1$ and $k_2$ increase. Our results extend previous studies over prime-power rings, offering insights into the emergence of complexity in digital nonlinear maps and supporting the security analysis of their cryptographic applications.

</details>


### [11] [A Large Language Model Approach to Generating Bypass Rules for Malware Evasion in Analysis Sandbox](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21821)
*Zhiyong Sui,Lamine Noureddine,Mst Eshita Khatun,Sideeq Bello,Justin Woodring,Aisha Ali-Gombe*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出ABLE系统，利用大语言模型自动生成YARA规则来绕过沙箱逃逸检测。通过分析恶意软件执行轨迹、多策略推理、自动清洗和迭代优化，在334个真实样本上实现了79%的绕过成功率，迭代优化贡献了29.5%的成功案例，并比现有平台多识别47%的恶意软件家族分类。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有沙箱逃逸检测依赖人工逆向分析的绕过规则，无法应对快速演变的逃逸技术，迫切需要自动化的、可扩展的绕过规则生成方法。

Method: ABLE系统：1) 分析被终止恶意软件的执行轨迹；2) 采用多种推理策略生成YARA绕过规则；3) 引入自动清洗管道和反馈驱动的迭代优化来修复语法错误并提升规则有效性。

Result: 在334个真实恶意软件样本、四种开源LLM上，ABLE达到79%绕过成功率；迭代优化贡献了29.5%成功案例；相比现有平台多识别47%恶意软件家族分类，并暴露了此前隐藏的行为。

Conclusion: 基于LLM的自动绕过规则生成方法显著优于传统人工方式，具备良好的可扩展性和实际应用价值，能够有效揭示恶意软件的隐藏行为。

Abstract: Sandbox evasion remains a critical challenge for automated malware analysis, as modern malware employs environment checks to detect analysis platforms and suppress malicious behavior. Existing approaches rely on manually crafted bypass rules that require deep reverse engineering of each evasion mechanism -an approach that cannot scale against rapidly evolving evasion techniques. In this paper, we leverage large language models (LLMs) to automatically generate YARA rules that bypass evasion checks in sandbox environments. We propose ABLE, which analyzes execution traces from malware terminated due to potentially evasive behavior and employs multiple reasoning strategies to generate targeted bypass rules. To address syntactic errors and improve the efficacy of the bypass rules in the LLM outputs, we introduce an auto-sanitization pipeline and feedback-driven iterative refinement. We evaluate ABLE on 334 real-world malware samples across four open-weight LLMs. ABLE achieves a 79% bypass success rate, with iterative refinement contributing 29.5% of successful cases. Compared to existing analysis platforms, ABLE identifies 47% more malware family classifications and exposes previously hidden behaviors.

</details>


### [12] [Quality-Assured Fuzz Harness Generation via the Four Principles Framework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21824)
*Ze Sheng,Dmitrijs Trizna,Luigino Camastra,Zhicheng Chen,Qingxiao Xu,Jeff Huang*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: QuartetFuzz是一个基于LLM的自动化模糊测试桩生成系统，通过"四项原则"框架（逻辑正确性P1、API协议合规P2、安全边界尊重P3、入口点充分性P4）在生成阶段系统性地保证测试桩的正确性。在23个开源项目上发现42个漏洞（含3个CVE），误报率仅4.8%，并作为质量审计工具识别出53个现有生产测试桩的违规。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自动化测试桩生成工具无法系统性地保证测试桩的正确性——逻辑错误、API误用和生命周期违规在源代码层面未被检测。随着LLM驱动的大规模测试桩生成，不受控的质量问题使规模成为负担，亟需在生成阶段内建正确性保障机制。

Method: 提出"四项原则"（P1-P4）框架，首次从源代码层面定义测试桩正确性并给出数学规范和可实现的检查；构建自主LLM智能体，通过"生成-检查-修复"循环在模糊测试开始前确保满足P1-P4。

Result: 在23个C/C++、Java和JavaScript开源项目上提交42个漏洞报告，29个已修复或确认（含3个CVE），仅2个被拒绝（4.8%误报率）；P1/P2检查自动拦截了58个本会成为误报的测试桩引发的崩溃；审计586个现有生产测试桩识别53个违规（45确认，35修复）。

Conclusion: 通过内建的正确性检查与LLM自主生成相结合，QuartetFuzz能显著提升测试桩质量，降低误报率，并有效发现真实漏洞，为大规模自动化测试桩生成提供了可行的质量控制方案。

Abstract: Fuzz testing is the dominant technique for finding memory-safety vulnerabilities in C/C++ software, yet its effectiveness hinges on the quality of fuzz harnesses -- the programs that bridge fuzzers and library APIs. A growing body of tools now automate harness generation, but none systematically ensures the correctness of produced harnesses: logic errors, API misuse, and lifecycle violations go undetected at the source level. As LLM-driven generation scales harness creation, uncontrolled quality turns scale into a liability.
  We present QuartetFuzz, an autonomous harness-generation system that systematically improves correctness throughout the generation process. At its core is the Four Principles framework -- Logic Correctness (P1), API Protocol Compliance (P2), Security Boundary Respect (P3), and Entry Point Adequacy (P4) -- the first source-level definition of harness correctness with mathematical specifications and implementable checks. We operationalize these principles in an autonomous LLM agent that produces harnesses satisfying P1-P4 through a generate-check-fix loop before any fuzzing begins.
  Deployed on 23 open-source projects spanning C/C++, Java, and JavaScript, the system submits 42 bug reports, of which 29 are fixed or confirmed upstream (including 3 CVEs) and only 2 are rejected (4.8% FP rate). During generation, the built-in P1/P2 checks automatically intercepted 58 harness-induced crashes that would otherwise have been false positives. Applied as a quality auditor to 586 existing production harnesses across 70 projects, the system identifies 53 violations (45 confirmed, 35 fixed). We release a dataset of 100 labeled harnesses for reproducible evaluation. Code and dataset are available at https://github.com/OwenSanzas/QuartetFuzz

</details>


### [13] [SPIDER: Two Server Functionality for the Cost of Zero](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21857)
*Ofir Dvir,Kali Hale,Javin Zipkin,Divyakant Agrawal,Dahlia Malkhi*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出 baseSPIDER 和 SPIDER 两种私有信息检索（PIR）方案。baseSPIDER 在单服务器有状态客户端场景下，匹配了最优渐进通信复杂度并改进了常数因子，设计更简洁。SPIDER 是首个无需服务器特殊合作、无需专用 API 或协议特定交互的单服务器 PIR 方案，通过简单转换 baseSPIDER 实现，消除了部署障碍。该转换方法还可推广至其他三种 PIR 方案，SPIDER 相比这些改进方案设计更简单但客户端计算开销更高。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有单服务器 PIR 方案在通信复杂度上虽有渐进最优结果，但常数因子较大，且通常需要服务器提供特殊 API、辅助状态或协议特定交互，部署门槛高。本文旨在设计一种通信效率更高、概念更简单，且能直接应用于现有数据库系统（无需服务器合作）的 PIR 方案。

Method: 本文提出 baseSPIDER，采用单服务器有状态客户端模型，客户端通过预处理和存储提示（hints）来优化后续查询，在渐进最优通信复杂度基础上改进了常数因子，且设计更简洁。然后通过简单转换将 baseSPIDER 改造为 SPIDER，使其在默认数据库接口（仅支持常规索引访问）下工作，无需服务器提供任何特殊配合。该转换方法还可推广应用于其他三种近期 PIR 方案。

Result: baseSPIDER 在单服务器有状态客户端设置下实现了与现有最优方案相同的渐进通信复杂度，但常数因子更小，尤其适合大条目数据库。SPIDER 是首个在默认服务器接口下工作的单服务器 PIR 方案，无需专用 API、辅助服务器状态或协议特定交互，消除了部署障碍。SPIDER 相比其他通过相同转换方法得到的改进方案，设计更简单但客户端计算开销更高。

Conclusion: 本文通过 baseSPIDER 和 SPIDER 展示了单服务器 PIR 在通信效率和易部署性两方面的进步。baseSPIDER 提升了通信常数因子，SPIDER 首次实现了无需服务器任何配合的 PIR，大大降低了实际部署门槛。所提出的转换方法具有普适性，可应用于其他 PIR 方案，为实际系统中隐私保护数据检索提供了可行路径。

Abstract: We introduce baseSPIDER and SPIDER, private information retrieval (PIR) schemes that embody two technical advancements. The baseSPIDER protocol operates with a single server and a stateful client that performs pre-processing and stores hints for future queries. In this setting, baseSPIDER introduces a new approach that matches the asymptotically optimal communication complexity of state-of-the-art schemes while improving constant factors--an advantage that is particularly significant for databases with large entries. In addition, baseSPIDER offers a conceptually simpler design relative to prior protocols. SPIDER operates over a default database interface and requires no cooperation from the server at any stage. To our knowledge, SPIDER is the first single-server PIR construction of this design, achieving privacy without specialized APIs, auxiliary server state, or protocol-specific interaction beyond conventional indexed access. SPIDER is built via a simple transformation of baseSPIDER to the default server setting, eliminating deployment barriers and enabling immediate applicability to existing systems. This transformation can be applied more broadly to three recent PIR solutions, adapting them for use in the default-server paradigm and yielding solutions of independent interest. SPIDER compares to the resulting modified solutions by exhibiting a simpler design while incurring higher client computational work.

</details>


### [14] [Blind Spots in the Guard: How Domain-Camouflaged Injection Attacks Evade Detection in Multi-Agent LLM Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22001)
*Aaditya Pai*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文发现LLM智能体的注入检测器存在系统性盲区：当攻击载荷模仿目标文档的领域词汇和权威结构（域伪装注入）时，检测率从93.8%骤降至9.7%（Llama 3.1 8B）和从100%降至55.6%（Gemini 2.0 Flash）。跨45个任务、3个领域、2个模型家族的实验显示Camouflage Detection Gap (CDG)显著且统计可靠。Llama Guard 3对伪装载荷检测率为0。多智能体辩论架构会放大静态注入攻击。该漏洞对弱模型而言是架构性的，仅靠增强检测器无法完全修复。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的LLM智能体注入检测器基于静态、模板化的攻击载荷进行校准，这些载荷明确声明自己是覆盖指令。研究者发现当攻击载荷模仿目标文档的领域词汇和权威结构时，标准检测器会失效，形成了系统性盲区。

Method: 形式化定义了Camouflage Detection Gap (CDG)——静态载荷与伪装载荷之间的注入检测率差异。跨45个任务、3个领域（推测可能包括金融、医疗等）、2个模型家族（Llama 3.1 8B和Gemini 2.0 Flash）进行实验，并使用卡方检验验证统计显著性。同时评估了生产级安全分类器Llama Guard 3，以及多智能体辩论架构的影响和针对性检测器增强的效果。

Result: 静态注入检测率93.8%→伪装注入检测率9.7%（Llama 3.1 8B）；静态100%→伪装55.6%（Gemini 2.0 Flash）。CDG显著（Llama: chi²=38.03, p<0.001；Gemini: chi²=17.05, p<0.001），零反向不一致对。Llama Guard 3对伪装载荷检测率为0。多智能体辩论架构在弱模型上放大静态注入攻击达9.9倍。检测器增强仅提供部分修复（Llama提升10.2%，Gemini提升78.7%）。

Conclusion: 域伪装注入揭示了当前LLM安全机制的根本性缺陷，该漏洞对较弱模型而言是架构性的而非偶然性的，仅靠增强检测器无法完全解决。研究公开了框架、任务库和载荷生成器以推动后续研究。

Abstract: Injection detectors deployed to protect LLM agents are calibrated on static, template-based payloads that announce themselves as override directives. We identify a systematic blind spot: when payloads are generated to mimic the domain vocabulary and authority structures of the target document, what we call domain camouflaged injection, standard detectors fail to flag them, with detection rates dropping from 93.8% to 9.7% on Llama 3.1 8B and from 100% to 55.6% on Gemini 2.0 Flash. We formalize this as the Camouflage Detection Gap (CDG), the difference in injection detection rate between static and camouflaged payloads. Across 45 tasks spanning three domains and two model families, CDG is large and statistically significant (chi^2 = 38.03, p < 0.001 for Llama; chi^2 = 17.05, p < 0.001 for Gemini), with zero reverse discordant pairs in either case. We additionally evaluate Llama Guard 3, a production safety classifier, which detects zero camouflage payloads (IDRcamouflage = 0.000), confirming that the blind spot extends beyond few-shot detectors to dedicated safety classifiers. We further show that multi-agent debate architectures amplify static injection attacks by up to 9.9x on smaller models, while stronger models show collective resistance. Targeted detector augmentation provides only partial remediation (10.2% improvement on Llama, 78.7% on Gemini), suggesting the vulnerability is architectural rather than incidental for weaker models. Our framework, task bank, and payload generator are released publicly.

</details>


### [15] [Parser-Free Querying of Security Logs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22027)
*Evan Luo,Julien Piet,David Wagner*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: Sieve通过大语言模型自动生成查询代码，使安全分析师能用自然语言直接查询原始日志，相比手动脚本编写将复杂时序查询的错误率降低了3倍以上。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 安全日志格式异构且半结构化，传统方案要么需要为每种格式构建解析器（工程成本高），要么需要分析师掌握各日志源的格式细节（认知负担重），且无法表达安全调查所需的多行时序查询。

Method: Sieve利用轻量级自动提取的日志格式上下文来引导大语言模型，仅需一次LLM调用即可生成可执行的查询代码，随后进行确定性执行。

Result: 在5种日志类型、133个安全查询的评估中，Sieve在复杂的时序和跨事件查询上相比手动分析师脚本实现了超过3倍的错误率降低，在多行关联任务上提升最为显著。

Conclusion: LLM生成的代码能够弥合结构化日志查询的表达能力与原始日志文件直接使用之间的差距。

Abstract: Security analysts routinely query system logs to detect threats and investigate incidents, but each log source uses its own semi-structured format: logs are cheap to produce, but expensive to use. The standard approach, building per-source parsers to normalize logs into structured schemas, is powerful but requires continuous engineering effort for each new format. Querying raw logs directly with tools like grep avoids this cost, but requires analysts to know each source's message variants and cannot express the multi-line temporal queries that security investigations demand. We present Sieve, a system that generates executable query code from natural-language security questions by grounding a large language model with lightweight, automatically extracted log-format context, requiring only one LLM call per query followed by deterministic execution. Evaluating 133 security queries across 5 log types, we find that Sieve achieves over a 3x reduction in error rate on complex temporal and cross-event queries compared to manual analyst scripting, with the largest gains on the multi-line correlation tasks most critical to active investigations. Our results and benchmark provide evidence that LLM-generated code can bridge the gap between the expressiveness of structured log querying and the immediacy of working directly with raw files.

</details>


### [16] [Safeguarding Text-to-Image Generative Models Against Unauthorized Knowledge Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22060)
*Yilan Gao,Sida Huang,Hongyuan Zhang,Xuelong Li*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: WaveGuard是一种基于生成器的单次防护框架，通过在频域注入结构化、不可感知的扰动，保护闭源生成式API输出的合成图像，防止被用于未经授权的模型窃取（知识蒸馏），在WikiArt合成输出蒸馏场景下实现了功效-保真度-效率的有利平衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 闭源生成式服务通过查询式API部署时，攻击者可通过反复查询收集大量合成图像，用于训练替代模型（模型窃取/知识蒸馏），现有防御需要在保持图像视觉保真度、显式控制扰动幅度、高效扩展到大容量输出之间取得平衡。

Method: 提出WaveGuard，一种单次（single-pass）、基于生成器的防护框架。核心是一个频率感知的扰动生成器，在用户指定的扰动预算下，向合成图像注入结构化、不可感知的频域扰动，在维持良善用户视觉感知的同时，降低被保护图像作为未授权学生模型训练数据的效用。

Result: 在WikiArt相关合成输出蒸馏设置下的广泛实验表明，WaveGuard实现了功效-保真度-效率的有利权衡，具有显式的不可感知性控制，并在防护效率上取得显著提升。

Conclusion: WaveGuard有效缓解了闭源生成式服务中的模型窃取威胁，通过频域扰动注入实现对抗知识蒸馏的实用防御，兼顾了视觉保真度、扰动可控制性和大规模输出场景下的效率。

Abstract: Closed-weight generative services are increasingly deployed through query-based APIs, where users can obtain generated outputs while model parameters remain inaccessible. However, such deployment does not prevent model stealing: an attacker can repeatedly query the service, collect large volumes of released synthetic images, and use them as training data for a private substitute model. This query-output-driven process enables unauthorized knowledge distillation and capability replication without direct access to the original weights. To mitigate this threat, a practical defense should preserve the visual fidelity of released images, provide explicit control over perturbation magnitude, and scale efficiently to large-volume output release. We present WaveGuard, a single-pass, generator-based protection framework that safeguards released synthetic images under a user-specified perturbation budget. WaveGuard employs a frequency-aware perturbation generator to inject structured, imperceptible perturbations that maintain perceptual utility for benign viewers while reducing the usefulness of protected images as training data for unauthorized student models. Extensive experiments under WikiArt-related synthetic-output distillation settings show that WaveGuard achieves a favorable efficacy--fidelity--efficiency trade-off, with explicit imperceptibility control and substantial gains in protection efficiency.

</details>


### [17] [QT-PUF: Quantum Tunneling Leakage Based PUF for Implantable IoMT Devices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22113)
*Yueqi Ma,Vivek Mohan,Chip-Hong Chang,Emmanuel M. Drakakis*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出QT-PUF，一种基于CMOS栅极隧穿泄漏电流的物理不可克隆函数，利用工艺偏差产生的量子力学栅极泄漏，通过伪电阻I-V前端差分读出电路将皮安级电流变化转换为数字响应。在65nm CMOS工艺下，熵值为0.9999998，汉明距离分数为0.5001，平均功耗96.04 nW/bit（19.21 fJ/bit），在0.9–1.3V和0–100°C范围内可靠运行，适用于超低功耗医疗物联网设备。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 医疗物联网（IoMT）设备面临物理篡改和仿冒威胁，现有PUF（如存储器型、环形振荡器型、仲裁器型）因额外电路开销、功耗高或稳定性差，不适合超低功耗的可穿戴和植入式设备，因此需要一种无需外部激励、静态偏置即可工作且超低功耗的新型PUF方案。

Method: 利用标准CMOS工艺中由工艺偏差引起的栅极隧穿泄漏电流（量子力学效应），设计伪电阻I-V前端差分读出电路，将皮安级电流差异转换为数字响应。PUF无需外部激励或稳定化电路，在静态偏置条件下工作。

Result: 65nm CMOS仿真：熵值0.9999998，平均汉明距离分数0.5001，平均功耗96.04 nW/bit（19.21 fJ/bit），在1.0–1.3V和10–70°C范围内平均误码率低于0.000163。

Conclusion: QT-PUF在超低功耗、高安全性、小面积开销方面优于传统PUF结构，特别适合资源受限的植入式医疗设备和IoMT应用场景。

Abstract: The Internet of Medical Things (IoMT) marks a shift toward decentralized healthcare, enabling continuous monitoring and personalized care through connected wearable and implantable devices. However, ensuring the trust and integrity of these devices themselves remains a major challenge, as physical compromise or counterfeiting can directly endanger patient safety, privacy, and data integrity. This work presents QT-PUF, a gate-tunneling-leakage-based physical unclonable function (PUF) that leverages quantum-mechanical gate leakage resulting from process-induced variations in standard CMOS devices. A differential readout circuit with a pseudo-resistor I-to-V frontend is proposed to convert the picoampere-level leakage variations into digital responses. Unlike existing PUFs such as those based on memory, ring oscillators, or arbiters, which are less suitable for ultralow-power IoMT devices (due to additional circuitry, power overhead, or poor stability), QT-PUF requires no external excitation or stabilization and operates under static bias. Simulation-based measurements for a $\mathbf{65}$~nm CMOS process demonstrate an entropy of $\mathbf{0.9999998}$, an FHD of $\mathbf{0.5001}$, and an average power (energy) consumption of $\mathbf{96.04}$~nW/bit ($\mathbf{19.21}$~fJ/bit, respectively) at $\mathbf{1.2\,V}$ and $\mathbf{35\,^{\circ}C}$ for the proposed PUF. It operates reliably across $\mathbf{0.9}\text{--}\mathbf{1.3}$~V and $\mathbf{0}\text{--}\mathbf{100\,^{\circ}C}$ with an average BER below $\mathbf{0.000163}$ across $\mathbf{1.0}\text{--}\mathbf{1.3}$~V and $\mathbf{10}\text{--}\mathbf{70\,^{\circ}C}$ within the operating conditions of typical implantable devices.

</details>


### [18] [Benchmarking Autonomous Agents against Temporal, Spatial, and Semantic Evasions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22321)
*Jianan Ma,Xiaohu Du,Ruixiao Lin,Yaoxiang Bian,Jialuo Chen,Jingyi Wang,Xiaofang Yang,Shiwen Cui,Changhua Meng,Xinhao Deng,Zhen Wang*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出针对LLM智能体系统的多维规避框架（Temporal/Spatial/Semantic evasion），构建A3S-Bench基准（2,254条真实轨迹），在10个主流LLM骨干上测试20种威胁场景，将平均风险触发率从28.3%提升至52.6%，揭示了现有自治智能体系统的架构级安全漏洞。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有漏洞分析主要关注单轮、无状态行为，忽视了具有状态的多轮交互和动态工具调用所带来的扩展攻击面。随着自治智能体（如OpenClaw）以深度系统级权限运行以执行复杂任务，它们引入了严重且未缓解的安全风险。

Method: 提出三维度规避框架：(1) 时域规避——跨交互轮次分片恶意负载；(2) 空域规避——将负载隐藏于复杂外部工件中绕过LLM解析；(3) 语义规避——在良性上下文噪声中掩盖恶意意图。构建A3S-Bench基准（2,254条真实智能体执行轨迹），集成10个主流LLM骨干，评估20种实际威胁场景。

Result: 规避框架将平均风险触发率从28.3%基线提升至52.6%，揭示了现有防御无法解决的系统性架构级漏洞。

Conclusion: 当前自治智能体系统存在架构级安全缺陷，亟需针对这些独特威胁定制的防御机制。

Abstract: As autonomous agents (e.g., OpenClaw) increasingly operate with deep system-level privileges to execute complex tasks, they introduce severe, unmitigated security risks. Current vulnerability analyses overwhelmingly focus on single-turn, stateless behaviors, overlooking the expanded attack surface inherent in stateful, multi-turn interactions and dynamic tool invocations. In this paper, we propose a novel, multi-dimensional evasion framework targeting LLM-based agent systems. We introduce three stealthy attack vectors: (1) Temporal evasion, which fragments malicious payloads across sequential interaction turns; (2) Spatial evasion, which conceals payloads within complex external artifacts that evade standard LLM parsing mechanisms; and (3) Semantic evasion, which obscures malicious intents beneath benign contextual noise. To systematically quantify these threats, we construct A3S-Bench, a comprehensive benchmark comprising 2,254 real-world agent execution trajectories. Evaluating a standard agent framework separately integrated with 10 mainstream LLM backbones against 20 practical threat scenarios, we demonstrate that our evasion framework elevates the average risk trigger rate from a 28.3\% baseline to 52.6\%. These findings reveal systemic, architecture-level vulnerabilities in current autonomous agent systems that existing defenses fail to address, highlighting an urgent need for defense mechanisms tailored to the unique threats.

</details>


### [19] [PACT: Reducing Alert Fatigue in Low-Prevalence SOC Streams with Triggered Active Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22324)
*Samuel Ndichu,Tao Ban,Seiichi Ozawa,Takeshi Takahashi,Daisuke Inoue*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: PACT是一种帕累托感知的触发式主动学习控制器，通过自适应窗口得分漂移触发和混合采集规则，在低流行率安全运营场景中，相比冻结基线和定期均匀随机更新，分别降低43%和21%的良性归一化误报负担，同时减少3.8倍和5.2倍的分析师查询量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 安全运营中心面临持续的告警疲劳问题：在低流行率数据流中，即使很低的误报率也会产生大量调查负载，而聚合F1分数会掩盖分析师的真实负担。

Method: PACT包含三个核心组件：(1) 包装一个已部署的冻结XGBoost-Focal分类器；(2) 自适应窗口得分漂移触发机制；(3) 结合阈值相对不确定性与高得分采样的混合采集规则。

Result: 在AIT-ADS和BOTSv1两个基准上，PACT实现了自适应方法中最低的良性归一化误报负担；相比冻结基线分别降低43%和21%的负担；相比周期性均匀随机更新，分析师查询量减少3.8倍和5.2倍。

Conclusion: 匹配触发消融实验表明，采集机制对性能的贡献超越了触发时机本身，但代价是自由运行触发下正向窗口召回率下降约十个百分点；纯误报最小化的冻结阈值基线会以BOTSv1召回率暴跌55个百分点为代价，在评估的工作负载假设下，纯粹的误报最小化会导致不可接受的召回损失。

Abstract: Security operations centers face persistent alert fatigue: in low-prevalence streams, even low false-positive rates generate substantial investigation load, while aggregate F1 scores obscure analyst burden. We introduce PACT, a Pareto-aware controller for triggered active learning, which wraps an already-deployed frozen XGBoost-Focal screener with an adaptive windowing score-shift trigger and a hybrid acquisition rule combining threshold-relative uncertainty with high-score sampling. On two public low-prevalence benchmarks, AIT-ADS (AIT Alert Data Set), and BOTSv1 (Boss of the SOC version 1), PACT attains the lowest benign-normalized false-positive (FP) burden among the adaptive methods tested. It reduces burden by 43% and 21%, respectively, relative to a frozen baseline, while using 3.8x and 5.2x fewer analyst queries than periodic uniform-random updating. A matched-trigger ablation controls trigger timing and shows that acquisition contributes beyond timing alone, at the cost of approximately ten percentage points of positive-window recall under free-running triggers. A frozen threshold-only baseline pushes FP lower still but collapses BOTSv1 recall by 55 percentage points. Under the evaluated workload assumptions, pure FP minimization trades unacceptable recall for that lower burden.

</details>

Result: 该项目旨在保护电力网和医院等关键服务，确保欧洲公民的数据安全。

Conclusion: SEEWQCI项目通过融合多种通信技术和安全规则，为欧洲关键基础设施提供量子安全防护。

Abstract: The fast growth of quantum computing can lead to amazing scientific breakthroughs while on the same time can be used to break today's security systems, raising new risks for existing digital systems. Facing this challenge, the European's Union's deployement of the European Communication Infrastructure (EuroQCI) is crucial. The SEEWQCI project combines fiber cables, satellite communications and enhanced security rules to build a strong digital shield. Its focus is to protect vital services like power grids and hospitals keeping Europeans' data safe.

</details>


### [21] [A First Measurement Study on Authentication Security in Real-World Remote MCP Servers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22333)
*Huijun Zhou,Xiaohan Zhang,Haozhe Zhang,Haoyang Zhang,Mi Zhang,Min Yang*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文首次对MCP（模型上下文协议）远程服务器的认证安全性进行了大规模测量研究，发现7,973个活跃远程MCP服务器中40.55%暴露工具无需认证；在OAuth认证的服务器中，所有119个可测试服务器均存在至少一个安全缺陷，共发现325个缺陷，其中动态客户端注册缺陷影响96.6%的服务器，可导致敏感信息泄露和账户接管。已获取9个CVE编号。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MCP协议正成为连接大语言模型与外部服务的关键接口，远程MCP服务器需处理用户链接的在线服务（如社交、金融等）认证，但其认证安全性尚未得到系统研究。

Method: 大规模扫描发现7,973个活跃远程MCP服务器，分析其认证状态；提出包含4大类9种具体缺陷类型的MCP认证缺陷分类体系；实现结合被动流量检查与主动动态探测的半自动化缺陷检测框架，对119个支持OAuth的服务器进行测试。

Result: 40.55%的远程MCP服务器无认证直接暴露工具；119个OAuth服务器均存在至少一个缺陷，共325个缺陷；动态客户端注册缺陷占96.6%；已获9个CVE ID。

Conclusion: MCP生态系统中存在普遍的认证安全弱点，尤其是OAuth远程部署面临开放客户端环境、动态客户端注册和委托授权等新攻击面，亟需加强安全防护。

Abstract: The Model Context Protocol (MCP) is emerging as a common interface connecting large language models (LLMs) with external services. Remote deployments are becoming increasingly important as agents connect to user-linked online services, such as social, productivity, and financial services. In such deployments, the authentication boundary between MCP clients and remote servers becomes security-critical, yet remains underexplored.
  We present the first measurement study of authentication security in real-world remote MCP servers. We identify 7,973 live remote MCP servers, finding that 40.55% expose tools without authentication. Among authenticated servers, OAuth is the dominant authorization mechanism for reaching remote services, and OAuth deployments in the MCP ecosystem commonly exhibit three characteristics: open client environments, dynamic client registration, and delegated authorization. These characteristics distinguish MCP deployments from traditional OAuth and introduce new attack surfaces. Guided by this observation, we derive a taxonomy of authentication flaws comprising three MCP-specific categories and conventional OAuth misconfigurations, for a total of four categories and nine concrete flaw types. To evaluate these flaws at scale, we implement a semi-automated detection framework that combines passive traffic inspection with active dynamic probing. Applying it to 119 testable real-world OAuth-enabled MCP servers, we find that each server exhibits at least one flaw, with a total of 325 flaws identified, among which dynamic client registration flaws affect 96.6% of tested servers. Many of these flaws can lead to sensitive information leakage and account takeover. Through responsible disclosure, we obtained 9 CVE IDs. Our findings expose pervasive authentication weaknesses in the MCP ecosystem and underscore the urgent need for hardened OAuth-based remote deployments.

</details>


### [22] [TimeGuard: Channel-wise Pool Training for Backdoor Defense in Time Series Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22365)
*Quang Duc Nguyen,Siyuan Liang,Yiming Li,Fushuo Huo,Dacheng Tao*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文系统评估了13种后门防御方法在时间序列预测中的失效模式，发现数据纠缠导致通道级信号稀释、任务形式偏移导致训练损失退化两大根本问题，并提出基于通道池训练和距离正则化损失选择的训练时后门防御方法TimeGuard，在多个数据集上MAE_P提升1.96倍，干净性能损失控制在5%以内。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 时间序列预测容易受到后门攻击，但针对TSF的后门防御研究严重不足，主要面临数据纠缠和任务形式偏移两大挑战，现有防御方法在TSF场景下失效原因尚未被系统分析。

Method: 提出TimeGuard防御方法：(1) 采用通道池训练范式，利用时间感知准则初始化高置信度池以缓解信号稀释；(2) 引入距离正则化损失选择机制，在训练过程中逐步扩展可靠池并缓解损失退化问题。

Result: 在多个数据集、预测架构和TSF后门攻击下，TimeGuard的主任务MAE比现有最优基线提升1.96倍，同时干净性能损失控制在5% MAE以内。

Conclusion: TSF后门防御面临数据纠缠和任务形式偏移两大特有挑战，现有通用防御在此场景下失效；TimeGuard通过通道池训练和距离正则化损失选择有效缓解了这些问题，显著提升了TSF对后门攻击的鲁棒性。

Abstract: Time Series Forecasting (TSF) plays a critical role across many domains, yet it is vulnerable to backdoor attacks. However, backdoor defenses tailored to TSF remain underexplored, due to data entanglement and task-formulation shift challenges. To fill this gap, we conduct a systematic evaluation of thirteen representative backdoor defenses across the TSF life cycle and analyze their failure modes. Our results reveal two fundamental issues: (1) data entanglement induces channel-level signal dilution, rendering sample-filtering and trigger-synthesis defenses ineffective at localizing backdoors; and (2) task-formulation shift leads to training-loss degeneration, causing poisoned and clean windows to become indistinguishable at training stages. Based on these findings, we propose a training-time backdoor defense for TSF, termed TimeGuard. Our method adopts channel-wise pool training as the core paradigm and initializes a high-confidence pool using time-aware criteria to mitigate signal dilution. Moreover, we introduce distance-regularized loss selection to progressively expand the reliable pool during training and ease loss degeneration. Extensive experiments across multiple datasets, forecasting architectures, and TSF backdoor attacks demonstrate that TimeGuard substantially improves robustness, boosting $\mathrm{MAE}_\mathrm{P}$ by $1.96\times$ over the leading baseline, while preserving clean performance within 5% $\mathrm{MAE}_\mathrm{C}$.

</details>


### [23] [Measuring Security Without Fooling Ourselves: Why Benchmarking Agents Is Hard](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22568)
*Sahar Abdelnabi,Chris Hicks,Konrad Rieck,Ahmad-Reza Sadeghi*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 当前用于评估安全关键型AI Agent的基准测试存在三大核心缺陷：基准漏洞、时间陈旧性和运行时不确定性，本文分析了这些问题并提出了构建更稳健评估框架的方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准测试在评估安全关键型AI Agent时存在严重缺陷，无法可靠衡量其安全性能，亟需识别问题根源并改进评估方法。

Method: 基于近期实证证据，系统性地识别并表征了安全评估面临的三大核心挑战：基准漏洞（benchmark vulnerabilities）、时间陈旧性（temporal staleness）和运行时不确定性（runtime uncertainty），并据此提出改进方向。

Result: 明确了三大核心挑战的具体内涵：基准漏洞指评估基准本身存在可被利用的弱点；时间陈旧性指评估标准随时间推移而失效；运行时不确定性指实际部署中模型行为不可预测。

Conclusion: 需要构建更稳健和可信的安全评估框架，以弥补现有基准测试的不足，确保AI Agent在安全关键场景中的可靠性。

Abstract: The benchmarks used to evaluate AI agents in security-critical roles suffer from crucial weaknesses. Building on recent empirical evidence, we characterize three core challenges that undermine security evaluations: benchmark vulnerabilities, temporal staleness, and runtime uncertainty. We then outline practical directions toward building more robust and trustworthy evaluation frameworks.

</details>


### [24] [A Formal Basis for Quantum Cryptographic Exposure Measurement under HNDL Threat](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22569)
*Matheus Rufino,Rafael Duarte Marcelino,Julio Smanioto Garcia*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文证明，HNDL暴露概率的函数形式并非校准选择，而是由三个关于对抗性生产和价值衰减动态的假设在结构上决定的。该框架通过时间风险、密码脆弱性与运营暴露的乘积项、以及由防御-攻击强度比决定的饱和分母构成，揭示了加性评分框架无法复现的结构性特征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 回答一个核心问题：在攻击者今天复制加密流量、等待量子计算机未来解密的场景下，组织的暴露程度如何量化？现有加性评分框架在结构上存在缺陷，需要更严格的数学基础。

Method: 基于三个关于对抗性生产和价值衰减动态的结构性假设，推导HNDL暴露概率的因子分解形式，包含时间风险项、密码脆弱性与运营暴露的乘积项、以及饱和分母项。

Result: HNDL暴露概率因子分解为：时间风险 × (密码脆弱性 × 运营暴露) / (1 + 防御-攻击强度比)。边际敏感性是组织在脆弱性-暴露平面中位置的内生函数，而非固定全局常数。加性框架因缺少交互项而无法复制此结构。

Conclusion: 该框架为部分可观测条件下的运营HNDL暴露优先级排序提供了结构上有理有据的基础。

Abstract: An adversary copies your encrypted traffic today and waits for a quantum computer to decrypt it later. How exposed are you?
  We show that the functional form of the answer is not merely a calibration choice -- it is structurally justified by three assumptions about adversarial production and value-decay dynamics. Under those assumptions, the HNDL compromise probability factorises into a temporal hazard, a multiplicative cryptographic-vulnerability and operational-exposure term, and a saturation denominator governed by the defense-attack intensity ratio; the marginal sensitivity to each dimension is endogenous to the organisation's position in the vulnerability-exposure plane, not a fixed global constant. Additive scoring frameworks cannot reproduce this structure because the interaction between cryptographic vulnerability and operational exposure is absent by construction, regardless of calibration. The resulting framework provides a structurally grounded basis for operational HNDL exposure prioritisation under partial observability.

</details>


### [25] [Building an Open Source Operational Technology Pentesting Platform: Lessons from LINICS](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22590)
*Awais Rashid,Joseph Gardiner,Louise Evans*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文介绍了LINICS的构建经验，这是一个用于运营技术(OT)渗透测试和安全分析的开源平台，填补了IT领域（如Kali Linux）与OT领域之间缺乏类似开源工具的空白。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: IT安全专业人员有Kali Linux等开源平台，但支撑工业控制系统的运营技术(OT)领域缺乏类似的开源平台，存在工具空白。

Method: 作者分享了架构、构建和发布LINICS开源平台的过程与经验。

Result: 成功构建并发布了LINICS，一个面向OT渗透测试和安全分析的开源平台。

Conclusion: LINICS为OT安全领域提供了类似Kali Linux的开源平台，填补了OT渗透测试工具的空缺。

Abstract: Information Technology (IT) security professionals have ready access to open-source platforms such as Kali Linux. But no such platform exists for Operational Technology (OT) that underpins Industrial Control Systems. We discuss experiences of architecting, building and releasing LINICS, an open-source platform for OT pentesting and security analysis.

</details>

### [26] [Flying Together: Human-Guided Immersive Shared Control for Aerial Robot Teams in Unknown Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21680)
*Lou De Bel-Air,Luca Morando,Ruitao Chen,Keru Wang,Benjamin Jarvis,Charbel Toumieh,Yang Zhou,Ken Perlin,Dario Floreano,Giuseppe Loianno*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于虚拟现实的共享控制框架，用于多无人机在受限未知环境中的导航，通过操作员引导的运动基元规划器和导纳控制器实现人机协同探索。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自主多机器人系统虽能实现安全协调导航，但在非结构化环境中难以适应未预见条件和捕捉操作员意图，需要引入人类智能进行实时引导。

Method: 1) 基于用户引导的运动基元规划器，持续计算无碰撞轨迹并整合操作员输入；2) 导纳控制器使操作员灵活影响团队行为；3) 支持混合现实操作（物理+仿真无人机）；4) 双向VR界面通过迁移点引导并反馈团队状态。

Result: 共享控制改善了避障性能，维持了机间距离，并降低了操作员负担，验证了沉浸式人在回路多机器人导航的可行性与优势。

Conclusion: VR共享控制框架能有效结合自主规划与人类智能，提升多无人机在未知环境中的导航灵活性和鲁棒性。

Abstract: While autonomous multi-robots can achieve safe and coordinated navigation, they often struggle to adapt to unforeseen conditions and to capture operator-driven objectives in unstructured environments. We present a Virtual Reality (VR)-based shared control framework for teams of drones operating in constrained and unknown environments, enabling real-time, user-guided exploration. At the core of our approach is a novel, user-guided motion-primitive-based planner that computes continuous, collision-free trajectories while continuously integrating operator input. This planner is coupled with an admittance controller, allowing the operator to flexibly influence team behavior and guide drones toward regions of interest that autonomous planners may overlook. The system supports mixed-reality operations with both physical and simulated drones, and implements a bilateral VR-based interface, allowing the operator to guide the robot team via migration points while receiving immediate visual feedback of the team state. Experimental results show that shared control improves obstacle avoidance, maintains inter-agent spacing, and reduces operator effort, demonstrating the feasibility and advantages of immersive, human-in-the-loop multi-robot navigation.

</details>


### [27] [Distributed Multi-Coverage for Robot Swarms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21686)
*Mariem Guitouni,Aaron T. Becker*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种分布式多覆盖算法，使无人机集群在仅依赖局部感知和通信的条件下，能根据资产重要性实现冗余覆盖，无需全局协调。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实际部署中无人机集群面临通信范围有限、机载计算资源受限、部分系统故障需避免任务中止等约束，而现有集中式整数规划方法无法满足分布式需求。

Method: 设计了一种分布式多覆盖算法，集群中的机器人仅通过局部感知和局部通信进行协作，无全局协调机制。

Result: 该算法能在局部约束下实现按资产重要性分级的冗余覆盖，并容忍部分机器人故障。

Conclusion: 所提出的分布式方法有效解决了实际部署中通信、计算和鲁棒性方面的挑战，为无人机集群在关键资产监控等任务中提供了可行的多覆盖解决方案。

Abstract: Autonomous drone swarms deployed for surveillance, environmental monitoring, and infrastructure inspection must maintain reliable coverage of critical assets despite robot failures. This requires multicoverage: each asset must be observed by multiple robots for redundancy, with coverage requirements varying by asset importance. While recent work has solved the centralized problem optimally using integer programming, practical deployments face constraints that demand distributed solutions: robots operate with limited communication ranges, onboard computation restricts global planning, and partial system failures must not cause mission abort. We present a distributed multicoverage algorithm for robot swarms operating with local sensing, local communication, and no global coordination.

</details>


### [28] [Motion Design for Grasp-Based Dynamic Locomotion in Microgravity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21704)
*Chaerim Moon,Joohyung Kim,Justin K. Yim*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文研究了多肢体机器人在微重力环境下利用稀疏不规则锚点进行动态抓取移动的设计参数，提出了可参数化的移动规划框架，通过两种四足形态的物理仿真验证，发现扩大可行接触力空间和减弱冲击性全身动力学能提升移动性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 微重力环境下的移动常依赖稀疏且不规则的锚点，需要多肢体抓取式移动，但动态移动受到运动学和动力学耦合约束的挑战，缺乏系统的设计参数研究。

Method: 提出可参数化的移动规划框架，支持步态模式、步幅、移动速度和标称姿态等参数的变化；采用两种代表性四足形态在物理仿真中评估移动性能（稳定性和驱动力需求）。

Result: 扩大可行接触力空间和减弱冲击性全身动力学可有效提升微重力环境下的抓取式动态移动性能。

Conclusion: 这些发现为多肢体系统在微重力环境下的接触配置选择和全身协调策略提供了设计指导。

Abstract: Locomotion in microgravity often relies on sparsely and irregularly arranged anchors, motivating grasp-based mobility with multiple limbs. In this setting, dynamic locomotion is feasible only through deliberate regulation of both anchored interactions and whole-body coordination under coupled dynamic and kinematic constraints. This paper presents design insights for grasp-based dynamic locomotion with multi-limbed robotic systems in microgravity, targeting scenarios that require 6D limb manipulation to establish contacts with candidate anchors. The investigated design parameters include gait pattern, stride length, locomotion speed, and nominal posture. A parameterizable locomotion planning framework is proposed to support variations of these parameters and to evaluate the resulting locomotion performance in terms of stability and actuation demand. Two representative quadruped morphologies are adopted for evaluation in physics-based simulation. The results demonstrate that enlarging the feasible contact wrench space and attenuating impulsive whole-body dynamics improve locomotion performance. These findings inform strategies for contact configuration selection and whole-body coordination in microgravity locomotion with multi-limbed systems.

</details>


### [29] [Closed-Loop Sim-to-Real Reinforcement Learning for Deformable Microfiber Shape Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21688)
*Alessandro Amici,Houari Bettahar,Veeti Jaakkola,Quan Zhou*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种闭环仿真到现实（sim-to-real）强化学习方法，用于微纤维在表面上的形状控制。该方法在简化无摩擦仿真器中训练几何形状调节策略，通过实时视觉反馈在部署中迭代修正未建模的表面相互作用影响。在不进行重训练或域自适应的情况下，策略直接迁移到物理双夹持微操作系统（40 Hz），在多种直径（50-120 μm）和操作长度（10-20 mm）的丝微纤维上实现了亚毫米级形状误差，验证了闭环反馈可补偿仿真与现实不匹配效应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于接触的微操作因微尺度表面和界面相互作用难以精确建模，传统基于模型的控制和仿真到现实学习受到限制。作者旨在解决这一挑战，提出一种不依赖精确物理模型的微纤维形状控制方法。

Method: 采用闭环仿真到现实强化学习方法：在简化无摩擦仿真器中训练RL策略（几何形状调节），部署时通过实时视觉反馈（40 Hz）闭环修正未建模的表面相互作用效应。策略直接迁移到物理双夹持微操作系统，无需重训练或域自适应。

Result: 在24种不同初始构型上，策略实现平均点状形状误差270±80 μm。在9个样本（3种直径×3种操作长度）上，无需重训练或重调参数即可实现亚毫米级最终形状误差。

Conclusion: 在简化仿真器中学习的策略，只要任务相关的仿真-现实不匹配效应在闭环反馈回路中保持可观测且可修正，就能在表面接触下实现可重复的实世界微纤维形状调节。

Abstract: Autonomous contact-based micromanipulation is challenging because surface and interfacial interactions at the microscale are difficult to model accurately, limiting the use of conventional model-based control and sim-to-real learning. We present a closed-loop sim-to-real reinforcement learning (RL) approach for microfiber shape control on a surface. The central idea is to train geometric shape regulation in a simplified frictionless simulator and rely on real-time visual feedback during deployment to iteratively correct the observed effects of unmodeled surface interactions. An RL policy trained entirely in simulation is transferred directly to a physical dual-gripper micromanipulation system operating at 40 Hz, without retraining or domain adaptation. Using silk microfibers as a testbed, the policy achieves a mean point-wise shape error of 270 $\pm$ 80 $μ$m across twenty-four diverse initial configurations. Across nine specimens covering all combinations of three fiber diameters (50, 80, and 120 $μ$m) and three manipulated lengths (10 mm, 15mm, and 20 mm), the same policy achieves sub-millimeter final shape error without any retraining or retuning. These results show that a policy learned in a simplified simulator can achieve repeatable real-world microfiber shape regulation under surface contact, provided that the task-relevant effects of the sim-to-real mismatch remain observable and correctable within the closed feedback loop.

</details>


### [30] [Mind the Gaps: Multi-Robot Feedback-Driven Ergodic Coverage in Unknown Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21719)
*Thales Costa Silva,Nora Ayanian*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种自适应覆盖策略，利用实时环境模型反馈和遍历轨迹优化，使多机器人团队能够在未知先验分布的环境中动态调整采样行为，提高覆盖效率和资源分配。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的遍历搜索方法假设已知目标分布，但在实际环境中，环境的先验分布往往是未知的，这限制了多机器人自适应覆盖的效果。需要一种能够在线适应未知环境条件的策略。

Method: 基于参数化环境模型构建目标空间信息分布，并在遍历轨迹优化中融合实时反馈，在线更新模型，动态优先覆盖高兴趣区域，同时为每个智能体合成有效的控制策略。

Result: 通过仿真验证，该方法在覆盖效率和资源分配方面优于传统方法。

Conclusion: 提出的自适应覆盖框架能够有效应对未知先验分布的环境，提升多机器人系统的动态采样和资源优化能力。

Abstract: In this work, we address the problem of multi-robot adaptive coverage, where teams of robots perform dynamic sampling by continuously adjusting their positions to collect data in an environment. This task can be challenging, particularly when robots must be efficiently allocated to new sampling locations over time. Ergodic search methods optimize robot trajectories by ensuring that the robots' time-averaged spatial distribution aligns with the spatial distribution of environmental information. While these methods promote effective exploration provided a target distribution, they often fail to account for unknown prior distributions of the environment. To overcome this limitation, we propose an adaptive coverage strategy that utilizes real-time feedback from an environmental model to adjust robot sampling behavior in response to unknown conditions. Our approach enhances traditional ergodic trajectory optimization by constructing a target spatial information distribution based on parametric models of the environment, which are updated online. This strategy assumes that the environment is either static or changes slowly compared to the robot's motion. Our framework allows robots to dynamically prioritize regions of high interest, improving coverage efficiency, synthesizing effective control policies for individual agents, and optimizing resource use in settings with unknown prior distributions. We validate our approach through simulations, demonstrating its effectiveness in enhancing coverage and resource allocation.

</details>


### [31] [PGDG: Physically Grounded Data Generation for Robust Bimanual Policy Learning from a Single Demonstration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21710)
*Cunxi Dai,Haoran Chang,Aditya Nisal,Rahul Kumar,Guofei Chen,Tao Chen,Yuzhe Qin,Guanya Shi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: PGDG是一种零样本数据生成框架，通过将单个演示扩展为包含多样化恢复行为的紧凑数据集，显著提升了双臂接触式操作的行为克隆性能，在仿真和真实世界中均取得大幅成功率提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 接触丰富的双臂操作的行为克隆面临两个核心挑战：多样化演示数据采集成本高昂，且微小扰动会导致系统进入脱离流行状态（off-manifold states），而在此状态下缺乏恢复监督信号。

Method: PGDG采用物理驱动采样器与数据集策展器之间的迭代机制。策展器选择信息量大、非冗余且可恢复的行为来更新采样分布，使其覆盖不足的恢复模式；采样器从更新后的分布中抽取物理可行的轨迹候选并保留成功轨迹。此外，通过短视采样控制对选中的高风险状态重新标注纠正动作以提升数据质量。

Result: 在四项双臂操作任务中，PGDG在仿真和零样本真实世界迁移中均持续优于仅空间增强方法。RotateBox-Pitch任务中，仿真成功率从38%提升至93%，真实世界从35%提升至82%。同时，PGDG有效支持GR00T等基础模型微调，成功率从46%提升至77%。

Conclusion: PGDG通过零样本数据生成框架，从单条演示中构建高质量恢复行为数据集，有效解决了接触丰富双臂操作中行为克隆的泛化与鲁棒性问题，展示了在仿真与真实世界中的显著优势。

Abstract: Behavior cloning for contact-rich bimanual manipulation remains challenging because diverse demonstrations are expensive to collect, and even small disturbances can push the system into off-manifold states where no recovery supervision is available. We propose PGDG, a data generation framework with zero-shot curation that expands a single demonstration into a compact dataset of physically plausible, successful, and diverse recovery behaviors without additional human labeling. PGDG iterates between a physics-grounded sampler and a dataset curator, where the curator selects informative, non-redundant, and recoverable behaviors to update the sampling distribution toward under-covered recovery modes, and the sampler draws physically plausible rollout candidates from this updated distribution and retains successful trajectories. To further improve data quality, PGDG applies short-horizon sampling-based control to relabel selected risky states with corrective actions. Across four bimanual manipulation tasks, PGDG consistently outperforms spatial-only augmentation in both simulation and zero-shot real-world transfer. On RotateBox-Pitch, success improves from 38% to 93% in simulation and from 35% to 82% in the real world. PGDG also enables effective foundation models fine-tuning such as GR00T, increasing success from 46% to 77%. Additional results are available in our website: https://cunxid.github.io/PGDG/.

</details>


### [32] [Safe and Steerable Geometric Motion Policies for Robotic Dexterous Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21811)
*Albert Wu,Riccardo Bonalli,Thomas Lew,C. Karen Liu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出Safe Pullback Bundle Dynamical Systems (SafePBDS)，一种几何一致的框架，能够在任意任务流形上从目标和安全需求计算最优、可证明安全的配置流形加速度。该方法包含两个创新：pullback控制障碍函数构造和任务流形动作接口。在23-DOF Franka Panda-Allegro Hand平台上验证，灵巧抓取成功率达92.5%，并通过一维动作实现94.4%的三指抓取成功率，同时首次实现基于模型的、全驱动的掌心向下手中重定向，超过360°偏航旋转。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人灵巧操作需要在异构几何空间上不断协调目标和约束：机器人可能在ℝ⁷配置流形上控制，同时需要跟踪SE(3)上的末端执行器位姿并满足ℝ中的避障裕度。现有方法缺乏几何一致的框架来处理这种异构空间上的安全约束与目标协调问题。

Method: SafePBDS包含两个核心创新：1) Pullback控制障碍函数构造，将任务流形上的安全条件转换为配置流形加速度上的线性约束；2) 任务流形动作接口，允许高层策略注入低维残差运动，零输入时恢复自主行为，任意输入下均保持安全性。

Result: 在23-DOF Franka Panda-Allegro Hand平台上，灵巧抓取实验在20个家用物品、120次试验中达到92.5%成功率；通过一维动作接口实现任意一个手指的排除，在3个物体36次试验中达到94.4%的三指抓取成功率；首次实现基于模型的、全驱动的掌心向下手中重定向，在变化物体重量和手腕运动下超过360°偏航旋转。

Conclusion: SafePBDS提供了一个几何一致的框架，有效解决了异构几何空间上的安全约束与目标协调问题，实现了高效规划和安全保证，在灵巧抓取和手中重定向任务上表现出色。

Abstract: Robotic dexterous manipulation requires continuously reconciling objectives and constraints defined on heterogeneous geometric spaces: a robot controlled on a $\mathbb{R}^7$ configuration manifold may need to track end effector poses on $\mathrm{SE}(3)$ while satisfying obstacle avoidance margins in $\mathbb{R}$. We present Safe Pullback Bundle Dynamical Systems (SafePBDS), a geometrically consistent framework that computes optimal, certifiably safe configuration manifold accelerations from objectives and safety requirements on arbitrary task manifolds. SafePBDS builds on prior work that combines predefined task manifold dynamical systems to produce autonomous motion. Its first innovation is a pullback control barrier function construction, which converts task manifold safety conditions into linear constraints on configuration manifold accelerations. The second innovation is a task manifold action interface that allows a high-level policy to inject low dimensional residual motions; zero input recovers the autonomous behavior, while safety is preserved under arbitrary inputs. This lets high-level policies efficiently steer exploration while leaving precise motion to the autonomous behavior. We validate SafePBDS in simulation and on a 23-DOF Franka Panda-Allegro Hand platform. On dexterous grasping, SafePBDS achieves a $92.5\%$ success rate across 20 household objects and 120 trials. Using the action interface, the method can exclude any one of the four fingers during grasping via a one-dimensional action, achieving $94.4\%$ 3-finger grasp success across 3 objects and 36 trials. The efficient planning and safety guarantee of SafePBDS also enables the first model-based, fully actuated palm-down in-hand reorientation, exceeding $360^\circ$ of yaw rotation in both directions under varying object weight and wrist motion. Demo video and details: https://tml.stanford.edu/safe-pbds

</details>


### [33] [Analytical and Experimental Force Analysis of a Soft Linear Pneumatic Actuator](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21836)
*Mohammed Abboodi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文针对线性软 sleeve 致动器（LSSA）建立了准静态分析模型，通过实验验证了其力输出随位移、压力、负载和轴向刚度的耦合变化规律，发现125 kPa下力从112 N（零位移）降至接近0（40 mm位移），静态负载会延迟并降低力输出。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 软 sleeve 致动器（SSA）虽已提出用于可穿戴系统，但其力生成行为，尤其是输出力随伸长量的变化、外部负载影响以及轴向刚度的机械作用，尚未得到充分解释。

Method: 提出准静态解析模型，将净轴向力表达为气压在帽部和折叠壁上产生的贡献减去轴向刚度相关的力；模型包含内部压力、投影面积、折叠壁几何、轴向位移以及实验拟合的轴向刚度关系，并通过预设伸长和静态负载实验进行评估。

Result: 125 kPa下，致动器力从零伸长时的约112 N降至40 mm伸长时的接近0 N；静态负载延迟了可测量的力生成并降低了力输出，尤其在低中压力下显著。

Conclusion: LSSA的力生成受压力、几何、位移、负载和轴向刚度的耦合效应控制，模型能有效描述其行为。

Abstract: Soft sleeve actuators (SSAs) have recently been developed as a pneumatic actuation approach for wearable and assistive robotic systems. By integrating the actuation structure into a sleeve-like geometry, these actuators can reduce reliance on external attachment layers and transmission mechanisms while maintaining compliance with limb-shaped surfaces. However, the force-generation behavior of SSAs remains insufficiently explained, particularly with respect to the variation of output force during extension, the influence of external loading, and the mechanical role of axial stiffness. This paper presents an analytical and experimental force analysis of a linear soft sleeve actuator (LSSA). A quasi-static analytical model was developed by expressing the net axial force as the pressure-generated contribution from the cap and folded walls, reduced by the force associated with axial stiffness. The model incorporates internal pressure, projected pressure areas, folded wall geometry, axial displacement, and an experimentally fitted axial stiffness relation. Prescribed-extension and static-load experiments were conducted to evaluate the actuator response. At 125 kPa, the generated force decreased from approximately 112 N at zero extension to nearly zero at 40 mm. Static loading delayed measurable force generation and reduced force output, particularly at low and intermediate pressures. The results show that LSSA force generation is governed by coupled effects of pressure, geometry, displacement, loading, and axial stiffness.

</details>


### [34] [Superhuman Safe and Agile Racing through Multi-Agent Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22748)
*Ismail Geles,Leonard Bauersfeld,Markus Wulfmeier,Davide Scaramuzza*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文证明多智能体强化学习（MARL）能够为真实世界的交互提供安全保障。以高速四旋翼竞速为测试平台，通过联赛式自对弈训练智能体应对复杂空气动力学交互和战术机动，在超过22 m/s的速度下超越人类冠军飞行员，同时相比单智能体基线降低50%碰撞率，并能零样本泛化到更安全的人机交互。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自主系统在隔离或仿真环境中表现超人类，但在共享、动态的真实物理空间中仍然脆弱。这是因为主导的单智能体范式将其他参与者忽略或视为环境噪声，无法实现有效协调。

Method: 采用多智能体强化学习（MARL）框架，以高速四旋翼竞速为高保真测试环境，通过联赛式自对弈（league-based self-play）训练智能体处理可变数量的竞速者，学习复杂空气动力学交互（如下洗气流）和策略机动（如主动碰撞规避、超车）。

Result: 在超过22 m/s速度的多机竞速中，智能体超越人类冠军级飞行员；相比最先进单智能体基线，碰撞率降低50%；通过多样化人工智能体训练实现零样本泛化到更安全的人机交互。

Conclusion: 实现鲁棒机器人共存的关键不在于孤立的单智能体安全约束，而在于多智能体交互的严苛训练要求。

Abstract: Autonomous systems have achieved superhuman performance in isolation or simulation, yet they remain brittle in shared, dynamic real-world spaces. This failure stems from the dominant single-agent paradigm for physical applications, where other actors are ignored or treated as environmental noise, preventing effective coordination. Here we show that multi-agent reinforcement learning provides the essential safety scaffolding required for real-world interaction. Using high-speed quadrotor racing as a high-stakes testbed, we train agents to navigate complex aerodynamic interactions and strategic maneuvering with a variable number of racers. Through league-based self-play, agents evolve sophisticated anticipatory behaviors, including proactive collision avoidance, overtaking, and handling multi-agent physical interactions, including aerodynamic downwash. Our agents outperform a champion-level human pilot in multi-player races at speeds exceeding 22 m/s, while simultaneously reducing collision rates by 50 % compared to state-of-the-art single-agent baselines. Crucially, training with diverse artificial agents enables zero-shot generalization to safer human interaction. These results suggest that the path to robust robotic co-existence lies not in isolated safety constraints, but in the rigorous demands of multi-agent interaction. Multimedia materials are available at: https://rpg.ifi.uzh.ch/marl

</details>

*Chushan Zhang,Ruihan Lu,Jinguang Tong,Xuesong Li,Yikai Wang,Hongdong Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: EvoScene-VLA提出了一种跨动作块的持久性场景先验机制，通过循环场景前缀在视觉-语言-动作模型中维持动作更新的场景状态，在31个RoboTwin任务和Galaxea R1-Lite真实机器人上均取得了优于现有方法的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的分块VLA策略在预测多步控制时，每次更新仅依赖当前视觉观测，忽略了动作执行过程中发生的接触、遮挡和物体运动等几何变化，导致后续决策缺乏动作更新的场景先验信息。

Method: 提出EvoScene-VLA，包含：(1) 循环场景前缀（Recurrent Scene Prefix），在VLM调用间传递几何感知的场景状态；(2) 动作解码器同时输出下一动作块和紧凑的场景更新；(3) 训练时使用Scene Predictor提供未来场景token目标，Geometric Anchor将场景slot与冻结的深度和3D教师模型对齐，推理时移除这两个模块。

Result: 在31个RoboTwin任务上，固定评估从87.2%提升至89.1%，随机评估从86.1%提升至88.5%；在Galaxea R1-Lite真实机器人上优于所有基线方法。

Conclusion: 在分块VLA策略中引入动作更新的持久场景先验是有效的，能够弥补视觉更新间隔期间的几何变化信息缺失，提升机器人操控的成功率。

Abstract: Chunked vision-language-action (VLA) policies predict multi-step robot controls, conditioning each update on the current visual observation alone. Yet robot actions cause contact, occlusion, and object motion, and the geometry that later decisions depend on can change before the next visual update arrives. Spatial VLAs improve current-frame geometry. Temporal VLAs aggregate past frames. Neither maintains an action-updated scene prior across chunks. We argue for a persistent action-updated scene state across control calls, and introduce EvoScene-VLA. Its recurrent scene prefix carries a geometry-aware scene state across chunks. At each vision-language model (VLM) call, the VLM combines scene information from the current observation with the action-updated prior from the previous chunk; the action decoder outputs both the next action chunk and a compact scene update. This update becomes the next prior, which the VLM corrects against the new observation when the next call arrives. Each control call therefore starts from a scene prior that reflects both recent actions and fresh visual evidence. During training, \textbf{Scene Predictor} supplies future scene-token targets, and Geometric Anchor aligns scene slots with frozen depth and 3D teachers. We discard both modules at deployment. On 31 RoboTwin tasks, EvoScene-VLA raises average success from 87.2% to 89.1% in fixed evaluation and from 86.1% to 88.5% in randomized evaluation. On the Galaxea R1-Lite real robot, EvoScene-VLA outperforms all baselines.

</details>

*Jonathan Reasoner,Nicola Bezzo*

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种动态认知规划框架，使多机器人在无通信环境下通过高阶信念推理实现隐式协调与长时域规划，并在仿真和物理实验中显著优于一阶基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在无通信环境中，多机器人系统无法进行常规的信息交换，传统协调策略失效，亟需一种不依赖显式通信的鲁棒协调方法。

Method: 提出动态认知规划框架，包含：1) 高阶信念粒子的生成与传播；2) 基于贝叶斯推断的世界信念更新；3) 通过行为树选择以预测队友决策的动作；4) 结合时间感知的MPPI控制器将推理集成到底层轨迹执行中。

Result: 在仿真和物理实验中，所提框架相较于仅使用一阶信念的基线方法，持续减少了任务完成时间。

Conclusion: 认知逻辑可作为通信受限环境下鲁棒协调的基础，为无通信多机器人系统提供有效的隐式协调能力。

Abstract: In communicationless environments, multi-robot systems must operate without the constant information exchange that many coordination strategies typically assume. This paper presents a novel dynamic epistemic planning framework that enables implicit coordination and long horizon planning through higher-order reasoning among robots. With our approach, robots form and propagate higher-order belief particles, update world beliefs using Bayesian inference, and select actions via a behavior tree that anticipates teammates' likely decisions. A temporally aware Model Predictive Path Integral (MPPI) controller integrates this reasoning into low-level execution, allowing robots to plan intercepts and adapt trajectories under partial observability. The proposed framework is evaluated in both simulations and physical experiments, where it consistently reduces task completion time compared to a first-order baseline, demonstrating that epistemic logic can serve as a robust foundation for resilient coordination in communication-restricted domains.

</details>

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于低成本自主无人机(UAV)的非接触式振动结构损伤检测方法。通过视觉运动跟踪从视频中提取振动信号，识别固有频率变化以判断结构退化。实验在实验室框架结构上进行，使用两台智能手机、USB摄像头和搭载自主对准系统的自制低成本无人机，在GPS拒止环境下验证了该方法。结果表明，所有平台均能成功捕获基频及其损伤引起的偏移，无人机虽因平台干扰误差略高(最高5.7%)，但能可靠检测损伤，且成本远低于商业系统。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统接触式传感器在结构健康监测中存在部署困难、成本高、可达性差等问题，尤其在难以接近或危险环境中。开发一种低成本、自主、非接触的无人机系统进行振动检测，可解决上述局限性，提供灵活可扩展的结构健康监测方案。

Method: 基于视觉的运动跟踪从视频中提取振动信号；采用低成本无人机搭载机载摄像头和自主对准系统（适用于GPS拒止环境）；将位移时域信号转换至频域分析；对比接触式加速度计和有限元模型参考测量值进行验证。

Result: 所有平台（智能手机、USB摄像头、无人机）均成功捕获了结构的基频和损伤引起的频率偏移。无人机虽存在平台扰动和感知限制，误差最高达5.7%，但仍能可靠检测损伤引起的频率变化，且成本显著低于商用无人机系统。

Conclusion: 低成本的自主无人机为结构健康监测提供了实用、灵活且可扩展的解决方案，尤其适用于接触式传感不可行的场景。研究还支持部署多架协作无人机以进一步增强检测覆盖范围和鲁棒性的潜力。

Abstract: This paper presents a non-contact approach for vibration-based structural damage detection using an autonomous and customized cost-effective unmanned aerial vehicle (UAV). Vibration signals are extracted from video recordings through vision-based motion tracking to identify shifts in natural frequencies indicative of structural degradation. A laboratory-scale frame structure is evaluated under healthy and simulated-damage conditions. The proposed system is validated through an experimental study involving two smartphones, a USB camera, and a custom-built low-cost UAV equipped with an onboard camera and an autonomous alignment system for operation in GPS-denied environments. The displacement time is extracted and analyzed in the frequency domain and compared to reference measurements from contact accelerometers and a finite element model. Experimental results show that all platforms successfully capture the fundamental frequency and its shift due to damage. Although the UAV exhibits slightly higher errors (up to 5.7%) due to platform-induced disturbances and sensing limitations, it reliably detects damage-induced frequency changes. Compared to commercial UAV systems, the proposed platform achieves comparable inspection performance at significantly lower cost. These results demonstrate that low-cost autonomous UAVs provide a practical, flexible, and scalable solution for structural health monitoring, particularly in scenarios where contact-based sensing is impractical. The findings also support the potential for the deployment of multiple cooperative UAVs to further enhance inspection coverage and robustness.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 突破现有沉浸技术的局限，弥合真实与虚拟世界之间的鸿沟，实现用户身体与虚拟环境的全身心、动态物理交互。

Method: 采用受自然启发的模块化机器人表面单元，构建可扩展、分布式的平台，通过分布式力、形状和运动反馈实现全身沉浸，动态实时适应交互行为。

Result: 实现了从个人到多用户场景的可扩展沉浸式环境，提供了前所未有的全身物理交互体验，增强了用户在虚拟空间中的参与深度。
