Abstract: Humanity is at the forefront of yet another digital revolution, where the lines between real and virtual worlds are dissolving, reshaping how we perceive and interact with our surroundings. In this context, we introduce a transformative paradigm for immersive virtual experiences centered around whole-body kinetic interactions. Our approach redefines immersion through three distinct levels: audio-visual immersion, capturing sensory realism; physical immersion, delivering haptic feedback; and full-body immersion (FBI), where dynamic bodily interaction integrates seamlessly with virtual environments. At the core of this innovation lies a scalable, distributable platform based on modular robotic surface units inspired by the adaptive designs of nature. These units enable the rendering of immersive environments at any scale, from intimate personal experiences to expansive multi-user settings, dynamically adapting to interactions in real-time. The modular system distributes force, shape, and motion feedback throughout entire spaces, replicating the physical characteristics of the environment and enabling new depth of engagement through FBI. By combining scalability, adaptability, and dynamic physical engagement, this framework bridges the gap between real and virtual worlds. It offers an unprecedented level of immersion where users can engage their entire bodies in symbiotic interactions with the virtual space. This work not only advances immersive technology but also redefines how humans and virtual environments coexist, setting a foundation for a new era of human-environment synthesis.

</details>


### [39] [Learning to Evolve: Multi-modal Interactive Fields for Robust Humanoid Navigation in Dynamic Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21935)
*Peifeng Jiang,Hong Liu,Jin Jin,Wenshuai Wang,Xia Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出多模态交互场（MIF），一种面向人形机器人的闭环感知-自适应系统，通过置信度感知的语义3D高斯泼溅、差异触发的空间记忆更新和任务驱动的几何重建，在动态环境中实现可靠的操纵导向导航，将重定位成功率从12%提升至94%，并减少91.4%的语义内存占用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现存的语义建图和场景图系统难以直接部署于人形机器人，因为它们通常假设稳定的相机轨迹、静态环境或粗略的物体几何，无法应对 locomotion 引起的感知失真、环境变化以及交互级几何安全约束。

Method: MIF耦合三个场：不确定性感知的3DGS外观场（抑制步态引起的模糊）、空间场（维护拓扑记忆）和几何场（支持操作前的交互位姿安全）。通过差异检测分数区分 locomotion 引起的假阳性变化与持久变化，仅更新局部不一致区域。

Result: 在实时动态办公室场景中，MIF将非静态环境下的重定位成功率从12%提升至94%，并通过特征蒸馏将语义内存占用减少91.4%。

Conclusion: MIF通过闭环感知-自适应管道有效解决了人形机器人在动态环境下的操纵导向导航问题，显著提升了可靠性和效率。

Abstract: Safe manipulation-oriented navigation for humanoid robots requires scene memory that remains reliable under locomotion-induced perceptual distortion, environmental changes, and interaction-level geometric safety constraints. Existing semantic mapping and scene-graph systems are difficult to deploy directly in this setting because they often assume stable camera trajectories, static environments, or coarse object geometry. We introduce the Multi-modal Interactive Field (MIF), a humanoid-oriented system that integrates confidence-aware semantic 3D Gaussian Splatting, discrepancy-triggered spatial memory updates, and task-driven geometric reconstruction within a closed-loop perception-adaptation pipeline. MIF couples three fields: an uncertainty-aware 3DGS Appearance Field that suppresses gait-induced blur, a Spatial Field that maintains topological memory, and a Geometry Field that supports Interaction Pose Safety (IPS) before manipulation. A discrepancy detection score is introduced to separate locomotion-induced false-positive changes from persistent changes and updates only locally inconsistent regions. On a Unitree-G1 humanoid in a real dynamic office, MIF improves relocation success in non-static environments from 12% to 94% compared with static scene-graph memory, while reducing semantic memory footprint by 91.4% through feature distillation for practical online operation. Project page and code: https://ziya-jiang.github.io/MIF-homepage/

</details>


### [40] [A Visitation Grid for Complete Coverage Foraging in Robot Swarms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21947)
*Qi Arturo Gonzalez,Yifeng Gao,Li Zhang,Qi Lu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于网格的随机随机觅食策略，通过维护全局访问密度估计来减少机器人冗余访问，在最终收集阶段将总收集时间缩短高达33%，收集效率提升超过48%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有群集觅食算法在收集大部分资源时表现良好，但在最终阶段（仅剩少量稀疏资源时）效率极低，消耗了大量任务时间。需要一种能提升末期收集效率、同时保持可扩展性的策略。

Method: 将未知搜索区域划分为网格地图，由轻量级中央服务器维护。服务器根据机器人报告的位置更新网格级访问计数，生成全局探索密度估计。每个机器人在新的觅食行程中，从其局部3×3邻域网格中按访问计数最低的概率选择下一搜索区域，偏向未充分探索区域同时保持随机性。

Result: 仿真实验表明：相比经典CPFA算法，总收集时间最多降低33%；最终阶段收集效率提升超过48%。策略在有限内存和计算资源下具备鲁棒性、灵活性和可扩展性。

Conclusion: 该网格随机觅食策略能有效加速机器人集群在未知大环境中的完全资源收集，尤其显著提升末期收集效率，可作为现有随机群集觅食方法的通用增强模块。

Abstract: The complete collection of sparse resources in large, unknown environments remains a challenging problem for autonomous robot swarms. Previous studies have shown that a substantial portion of total mission time is consumed during the final stage of collection, where only a small fraction of randomly scattered resources remain. Consequently, many existing swarm foraging algorithms (search and collection) focus on collecting most resources within a limited time window, rather than improving end-stage efficiency for collecting all resources. We propose a grid-based stochastic foraging strategy that explicitly reduces redundant visits and accelerates late-stage collection. The unknown search area is partitioned into a grid map, which is maintained by a lightweight central server. To maintain scalability, both robots and the server operate within limited memory and computational constraints. The server updates the grid-level visitation counts based on robot-reported locations, producing a global estimate of the exploration density. For each new foraging trip, a robot selects its next search area from a local 3 X 3 neighborhood of grids probabilistically with the lowest visitation count, thus biasing exploration toward under-visited regions while maintaining stochasticity. Extensive simulation experiments demonstrate that the proposed strategy consistently outperforms the canonical centrally placed baseline foraging algorithm (CPFA). Compared to CPFA, the proposed method reduces the total collection time by up to 33% and improves collection efficiency by more than 48% during the final stage of the mission. These results indicate that the proposed strategy is robust, flexible, and scalable for near-complete and complete resource collection in robot swarms and can serve as a general enhancement for stochastic swarm foraging methods under limited onboard resources.

</details>


### [41] [TacO: Benchmarking Tactile Sensors for Object Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21976)
*Anya Zorin,Zilin Si,Myungsun Park,Junsung Park,Alexiy Buynitsky,Sachin Bhadang,Taejun Park,Sohee John Yoon,Yong-Lae Park,Oliver Kroemer,Zeynep Temel,Michael T. Tolley,Sha Yi,Xiaolong Wang*

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管触觉传感被认为能改善机器人操作，但缺乏经验性指导来明确哪种触觉传感器最适合哪种操作任务。

Method: 针对四种不同模态（视觉、声学、磁学、电阻）的触觉传感器，在三个任务（未知质量拾取放置、物体重定向、插头插入）上分别训练操作策略，并分析传感器属性（空间分辨率、剪切传感、触觉表征）和材料摩擦对任务性能的影响。

Result: 触觉信息的效用并非普遍一致，而是强烈依赖于传感器模态、材料属性和具体操作任务。

Conclusion: 提供了基于操作策略性能的触觉传感器选择与评估框架，所有传感器、代码、数据和硬件将公开。

Abstract: Vision-based learning from demonstrations has achieved remarkable success in enabling robots to perform manipulation tasks and high-level semantic reasoning, yet it remains insufficient for complex, contact-rich manipulation. While there is broad agreement that tactile sensing improves manipulation, there is no empirical guidance on which tactile sensors are best suited for which manipulation tasks. In this paper, we provide a systematic, task-driven evaluation of tactile sensors for robot manipulation and propose a framework for selecting and evaluating sensors based on manipulation policy performance. Separate manipulation policies are trained for tactile sensors of four distinct modalities: visual, acoustic, magnetic, and resistive, across three tasks: pick-and-place with unknown mass, object reorientation, and plug insertion. For each task, an analysis of how sensor properties such as spatial resolution, shear sensing, and tactile representation, and the inherent material friction affect task performances is done. Rather than tactile sensing being universally beneficial in the same way, our results show that the usefulness of tactile information depends strongly on sensor modality, material properties, and the specific manipulation tasks. All of the tactile sensors, code, data, and hardware setup will be publicly available on the project website.

</details>


### [42] [Industrial Dual-Arm Box Handling via Online Inertial Estimation and Convex Wrench Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22021)
*Kenzhi Iskandar Wong,Lin Yang,Qian Ying Lee,Domenico Campolo*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种摩擦感知的双臂箱体操作框架，通过在线估计未知惯性参数（质量和质心），并利用二阶锥规划在椭球摩擦极限面约束下计算摩擦可行的接触力和扭转力矩，实现了对未知惯性属性物体的稳定抓取，无需分别调整防滑和防挤压目标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工业机器人操作中，箱体等物体的质量和质心通常未知，这会影响提升时的力-力矩平衡，不当的接触力调节可能导致滑动、掉落、姿态偏差或过度挤压。

Method: 在线从测量的接触力估算物体质量和质心，通过二阶锥规划（SOCP）在椭球摩擦极限面约束下计算摩擦可行的接触力和扭转力矩，并包含离线轨迹优化阶段以减少不必要的物体-环境接触。

Result: 在实际双臂机器人系统上，针对不同质心配置的物体进行实验，证明了该方法能在保持稳定摩擦接触的同时提升未知惯性属性的物体。

Conclusion: 将摩擦可行性作为硬约束并在可行区域内最小化接触力，无需将防滑和防挤压作为独立调节的目标即可实现稳定提升。

Abstract: Industrial robotic object handling often involves boxes and packages whose mass and center of mass are not known in advance. These uncertainties affect the force--moment balance required for stable lifting, and improper regulation of contact wrenches can lead to slip, object drop, orientation deviation, or excessive squeezing. This paper presents a friction-aware dual-arm box-handling framework for objects with unknown inertial properties. The proposed approach estimates the object mass and center of mass online from measured contact wrenches, and computes friction-feasible contact forces and torsional moments through a second-order cone program (SOCP) under ellipsoidal friction-limit-surface constraints. An offline trajectory refinement stage is also included to reduce undesired object--environment contact when geometric constraints are present. By enforcing friction feasibility as a hard constraint and minimizing contact effort within the feasible region, the framework achieves stable lifting without treating slip avoidance and excessive squeezing as separately tuned objectives. Experiments on a real dual-arm robotic system under different center-of-mass configurations demonstrate that the method lifts objects with unknown inertial properties while maintaining stable frictional contact.

</details>


### [43] [CoRMA: Contrastive RMA for Contact-Rich Meta-Adaptation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22082)
*Wentian Wang,Chutong Wen,Hongxu Ma,Wuhao Wang,Zhexiong Xue,Abdul Haseeb Nizamani,Dandi Zhou,Xinhai Sun,Jianqiao Zhu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: CoRMA是一种基于上下文的元适应框架，通过紧凑的6D语义接触上下文和因果Transformer适配器，在无需演示、特权输入或梯度更新的情况下实现力控装配任务的在线适应，在多种装配任务中相比FORGE基线在真实硬件上取得了更高的成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的机器人运动适配方法（如RMA）在力主导的装配任务中表现不佳，而FORGE等基线方法在仿真中成功率高但在真实硬件上性能严重下降，亟需一种无需演示、特权输入或梯度更新的跨域适应方法，以弥合仿真到现实的差距。

Method: CoRMA将RMA的原始模拟器参数适应替换为紧凑的6D模拟器专属语义接触上下文（描述接触起始、侧向接合、引导过渡、接触方向和卡滞状态），并部署因果Transformer适配器，利用语义回归和力状态对比目标从力、本体感知和动作历史中在线推断该上下文。部署时移除Oracle上下文并由推断上下文替代，从而实现无演示、无特权输入、无梯度更新的幕内适应。

Result: 在PegInsert、GearMesh和NutThread三种装配任务以及真实Marvin机械臂上评估，相比于FORGE基线（仿真成功率高但硬件上严重退化），CoRMA在受控目标位姿噪声下保留了更高的真实验证成功率。

Conclusion: 语义接触推断可作为相关装配任务族内可复用的适应接口，但更广泛的未见任务泛化和Real2Sim校准仍是未来工作。

Abstract: We present CoRMA(Contrastive Robotic Motor Adaptation), a context-based meta-adaptation framework that modifies RMA for force-dominant assembly. CoRMA replaces raw simulator-parameter adaptation with a compact 6D simulator-only semantic contact context describing contact onset, lateral engagement, guided transition, contact direction, and jamming. A deployable causal Transformer adapter infers this context online from force, proprioceptive, and action histories using semantic regression and a force-regime contrastive objective. At deployment, oracle context is removed and replaced by the inferred context, enabling within-episode adaptation without demonstrations, privileged inputs, or gradient updates. We evaluate CoRMA on PegInsert, GearMesh, and NutThread in Isaac Lab / Isaac Sim~5.0 and on a real Marvin arm. Compared with FORGE baselines that achieve high simulation success but degrade substantially on hardware, CoRMA retains higher verified real success under controlled target-pose noise. These results support semantic contact inference as a reusable adaptation interface within a related assembly task family, while broader unseen-task generalization and Real2Sim calibration remain future work.

</details>


### [44] [Beyond Pixels: Learning Invariant Rewards for Real-World Robotics From a Few Demonstrations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22123)
*Tengye Xu,Yangting Sun,Ziju Shen,Guanqi Chen,Zhen Fu,Chen yizhou,Hua Chen,Jia Pan*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种从少量演示中学习不变符号化奖励函数的框架，通过发现行为不变量而非拟合视觉特征，实现奖励在位置、视角和物体变化上的零样本泛化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于视觉的奖励模型倾向于记忆特定像素分布，难以泛化到实验室环境之外的真实世界操作问题中任务的各种变体（不同物体实例、位置和相机视角）。

Method: 框架包含两个耦合组件：1）结构化的奖励公式化，编码任务层策略和物理约束，同时保持最优策略不变性；2）混合符号-数值过程，从仅需5次演示中蒸馏出这些不变量，无需在线交互。

Result: 在8个Meta-World任务和3个Franka操作任务上，该方法在过程对齐和策略滚动排序能力上优于基线，加速了下游策略学习。三个真实世界分布外实验表明，学习的奖励在位置、视角和物体变化上实现了零样本泛化。

Conclusion: 通过从视觉特征拟合转向行为不变量发现，单一奖励表示可以跨多样化的任务变体重用，显著提升了奖励函数在实际机器人操作中的泛化能力。

Abstract: Designing reward functions that generalize beyond controlled laboratory settings remains a fundamental challenge in reinforcement learning for robotics. In open-world manipulation problems, a single task can appear in numerous variants through different object instances, positions, and camera viewpoints. Recent vision-based reward models tend to memorize specific pixel distributions and fail to generalize beyond their training conditions. To address this, we propose a framework that learns invariant symbolic reward functions from as few as five demonstrations. The insight is to shift from visual feature-fitting to the discovery of behavioral invariants: task-level properties that remain constant across diverse visual instantiations. The framework has two coupled components: a structural reward formulation that encodes task-level strategies and physical constraints while preserving optimal policy invariance, and a hybrid symbolic-numerical procedure that distills these invariants from demonstrations without online interaction. Experiments on eight Meta-World tasks and three Franka manipulation tasks demonstrate that our method achieves stronger process alignment and policy rollout ranking abilities compared to baselines, accelerating downstream policy learning. Three real-world out-of-distribution experiments further show that the same learned reward generalizes zero-shot to position, viewpoint, and object variations, enabling a single reward representation to be reused across diverse task variants in practice.

</details>


### [45] [Action with Visual Primitives](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22183)
*Weilong Guo,Yuchen Wang,Renping Zhou,Yunfeng Zhang,Rui Fang,Yue Meng,Wenda Xu,Yuan He,Gao Huang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: AVP是一种端到端架构，通过让VLM推理下一阶段目标并生成视觉基元令牌来条件化流匹配动作专家，解耦了认知感知与动作控制，在通用抓取放置任务上相比pi_0.5提升27.61%的成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLA模型将指令理解、空间感知和运动控制纠缠在单一学习目标中，导致动作专家需要隐式重新学习VLM已具备的认知和感知能力，限制了学习效率和泛化能力。

Method: 提出AVP架构：VLM推理下一阶段目标并发射视觉基元令牌，通过末端执行器运动学监督，利用流匹配动作专家生成动作，实现视觉基元为中心的解耦接口。

Result: 在通用抓取放置任务中，AVP相比pi_0.5提升27.61%的成功率，并在数据效率、空间组合泛化和物体级迁移上取得一致提升。

Conclusion: 通过视觉基元接口解耦认知感知与动作控制，能有效提升VLA模型的学习效率与泛化能力。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models have emerged as a promising paradigm for generalist robotic manipulation. A common design in current architectures maps language instructions and visual observations to actions in a single forward pass. While conceptually simple, this formulation entangles instruction comprehension, spatial scene understanding, and motor control within a single learning objective. As a result, the action expert must implicitly relearn cognitive and perceptual capabilities already present in the pretrained VLM, which can limit both learning efficiency and generalization. We introduce AVP (Action with Visual Primitives), an end-to-end architecture that implements this visual-primitive-centric interface: the VLM infers the next-stage target and emits visual-primitive tokens that condition a flow-matching action expert, with supervision derived from end-effector kinematics. Real-robot experiments on general pick-and-place tasks show that AVP improves the success rate by 27.61% over pi_0.5 and outperforms other recent methods, with consistent gains in data efficiency, spatial-compositional generalization, and object-level transfer.

</details>


### [46] [Learning A Unified Risk Map for Autonomous Driving in Partially Observable Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22189)
*Jie Jia,Yaofeng Su,Zeyu Bao,Yun Hong,Bingzhao Gao,Zhongxue Gan,Wenchao Ding*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种面向部分可观测环境的统一风险地图建模与学习框架，通过时空建模整合交通流风险和碰撞风险，并引入基于扩散的场景生成框架产生逼真且对抗性的遮挡交互场景，在Waymo数据集上显著提升了碰撞时间指标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法要么基于可达状态高估风险，要么在高遮挡不确定性下难以预测准确轨迹，缺乏对遮挡引起风险的细粒度评估能力。

Method: 提出统一风险地图框架，整合交通流风险与碰撞风险的时空建模；引入扩散模型生成对抗性遮挡交互场景。

Result: 在Waymo Open Motion Dataset上，最小碰撞时间提升0.78倍，平均碰撞时间提升1.67倍，显著超越现有遮挡感知基线方法。

Conclusion: 该框架为部分可观测环境下的风险感知规划提供了全面且实用的解决方案。

Abstract: Occlusion-aware prediction remains a critical challenge in autonomous driving due to the inherent uncertainty of unobserved regions. Existing approaches either overestimate risk based on reachable states or struggle to predict accurate trajectories under high occlusion uncertainty. To address these limitations, we propose a unified risk map modeling and learning framework for partially observable environments. Our method integrates traffic flow risk and collision risk through spatiotemporal modeling, enabling fine-grained assessment of occlusion-induced hazards. To address the scarcity of scenarios involving occluded interactions, we introduce a diffusion-based scenario generation framework that produces realistic yet adversarial scenarios. We integrate the modeling and learning of a unified risk map into a framework that supports risk-aware planning under partial observability. Experiments on the Waymo Open Motion Dataset show that our method significantly outperforms the state-of-the-art occlusion-aware baseline, improving minimum time-to-collision by 0.78 times and average time-to-collision by 1.67 times. The proposed framework offers a comprehensive and practical solution for risk-aware planning in partially observable environments.

</details>


### [47] [Imagine2Real: Towards Zero-shot Humanoid-Object Interaction via Video Generative Priors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22272)
*Jiahe Chen,ZiRui Wang,Feiyu Jia,Xiao Chen,Xiaojie Niu,Weishuai Zeng,Tianfan Xue,Xiaowei Zhou,Jiangmiao Pang,Jingbo Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: Imagine2Real提出了一种零样本人形物体交互框架，通过将机器人和物体运动统一为4D点轨迹，并仅追踪稀疏关键点（基座、双手、物体），利用行为基础模型的潜在空间作为搜索域，绕过重定向过程，实现无需几何先验的灵活交互。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 全身人形-物体交互受限于高保真3D数据的稀缺性。现有方法依赖几何先验（如显式CAD模型）导致表征对齐问题，且大量变形与形态不匹配带来重定向复杂性。

Method: 1) 将机器人和物体运动统一为4D点轨迹，解决表征对齐问题；2) 提出关键点追踪器，仅追踪基座、双手和物体的稀疏关键点，完全绕过重定向过程；3) 利用行为基础模型（BFM）的潜在空间作为追踪器的搜索域，以在稀疏信号下维持自然步态；4) 采用渐进训练策略，仅用简单追踪奖励即可学习鲁棒行为。

Result: 方法支持零样本物理部署于动捕系统中，无需针对特定物体或场景的预训练。

Conclusion: 通过统一4D轨迹表征和基于BFM潜在空间的关键点追踪，有效解决了表征对齐与重定向复杂性两大瓶颈，实现了灵活、无需几何先验的零样本人形物体交互。

Abstract: Whole-body Humanoid-Object Interaction (HOI) is bottlenecked by the scarcity of high-fidelity 3D data. While video generative priors offer a promising alternative, existing methods suffer from \textit{Representation Misalignment} due to their reliance on geometric priors (e.g., explicit CAD models), and \textit{Retargeting Complexity} arising from intensive morphing and morphological mismatch. We propose Imagine2Real, a zero-shot HOI framework for flexible, geometry-free interaction. To resolve misalignment, we formulate robot and object motions as unified 4D point trajectories. To overcome retargeting complexity, our Keypoints Tracker tracks only sparse critical points (base, hands, and object), entirely bypassing the error-amplifying retargeting process. To maintain natural gaits despite these sparse signals, we utilize the latent space of a Behavior Foundation Model (BFM) as the tracker's search domain. Using a progressive training strategy, Imagine2Real learns robust behaviors with simple tracking rewards, enabling zero-shot physical deployment within a motion capture(mocap) system.

</details>


### [48] [Spatial Memory for Out-of-Vision Manipulation in Vision-Language-Action](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22283)
*Pengteng Li,Weiyu Guo,He Zhang,Tiefu Cai,Xiao He,Yandong Guo,Hui Xiong*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: SOMA是一个为视觉-语言-动作（VLA）模型设计的空间记忆框架，通过多视角观测构建持久化空间语义记忆，使VLA能够在目标物体超出视野时仍能进行推理和操作，在五个真实世界任务中显著提升了成功率并实现了近单次抓取能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLA模型隐式假设任务相关物体始终可见，当目标超出相机视野时会导致操作行为脆弱且被动，缺乏对视野外物体的推理能力。

Method: SOMA由三个核心组件构成：(1) 空间记忆构建（Spatial Memory Construction），通过可移动头部相机扫描并聚合多视角观测为统一的空间语义表示；(2) 动态记忆精炼（Dynamic Memory Refinement），随时间维护全局一致性；(3) 上下文记忆检索（Contextual Memory Retrieval），在操作过程中激活与指令相关的空间线索。

Result: 在五个具有挑战性的真实世界视野外操作任务中，SOMA提升了任务成功率，并展现出定性不同的操作行为：更快的目标定位、减少视角搜索、在部分可观测条件下实现近单次抓取。在RoboCasa GR1和SimplerEnv上的全可观测设置实验进一步验证了其有效性。

Conclusion: SOMA通过引入持久化空间记忆有效解决了VLA模型在目标超出视野时的操作难题，不仅提升了任务性能，还从根本上改变了模型的操作行为模式，使其能够在部分可观测环境中进行高效推理与操作。

Abstract: We introduce SOMA, the Spatial Memory framework for Out-of-Vision Manipulation in Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models. Most existing VLAs implicitly assume that task-relevant objects are always visible, leading to brittle and reactive behaviors when targets fall outside the camera's field of view. SOMA addresses this limitation by equipping VLAs with a persistent spatial memory constructed from multi-view observations acquired via a movable head camera, enabling reasoning beyond the current visual frustum. The framework consists of three components: Spatial Memory Construction, which aggregates angular-wise observations into a unified spatial-semantic representation through scanning; Dynamic Memory Refinement, which maintains global consistency over time; and Contextual Memory Retrieval, which activates instruction-relevant spatial cues during manipulation. We evaluate SOMA on five challenging real-world out-of-vision manipulation tasks, including multi-step and dual-arm scenarios where target objects are initially invisible. Experimental results show that SOMA not only improves task success rates, but also induces qualitatively different manipulation behaviors, with faster target localization, reduced viewpoint search, and near one-shot grasping under partial observability. Additional experiments on RoboCasa GR1 and SimplerEnv further validate the effectiveness of SOMA's memory design under conventional fully observable settings. Code will be released soon.

</details>


### [49] [How can reasoning capability empower the AI copilot robot in endoscopic surgery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22322)
*Guankun Wang,Long Bai,Hongliang Ren*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文探讨了推理能力在基于视觉-语言-动作（VLA）模型的AI副手手术机器人中的应用潜力，提出通过多模态线索整合、手术意图理解和隐藏组织动力学推理，将机器人从被动执行者转变为认知协作者，以提升内镜手术的精准度、安全性和可持续性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 推理能力在通用领域的复杂逻辑推理和机器人决策中已取得显著进展，但在基于VLA模型的内镜手术AI副手机器人中尚未被充分探索。现有手术机器人多为被动执行者，难以应对术中不确定性，增加了外科医生的认知负担。

Method: 提出将推理能力融入VLA模型框架，使AI副手机器人能够整合多模态线索、解读手术意图、推理隐藏的组织动力学，从而实现从反应式执行到认知协作的转变。

Result: 推理驱动的自主性可提升手术精准度、安全性及临床实践的可持续性，减轻术中不确定性和外科医生的认知负担。

Conclusion: 推理能力有望将AI副手机器人从被动执行者转变为认知协作者，为内镜手术带来变革性的临床价值。

Abstract: Reasoning capability has significantly advanced complex logical inference and robotic decision-making in general domains. However, its potential in the Artificial Intelligence (AI) copilot robot-particularly implemented based on the Vision-Language-Action (VLA) model-remains unexplored in endoscopic surgery. Effective reasoning should enable AI copilot robots to integrate multimodal cues, interpret surgical intent, and infer hidden tissue dynamics, thereby alleviating intraoperative uncertainty and cognitive burden on surgeons. Properly implemented, reasoning-driven autonomy can transform AI copilot robots from reactive executors into cognitive collaborators, enhancing precision, safety, and sustainability in clinical practice.

</details>


### [50] [Real-Time Auto-Optimization in Unknown Environments via Structure-Exploiting Dual Control for Exploration and Exploitation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22431)
*Shiying Dong,Haoyang Yang,Qiwei Liu,Wen-Hua Chen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文针对未知环境下的自动优化问题，提出了一种快速数值双控制勘探与利用（DCEE）方法。通过揭示DCEE奖励函数中固有的凸-非线性结构，开发了仅线性化非线性残差映射而保留凸外损失的结构化数值方法，将每个子问题转化为可可靠求解的结构化凸形式。该方法在车辆巡航自动优化问题上验证，实现了约一个数量级的加速，最大计算时间仅83微秒。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有DCEE方法主要通过标准优化包或显式梯度型更新律实现，未充分挖掘DCEE的数值结构，导致计算负担成为主动学习中的主要瓶颈。

Method: 提出一种结构利用型数值方法：发现DCEE奖励函数具有凸-非线性结构（凸外损失+非线性残差映射），通过仅线性化非线性残差映射而保留凸外损失，将子问题转化为结构化凸形式。得到的广义Gauss-Newton Hessian近似为半正定且仅依赖一阶导数，支持快速在线计算。

Result: 在车辆巡航自动优化问题的仿真和硬件在环实验中，所提方法相比现有方法提升控制性能，实现约一个数量级的加速，在典型车辆嵌入式CPU上最大计算时间仅83微秒（微秒级）。

Conclusion: 所提结构利用型数值DCEE方法有效解决了计算负担问题，实现了微秒级的在线计算速度，适用于计算资源受限的嵌入式平台。

Abstract: This paper develops a fast numerical dual control for exploration and exploitation (DCEE) method to address auto-optimization problems in unknown environments. In auto-optimization problems, the optimal operating condition is unknown a priori and may vary with the environment. As in classical dual control techniques, computational burden remains a major concern in DCEE for active learning. Existing DCEE methods provide a principled exploration-exploitation objective, but mainly realized through standard optimization packages or explicit gradient-type update laws, where the numerical structure of the DCEE has not been fully exploited. This paper shows that the reward function in DCEE has an inherent convex-over-nonlinear structure, where the exploitation and exploration terms form a unified nonlinear residual map equipped with a convex outer loss. Benefiting from this structure, a structure-exploiting numerical method is developed by linearizing only the nonlinear residual map while preserving the convex outer loss. Thus, each subproblem is transformed into a structured convex form that can be solved reliably. The resulting generalized Gauss-Newton Hessian approximation is positive semidefinite and depends only on first-order derivatives, thereby supporting fast online computation. The proposed method is evaluated on a vehicle cruising auto-optimization problem and compared with existing methods. Simulation and hardware-in-the-loop experimental results show that the proposed method improves control performance and achieves a speedup of approximately one order of magnitude, with a microsecond-level maximum computation time of only 83 μs on a typical vehicle embedded CPU.

</details>


### [51] [Terminal Constraint Model Predictive Control for Image-Based Visual Servoing of UAVs with Kalman Filter-Based Moment Loss Compensation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22443)
*X. Wang,Y. Cao,W. L. W. Leong,Y. R. Tan,S. Huang,S. H. R. Teo,C. Xiang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 针对IBVS中近目标失稳和视觉特征丢失问题，提出结合终端约束MPC与卡尔曼滤波状态预测的框架，实现无人机稳定视觉伺服控制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统IBVS控制器在无人机视觉伺服中存在两大关键问题：一是输入和状态约束导致近目标时闭环稳定性丢失；二是剧烈运动下基于矩的视觉特征间歇性丢失导致控制失败。

Method: 提出TC-MPC框架，在IBVS误差动力学中显式引入终端状态约束和终端代价，保证递归可行性和闭环稳定性；同时集成KF状态预测机制，在短时视觉退化时预测图像矩的时间演化，维持控制连续性。

Result: 通过实时无人机视觉伺服实验验证了所提方法的有效性。

Conclusion: TC-MPC结合KF预测能够有效解决IBVS在约束和视觉退化条件下的稳定性与连续性问题。

Abstract: Image-Based Visual Servoing (IBVS) provides an efficient vision-guided control paradigm for unmanned aerial vehicles (UAVs) by directly regulating image-space errors. However, conventional IBVS controllers are vulnerable to two critical issues: loss of closed-loop stability near the target due to input and state constraints, and control failure caused by intermittent loss of moment-based visual features under aggressive motion. To address these challenges, this paper proposes a terminal-constraint model predictive control (TC-MPC) framework for IBVS, integrated with a Kalman filter (KF)-based state-prediction mechanism. The TC-MPC explicitly incorporates terminal-state constraints and a terminal cost into the IBVS error dynamics, ensuring recursive feasibility, improved convergence behavior, and closed-loop stability under control and state constraints. In parallel, the Kalman filter predicts the temporal evolution of image moments during short-term visual degradation, enabling the controller to preserve control continuity when moment measurements are partially unavailable. The proposed approach is validated through real-time UAV visual servoing experiments.

</details>


### [52] [Steins;Gate Drive: Semantic Safety Arbitration over Structured Futures for Latency-Decoupled LLM Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22456)
*Anjie Qiu,Hans D. Schotten*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: SteinsGateDrive提出了一种延迟解耦的规划-运行时架构，通过世界线隐喻让LLM在控制时刻之前选择反事实驾驶未来，运行时仅在安全契约有效时复用该预测，从而将LLM推理延迟与车辆控制窗口解耦。在高速场景下，该方法将有效延迟从+3.07秒（1秒视界）降低至-0.01秒（4秒视界），且未牺牲碰撞安全边界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 云托管的LLM驱动智能体提供有用的语义判断，但其推理延迟超过步进式车辆控制窗口。现有的学习型世界模型能够预测未来，但通常将未来生成与动作选择置于大型耦合循环中，导致延迟累积。

Method: 提出SteinsGateDrive架构：LLM在最终控制时刻前选择反事实驾驶未来（世界线），运行时在安全契约有效时复用预测。生成器构建三类世界线角色：α（标称自我条件未来）、β（邻近车辆交互反事实）、γ（危险应力未来如刹车、切入、堵塞通道）。选定分支成为类型化的StrategicForecast，包含视界、有效/终止条件、回退方案和权威性。预测的复用在原子谓词运行时检查下执行。

Result: 在受试者内、匹配种子的正常高速公路协议下（10个种子，20步），GPT-5.4 mini将有效延迟从1秒视界时的+3.07秒降低至4秒视界时的-0.01秒，同时保持无碰撞安全边界。架构的安全贡献来自原子谓词运行时检查，而非漂移分数（后者作为刷新频率旋钮）。

Conclusion: SteinsGateDrive通过延迟解耦的规划-运行时架构，有效解决了LLM推理延迟与车辆实时控制之间的冲突，在不牺牲安全性的前提下实现了近似零延迟的有效控制。

Abstract: Cloud-hosted LLM driver agents provide useful semantic judgments, but their inference latency exceeds stepwise vehicle-control windows. Learned world models predict futures, but they usually keep future generation and action selection inside large coupled loops. We present SteinsGateDrive, a latency-decoupled planner-runtime architecture in which the worldline metaphor from the eponymous story names one plausible consequence of an intervention: the LLM selects counterfactual driving futures before the final control instant, and a runtime reuses the selected forecast only while safety contracts remain valid. The generator builds three world-line roles: alpha nominal ego-conditioned futures, beta interaction counterfactuals around nearby vehicles, and gamma hazard-stress futures such as braking, cut-ins, or blocked corridors. The selected branch becomes a typed StrategicForecast with horizon, validity/abort conditions, fallback, and authority. On a within-subject, matched-seed normal-highway protocol with 10 seeds and 20 steps, GPT-5.4 mini reduces effective lag from +3.07 s at 1-second horizon to -0.01 s at 4-second horizon while preserving the measured no-collision safety boundary. The architecture's safety contribution comes from the atom-predicate runtime check, not from the drift score, which functions as a refresh-frequency knob.

</details>


### [53] [SE3Kit: A Lightweight Python Library for Specialized Geometric Primitives in Robotics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22633)
*Daniyal Maroufi,Omid Rezayof,Farshid Alambeigi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: SE3Kit是一个轻量级、纯Python实现的李群运算库，专注于SE(3)和SO(3)的数学严格操作，填补了现有工具在轻量级与数学严谨性之间的空白，适用于嵌入式部署、快速原型设计和教育。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Python机器人学领域缺乏既轻量又数学严格的刚体变换库：现有库要么依赖繁重（如SpatialMath、PyPose），要么缺乏机器人学特定功能（如SciPy）。

Method: 采用纯Python、仅依赖NumPy的方式实现SE(3)和SO(3)的李群运算，避免深度学习或其他可视化软件的额外开销。

Result: 提供了一个轻量级、数学严格的机器人学李群运算库。

Conclusion: SE3Kit填补了机器人学中轻量级与数学严谨性之间的工具空白，适用于嵌入式部署、快速原型设计和教育场景。

Abstract: The Python robotics ecosystem faces a challenge: while many libraries exist for rigid body transformations, few are both lightweight and mathematically strict. This paper introduces SE3Kit, a lightweight Python library efficient operations on the Special Euclidean Group SE(3) and the Special Orthogonal Group SO(3). Unlike established frameworks that require heavy dependencies (e.g., SpatialMath, PyPose) or general tools that lack robotics-specific features (e.g., SciPy), SE3Kit targets the gap between these extremes. It is designed for embedded deployment, rapid prototyping, and education while providing rigorous mathematical implementation. It provides a pure-Python, NumPy-only implementation of Lie Group operations, without the overhead of deep learning or other visualization software.

</details>


### [54] [Symmetries Here and There, Combined Everywhere: Cross-space Symmetry Compositions in Robotics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22639)
*Loizos Hadjiloizou,Rodrigo Pérez-Dattari,Noémie Jaquier*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出跨空间对称组合框架，通过正向运动学的微分几何结构在构型空间与任务空间之间传递对称性，实现机器人策略的多重对称联合等变性，并在双机械臂上验证了其泛化性能提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人系统中存在多种对称性（如机械结构对称性和任务对称性），但现有方法通常孤立地处理每种对称性，未能充分利用它们的组合潜力。

Method: 提出跨空间对称组合框架，利用正向运动学的微分几何结构，将对称性从构型空间降维传递到任务空间，同时从任务空间提升到构型空间，在统一的表示空间中进行对称性组合，学习联合等变的机器人策略。

Result: 在仿真和真实世界的双机械臂实验中，联合利用多种对称性相比单独处理各对称性，显著提升了策略的泛化性能。

Conclusion: 跨空间对称组合框架能够有效整合机器人系统中的多重对称性，提升学习策略的泛化能力，为解决具有多重对称性的机器人任务提供了新思路。

Abstract: Robots exhibit a rich variety of symmetries arising from their mechanical structure and the properties of their tasks. Although many robotics problems exhibit several symmetries simultaneously, existing approaches typically treat them in isolation, failing to exploit their combined potential. This paper introduces cross-space symmetry compositions, a framework for learning robot policies that are jointly equivariant to multiple symmetries across configuration and task spaces. Leveraging the differential-geometric structure of the forward kinematics map, we both descend symmetries from configuration to task space and lift symmetries from task to configuration space, enabling their composition within a unified representation space. We validate our framework on simulated and real-world experiments on a dual-arm robot, demonstrating that jointly leveraging multiple symmetries yields improved generalization.

</details>


### [55] [GesVLA: Gesture-Aware Vision-Language-Action Model Embedded Representations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22812)
*Wenxuan Guo,Ziyuan Li,Meng Zhang,Yichen Liu,Yimeng Dong,Chuxi Xu,Yunfei Wei,Ze Chen,Erjin Zhou,Jianjiang Feng*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出GesVLA模型，将手势作为并行指令模态融入VLA框架，通过双VLM架构和可扩展数据生成管道，在复杂场景中显著提升目标定位精度和人机交互效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLA系统主要依赖文本指令，在包含多个相似物体的复杂场景中难以解决空间歧义问题，限制了机器人在杂乱环境中的操作能力。

Method: 1) 将手势特征编码到潜在空间，参与高层推理和低层动作生成；2) 采用双VLM架构实现手势表征与动作策略的紧耦合；3) 构建可扩展的手势数据生成管道，将手部模型渲染到真实场景图像上；4) 采用两阶段训练策略，分别赋予模型手势感知和动作预测能力。

Result: 在多种真实机器人任务（包括受控积木操作、产品和农产品选取等）上，手势指令持续提升了目标定位精度和人机交互效率，尤其在复杂杂乱环境中效果显著。

Conclusion: 手势作为并行指令模态能有效解决VLA系统中的空间歧义问题，提升机器人在复杂场景中的操作性能。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models have shown strong potential for general-purpose robot manipulation by unifying perception and action. However, existing VLA systems primarily rely on textual instructions and struggle to resolve spatial ambiguity in complex scenes with multiple similar objects. To address this limitation, we introduce gesture as a parallel instruction modality and propose a Gesture-aware Vision-Language-Action model (GesVLA). Our approach encodes gesture features directly into the latent space, enabling them to participate in both high-level reasoning and low-level action generation, and adopts a dual-VLM architecture to achieve tight coupling between gesture representations and action policies. At the data level, we construct a scalable gesture data generation pipeline by rendering hand models onto real-world scene images. This reduces the sim-to-real visual gap while producing rich data with diverse motion patterns and corresponding pointing annotations. In addition, we employ a two-stage training strategy to equip the model with both gesture perception and action prediction capabilities. We evaluate our approach on multiple real-world robotic tasks, including a controlled block manipulation task for validation and more practical scenarios such as product and produce selection. Experimental results show that incorporating gesture consistently improves target grounding accuracy and human-robot interaction efficiency, especially in complex and cluttered environments. Project page: https://gwxuan.github.io/GesVLA/.

</details>


### [56] [AwareVLN: Reasoning with Self-awareness for Vision-Language Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22816)
*Wenxuan Guo,Xiuwei Xu,Yichen Liu,Xiangyu Li,Hang Yin,Huangxing Chen,Wenzhao Zheng,Jianjiang Feng,Jie Zhou,Jiwen Lu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: AwareVLN通过引入结构化的自感知推理机制和自动化数据引擎，在完全端到端的方式下提升视觉-语言导航任务中智能体对自身状态和任务进展的理解能力，在Habitat模拟器的多个数据集上显著优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLN方法要么依赖端到端VLM但缺乏对智能体、指令和场景间关系的显式可解释理解，要么显式构建场景地图进行启发式规划但依赖额外3D传感器且阻碍大规模视觉-语言预训练，因此需要一种兼具两者优势的框架。

Method: 提出AwareVLN框架，包含两个关键创新：(1) 结构化的推理模块，用于培养空间和任务导向的自我感知；(2) 带有进度划分的自动化数据引擎，用于高效训练。整个框架是端到端且数据驱动的。

Result: 在Habitat模拟器的多个数据集上进行的广泛实验表明，AwareVLN显著优于之前的SOTA视觉-语言导航方法。

Conclusion: 通过引入自感知推理机制，可以在不依赖额外3D传感器和启发式规划的情况下，实现端到端可解释的视觉-语言导航，为VLN任务提供了新的研究范式。

Abstract: Vision-and-Language Navigation (VLN) requires an agent to ground language instructions to its own movement within a visual environment. While state-of-the-art methods leverage the reasoning capabilities of Vision-Language Models (VLMs) for end-to-end action prediction, they often lack an explicit and explainable understanding of the relationships between the agent, the instruction, and the scene. Conversely, explicitly building a scene map for heuristic planning is intuitively appealing but relies on additional 3D sensors and hinders large-scale vision-language pre-training. To bridge this gap, we propose AwareVLN, a novel framework that equips the navigation model with a self-aware reasoning mechanism, enabling it to understand the agent's state and task progress in a fully end-to-end and data-driven manner. Our approach features two key innovations: (1) a structural reasoning module that fosters spatial and task-oriented self-awareness, and (2) an automatic data engine with progress division for effective training. Extensive experiments on various datasets in Habitat simulator show our AwareVLN significantly outperforms previous state-of-the-art vision-language navigation methods. Project page: https://gwxuan.github.io/AwareVLN/.

</details>

### [57] [Temporal Contrastive Transformer for Financial Crime Detection: Self-Supervised Sequence Embeddings via Predictive Contrastive Coding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21490)
*Danny Butvinik,Yonit Marcus,Nitzan Tal,Gabrielle Azoulay*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了时间对比变换器（TCT），一种用于金融交易序列的自监督对比表示学习框架。实验表明，TCT嵌入单独使用能达到有意义的预测性能（AUC 0.8644），但与强领域工程特征结合时未带来显著提升（AUC 0.9205 vs 0.9245），说明学习到的表征与现有特征抽象存在重叠。研究处于中间阶段，虽未达到生产就绪，但证明了减少对特征工程依赖的可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有金融交易欺诈检测系统高度依赖手工特征工程，需要大量领域知识。作者旨在开发一种自监督表示学习方法，能够自动从原始交易序列中学习行为模式的时间动态特征，从而减少对人工特征工程的依赖。

Method: 提出时间对比变换器（TCT），采用自监督对比学习目标训练，对金融交易序列进行编码。将学到的嵌入作为梯度提升分类器的输入特征，在真实场景下评估欺诈检测性能。

Result: TCT嵌入单独达到AUC 0.8644，表明模型捕捉到了有意义的时序结构。但与领域工程特征结合时，AUC 0.9205 vs 基线0.9245，未观察到可测量的提升，表明学习表征与现有特征抽象存在较大重叠。

Conclusion: TCT作为表示学习方法能捕获相关行为信号，但面临在强领域特征基础上实现附加价值的挑战。达到与强特征工程基线相当的性能本身是有意义的成果，表明学习表征可在无需手动工程的情况下近似领域特定特征，指向了减少金融犯罪检测中对特征工程依赖的有前景方向。

Abstract: We introduce the Temporal Contrastive Transformer (TCT), a representation learning framework designed to capture contextual temporal dynamics in sequences of financial transactions. The model is trained using a self-supervised contrastive objective to produce embeddings that encode behavioral patterns over time, with the goal of supporting downstream fraud detection tasks. We evaluate TCT in a realistic setting by using the learned embeddings as input features to a gradient boosting classifier. Experimental results show that embeddings alone achieve meaningful predictive performance (AUC 0.8644), indicating that the model captures non-trivial temporal structure. However, when combined with domain-engineered features, no measurable improvement is observed over the baseline (AUC 0.9205 vs. 0.9245), suggesting that the learned representations largely overlap with existing feature abstractions. These findings position TCT as a promising representation learning approach that captures relevant behavioral signal, while highlighting the challenges of achieving additive value over strong domain features. The results reflect an intermediate stage in the development of temporal representation learning for financial crime detection and motivate further research on model architecture, training objectives, and integration strategies. At this early stage, achieving performance comparable to a strong feature-engineered baseline is itself a meaningful outcome, indicating that learned representations approximate domain-specific features without manual engineering. While not yet production-ready, these results point to a promising direction for reducing reliance on feature engineering in financial crime detection.

</details>


### [58] [Cross-Species RSA Reveals Conserved Early Visual Alignment but Divergent Higher-Area Rankings Across Human fMRI and Macaque Electrophysiology](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22401)
*Nils Leutenegger*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本研究通过五种学习规则（BP、FA、PC、STDP、随机基线）在猕猴电生理数据上验证了大脑对齐的跨物种泛化性。结果显示：早期视觉皮层（V1/V2）的对齐度在猕猴中高于人类fMRI，STDP和PC表现最佳；但IT层的学习规则排名在物种间无相关性，而预训练ResNet-50显著优于所有自定义CNN，表明IT层对齐受模型容量和训练数据限制而非学习规则。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究学习规则与大脑对齐的关系是否能在不同物种间泛化，即之前在人脑V1区域的发现是否适用于猕猴。

Method: 使用五种学习规则（BP、FA、PC、STDP、随机权重基线）训练CNN，通过表征相似性分析（RSA）比较模型与猕猴电生理数据（V1/V2/V4/IT）的神经对齐度，并与之前的人脑fMRI结果对比。

Result: 1) 所有模型在猕猴早期视觉皮层（V1/V2）的对齐度（rho=0.15-0.30）高于人脑fMRI（rho=0.01-0.08）；2) STDP和PC在猕猴V1/V2上对齐度最高（rho~0.30和0.28）；3) IT层学习规则排名在物种间无相关性（Kendall's tau=0.00, p=1.00）；4) 预训练ResNet-50在猕猴IT层达rho=0.25，远超自定义CNN（0.07-0.14）。

Conclusion: 早期视觉对齐在物种间具有稳健性，而高级视觉区域的对齐受模型容量和刺激域影响，不直接由学习规则决定。

Abstract: Does the relationship between learning rules and brain alignment generalize across species? We extend our prior finding that untrained CNNs match backpropagation at human V1 by testing the same five learning rules against macaque electrophysiology. The rules are backpropagation (BP), feedback alignment (FA), predictive coding (PC), spike-timing-dependent plasticity (STDP), and an untrained random-weights baseline. The macaque data come from two datasets: MajajHong2015 (V4/IT, 3,200 stimulus presentations, 88/168 neurons) and FreemanZiemba2013 (V1/V2, 135 stimuli, 102/103 neurons). Using RSA with identical model weights from our human study, we find: (1) all models achieve higher alignment with macaque early visual cortex (rho = 0.15-0.30 at V1/V2) than with human fMRI (rho = 0.01-0.08), consistent with the higher signal-to-noise ratio of electrophysiology; (2) STDP and PC produce the highest macaque V1/V2 alignment (rho ~ 0.30 and 0.28), consistent with their leading position among trained rules in human V1; (3) at IT, learning rule rankings show no detectable correlation across species (Kendall's tau = 0.00, p = 1.00), though this null result is expected given that n = 5 provides power only at tau = +/-1.0, and is further confounded by stimulus set differences; (4) a pretrained ResNet-50 (ImageNet) achieves rho = 0.25 at macaque IT, substantially above all custom CNN conditions (rho = 0.07-0.14), suggesting IT alignment is limited by model capacity and training data rather than by the learning rule. Noise ceilings, multi-seed variability (5 seeds), and a stimulus-control analysis are reported. These results demonstrate that early visual alignment is robust across species, while higher-area alignment is modulated by model capacity and stimulus domain.

</details>


### [59] [Teaching Language Models to Forecast Research Success Through Comparative Idea Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21491)
*Srujan P Mule,Aniketh Garikaparthi,Manasi Patwardhan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究语言模型能否在无需实验的条件下预测研究想法的实证成功。通过构建11,488个研究想法对的数据集，发现小模型经SFT后准确率达77.1%，超越GPT-5；基于强化学习的推理方法达71.35%且可解释。结果表明计算高效的小模型可作为客观验证器，助力自主科学发现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着语言模型自动生成研究假设，评估和筛选大量AI产生的想法成为新瓶颈。作者希望探索LM能否在运行实验之前预测研究想法的实证成功，从而加速科学发现流程。

Method: 构建基于PapersWithCode的11,488个想法的比较实证预测数据集；采用监督微调（SFT）和基于可验证奖励的强化学习（RLVR）两种方法训练模型进行想法比较预测。

Result: 8B参数小模型经SFT后达到77.1%准确率，超越GPT-5的61.1%；RLVR方法达71.35%且提供可解释推理；模型在分布外测试集上表现鲁棒。

Conclusion: 计算高效的小语言模型可以作为有效且客观的验证器，为自主科学发现提供可扩展路径。

Abstract: As language models accelerate scientific research by automating hypothesis generation and implementation, a new bottleneck emerges: evaluating and filtering hundreds of AI-generated ideas without exhaustive experimentation. We ask whether LMs can learn to forecast the empirical success of research ideas before any experiments are run. We study comparative empirical forecasting: given a benchmark-specific research goal and two candidate ideas, predict which will achieve better benchmark performance. We construct a dataset of 11,488 idea pairs grounded in objective outcomes from PapersWithCode. While off-the-shelf 8B-parameter models struggle (30% acc.), SFT dramatically boosts performance to 77.1%, outperforming GPT-5 (61.1%). By framing evaluation as a reasoning task via Reinforcement Learning with Verifiable Rewards (RLVR), we train models to discover latent reasoning paths, achieving 71.35% acc. with interpretable justifications. Through additional ablations and out-of-distribution tests, we show robustness to surface-level heuristics and transfer to both a cross-domain time-split test set and an independently constructed test set. Our results demonstrate that compute-efficient small language models can serve as effective, objective verifiers, offering a scalable path for autonomous scientific discovery.

</details>


### [60] [Harnesses for Inference-Time Alignment over Execution Trajectories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21516)
*Boyuan Wang,Bochao Li,Minghan Wang,Yuxin Tao,Fang Kong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文从推理时轨迹对齐的角度研究LLM Agent的"缰绳工程"（harness engineering），将缰绳分解为任务分解（task decomposition）和引导执行（guided execution）两种机制，量化了工作流粒度、重试预算和引导动作重加权对性能的影响，揭示了过度分解、过度剪枝和幻觉执行等失败模式，并发现部分缰绳（仅指定初始步骤）比完全结构化工作流更有效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的harness设计存在不一致性：更精细的分解和引导有时会提升性能，有时反而降低任务成功率。作者希望通过理论分析厘清harness设计中的权衡关系，找出性能边界和失败模式。

Method: 将harness设计分解为两个独立机制——任务分解（将任务结构化为子目标）和引导执行（在执行过程中重塑局部动作分布），通过推理时轨迹对齐的理论框架量化工作流粒度、重试预算和引导动作重加权的影响，并通过合成实验和真实终端Agent基准验证。

Result: 识别出三个关键失败模式：过度分解、过度剪枝和幻觉执行；发现部分缰绳（仅指导初始步骤）在某些场景下比完全结构化的工作流具有更高的通过率。

Conclusion: 有效的harness设计不必追求完全结构化，适度的引导（如仅指定初始步骤）可以达到更好的效果，为实际Agent系统设计提供了理论指导。

Abstract: Harness engineering has emerged as an important inference-time technique for large language model (LLM) agents, aiming to improve long-term performance through task decomposition and guided execution. However, more elaborate harnesses are not uniformly better: increasing decomposition or guidance can sometimes improve execution, but can also reduce final task success. We study harness design through the lens of inference-time trajectory alignment. This perspective separates harness into two mechanisms: task decomposition, which structures a task into sub-goals, and guided execution, which reshapes local action distributions during execution. This decomposition allows us to quantify how workflow granularity, retry budgets, and guidance-induced action reweighting shape the performance limits of harness design. It further reveals concrete failure modes, including over-decomposition, over-pruning, and hallucinated execution. We validate these predictions through controlled synthetic experiments and real terminal agent benchmarks. Inspired by the theory, we further show that effective harnesses can be partial: specifying only the initial steps and leaving the remaining execution to agent can achieve higher pass rate than fully structured workflows.

</details>


### [61] [Memory-R2: Fair Credit Assignment for Long-Horizon Memory-Augmented LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21768)
*Sikuan Yan,Ahmed Bahloul,Ercong Nie,Susanna Schwarzmann,Riccardo Trivisonno,Volker Tresp,Yunpu Ma*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Memory-R2是一个针对长程记忆增强型LLM智能体的训练框架，其核心算法LoGo-GRPO通过联合局部与全局组相对优化、共享参数协同设计以及渐进式课程学习，解决了多会话场景下因记忆状态不同导致轨迹级比较不公平的信用分配难题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在强化学习训练记忆增强型LLM智能体时，不同轨迹会写入、更新或删除不同的记忆，导致中间记忆状态不一致，使得轨迹级比较失去公平性，违反GRPO等组相对方法的假设，从而产生有偏的信用信号。

Method: 提出Memory-R2框架，核心算法LoGo-GRPO包含：(1) 全局目标保留长程轨迹级奖励的端到端学习；(2) 局部重展开在同一中间记忆状态下比较不同记忆操作结果，实现更公平的组比较；(3) 共享参数协同设计，通过角色提示从同一LLM主干实例化事实提取器和记忆管理器；(4) 渐进式课程学习，训练长度从8到16到32会话逐步增加。

Result: Memory-R2在长程多会话记忆增强型LLM智能体训练中有效解决了信用分配问题，提供了精准的监督信号，并实现了稳定的多步RL训练。

Conclusion: Memory-R2为记忆增强型LLM智能体在长程多会话场景下提供了一种有效的训练范式，通过LoGo-GRPO、共享参数设计和渐进式课程学习协同解决了记忆状态不一致带来的训练挑战。

Abstract: Memory-augmented LLM agents enable interactions that extend beyond finite context windows by storing, updating, and reusing information across sessions. However, training such agents with reinforcement learning in multi-session environments is challenging because memory turns the agent's past actions into part of its future environment. Once different rollouts write, update, or delete different memories, they no longer share the same intermediate memory state, making trajectory-level comparisons fundamentally unfair. This violates a key assumption behind group-relative methods such as GRPO, where rollouts are compared as if they were sampled from the same effective environment. Consequently, trajectory-level rewards provide noisy or biased credit signals for long-horizon memory operations. To address this challenge, we introduce Memory-R2, a training framework for long-horizon memory-augmented LLM agents. Its core algorithm, LoGo-GRPO, combines local and global group-relative optimization. The global objective preserves end-to-end learning from long-horizon trajectory-level rewards, while local rerollouts compare different memory-operation outcomes from the same intermediate memory state, yielding fairer group comparisons and more precise supervision for memory construction. Beyond credit assignment, Memory-R2 jointly optimizes memory formation and memory evolution with a shared-parameter co-learning design, where a fact extractor and a memory manager are instantiated from the same LLM backbone through role-specific prompts. To stabilize multi-step RL over long memory horizons, we adopt a progressive curriculum that increases the training horizon from 8 to 16 to 32 sessions. Together, these components provide an effective training paradigm for memory-augmented LLM agents in long-horizon multi-session settings.

</details>


### [62] [A Reproducible Log-Driven AutoML Framework for Interpretable Pipeline Optimization in Healthcare Risk Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21528)
*Rui Huang,Lican Huang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了yvsoucom-iterkit，一个确定性、日志驱动的自动化机器学习框架，将pipeline优化编码为可追溯的日志实体。在Pima Indians Diabetes和Stroke数据集上对超过18,000种pipeline配置进行实验，揭示了结构化且部分冗余的搜索空间，性能由少数关键组件决定。增强(0.454)、模型选择(0.198)和不平衡处理(0.101)是Pima数据集的主要驱动因素，而不平衡处理(0.406)主导Stroke数据集。组件相似性分析显示存在强冗余。集成模型在Pima上达到Weighted-F1 0.89、Macro-F1 0.88，在Stroke上达到Weighted-F1 0.94，但Macro-F1因类别不平衡仅0.67。跨种子分析揭示了性能-鲁棒性权衡，集成模型变异性(0.023-0.026)低于SVM。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 由于异质性特征、有限的样本量和严重的类别不平衡，准确且可重复的疾病风险预测仍然具有挑战性。现有AutoML方法在可重复性和组件间交互分析方面存在不足。

Method: 提出yvsoucom-iterkit框架，采用确定性、日志驱动的方式将每个pipeline编码为可追溯的日志实体，支持组件归因、交互、相似性和跨种子鲁棒性分析。使用随机森林重要性分析、组件相似性距离度量等方法进行系统评估。

Result: 在Pima和Stroke数据集上测试超过18,000种pipeline配置。识别出关键性能驱动因素：Pima上增强(0.454)、模型选择(0.198)、不平衡处理(0.101)；Stroke上不平衡处理(0.406)。发现组件间强冗余（如biMax-biMean RMS距离0.0252，mixup与无增强距离0.0279）。集成模型表现稳定，跨种子变异性低(0.023-0.026)。

Conclusion: 有效的AutoML优化可以聚焦于少数高影响力组件，搜索空间存在结构化冗余，组件间存在性能-鲁棒性权衡。

Abstract: Accurate and reproducible disease risk prediction remains challenging due to heterogeneous features, limited samples, and severe class imbalance. This study introduces yvsoucom-iterkit, a deterministic and log-driven automated machine learning framework that formulates pipeline optimization as a fully reproducible, configuration-level system. Each pipeline is encoded as a traceable log entity, enabling analysis of component attribution, interactions, similarity, and cross-seed robustness. Experiments on the Pima Indians Diabetes and Stroke datasets across more than 18,000 pipeline configurations reveal a structured and partially redundant search space, where performance is governed by a small subset of interacting components. Random Forest importance analysis identifies augmentation (0.454), model choice (0.198), and imbalance handling (0.101) as key drivers on Pima, while imbalance handling dominates Stroke (0.406). Component similarity analysis shows strong redundancy, with feature selection variants (biMax-biMean) exhibiting low RMS distance (0.0252), mixup closely matching no augmentation (0.0279), and TomekLinks aligning with no imbalance handling (0.0325), whereas Gaussian noise shows greater divergence from no augmentation (0.10). The framework achieves strong and stable performance using ensemble models (Weighted-F1 0.89, Macro-F1 0.88 on Pima; Weighted-F1 0.94 on Stroke), while Macro-F1 remains lower on Stroke (0.67) due to class imbalance. Cross-seed analysis reveals a performance-robustness trade-off, with ensembles showing lower variability (0.023-0.026) than SVM. These results indicate that effective AutoML optimization can focus on a reduced set of high-impact components.

</details>


### [63] [DualOptim+: Bridging Shared and Decoupled Optimizer States for Better Machine Unlearning in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21539)
*Xuyang Zhong,Qizhang Li,Yiwen Guo,Chen Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出DualOptim+框架，通过基态和增量态架构自适应桥接遗忘与保留目标的共享和分离表示，并推出8bit量化版本，在多个任务上实现更优的目标权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型机器遗忘中，遗忘目标和保留目标之间存在梯度方向冲突，现有方法难以有效平衡二者，需要一种能够自适应处理共享与解耦表示的优化框架。

Method: 引入基态（base state）捕获遗忘和保留目标的共享表示，增量态（delta states）保留目标特定的残差；根据遗忘梯度和保留梯度之间的方向冲突，自适应桥接共享态和解耦态；并推出8bit量化变体降低内存开销。

Result: 在虚构/真实世界遗忘、安全对齐、多任务学习等任务上，DualOptim+始终在不同目标之间取得了更优的权衡。

Conclusion: DualOptim+是一种有效的自适应优化框架，能显著提升大语言模型机器遗忘的性能，其量化版本在降低内存的同时保持性能不损。

Abstract: We propose DualOptim+, a novel optimization framework for improving machine unlearning in large language models. It introduces a base state to capture common representations shared by forgetting and retaining objectives and delta states to preserve objective-specific residuals. This architecture allows the optimizer to adaptively bridge shared and decoupled states based on the directional conflict between forgetting and retaining gradients. We further introduce DualOptim+ 8bit, a quantized variant that reduces memory overhead without compromising performance. Extensive experiments across fictitious and real-world unlearning, safety alignment, and multi-task learning tasks demonstrate that DualOptim+ consistently achieves a superior trade-off between different objectives. Codes are available at https://github.com/CityU-MLO/DualOptimPlus.

</details>


### [64] [Provable Joint Decontamination for Benchmarking Multiple Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21543)
*Zhenlong Liu,Hao Zeng,Hongxin Wei*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出联合包络共形选择（JECS）方法，通过共形p值聚合与包络重缩放，实现对多模型基准数据污染率的全局控制，在保证理论保证的同时提升检测效能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准数据污染检测方法缺乏理论保证（基于分数的方法）或仅适用于单模型（共形方法），无法在多模型场景下实现全局控制和公平比较。

Method: 计算每个模型的共形p值，按样本取最大值聚合；从右尾观测重构最大p值零分布的保守包络；对包络重缩放后的值应用自适应Benjamini-Hochberg过程进行选择，实现全局污染率控制。

Result: 在多种模型和基准上的实验表明，JECS在持续维持目标全局污染率控制的同时，比最大p值基线方法具有更高的统计功效。

Conclusion: JECS提供了首个具有理论保证的多模型基准去污方法，在控制全局污染率的前提下实现了更优的检测性能。

Abstract: Benchmark data contamination has become a central challenge in LLM evaluation: when evaluation examples appear in the training data of one or more audited models, reported performance can be inflated and cross-model comparisons become unreliable. A broad line of training-data detection work designs scores to quantify how strongly a model memorizes a given data point, but these score-based methods lack theoretical guarantees. Recent conformal approaches provide provable false-identification control for a single model; however, applying them separately to each model can produce model-specific benchmarks, undermining fair comparison across models. In this work, we formalize multi-model benchmark decontamination as a joint selection problem and propose Joint Envelope Conformal Selection (JECS), a conformal procedure that enables global contamination rate (GCR) control under stated assumptions. Specifically, JECS computes per-model conformal p-values, aggregates them by the per-item maximum, and reconstructs a conservative envelope of the max-p null distribution from right-tail observations above a data-driven threshold. By applying the adaptive Benjamini-Hochberg (BH) procedure to the envelope-rescaled values, we select a benchmark with provable GCR control. Extensive experiments across various models and benchmarks demonstrate that JECS achieves higher power than the max-p baseline while consistently maintaining the target GCR control.

</details>


### [65] [Tabular foundation models for robust calibration of near-infrared chemical sensing data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21544)
*Robin Reiter,Denis Cornet,Fabien Michel,Lauriane Rouan,Gregory Beurier*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文系统评估了表格基础模型TabPFN在近红外光谱化学传感中的校准性能，在66个NIR数据集上对比了PLS、Ridge、CatBoost、CNN-1D等方法，发现TabPFN在回归和分类任务中均取得最优平均排名，但在光谱异常值和外推样本上优势减弱。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 近红外光谱分析依赖校准模型处理高维共线光谱、小样本、预处理敏感性、异常值和外推等问题，现有方法存在局限。研究探索表格基础模型能否为NIR化学传感提供新的校准策略。

Method: 在66个NIR数据集（54回归+12分类）上，采用统一验证框架，仅在校准数据上进行预处理和模型选择。对比方法包括PLS/PLS-DA、Ridge、CatBoost、一维CNN，评估TabPFN在原始光谱和预处理优化两种模式下的性能。

Result: 回归任务中，预处理优化的TabPFN取得最佳平均排名，显著优于PLS、CatBoost、原始光谱TabPFN和CNN-1D，与Ridge统计相当。分类任务中，直接使用原始光谱的TabPFN取得最佳平均排名。在光谱异常值和外推样本上，TabPFN优势减弱，经典化学计量学模型仍有竞争力。

Conclusion: 表格基础模型可补充现有NIR化学传感的化学计量学工作流，尤其适用于小到中规模校准场景，但需开发光谱专用先验知识和不确定性感知部署策略。

Abstract: Near-infrared spectroscopy is increasingly used as a rapid, non-destructive chemical sensing technology for the analysis of food, pharmaceutical, biological, and environmental samples. However, the practical deployment of NIR sensors still depends on calibration models able to handle high-dimensional, collinear spectra, limited sample sizes, preprocessing dependence, spectral outliers, and extrapolation beyond the calibration domain. Here, we evaluate whether tabular foundation models can provide a new calibration strategy for NIR chemical sensing. We benchmark TabPFN on 66 NIR datasets covering 54 regression and 12 classification tasks, and compare direct inference on raw spectra with preprocessing-optimized inference against PLS/PLS-DA, Ridge, Catboost, and one-dimensional convolutional neural networks. The study uses a unified validation framework in which preprocessing and model selection are performed exclusively on calibration data before external test evaluation. In regression, preprocessing-optimized TabPFN achieves the best overall average rank and significantly outperforms PLS, CatBoost, TabPFN on raw spectra, and CNN-1D, while remaining statistically comparable to Ridge. In classification, TabPFN applied directly to raw spectra provides the best average rank, with performance close to the optimized variant. Robustness analyses show that TabPFN provides strong average predictive performance but that its advantage decreases on spectral outliers and extrapolated samples, where classical chemometric models remain competitive. These results suggest that tabular foundation models can complement established chemometric workflows for NIR chemical sensing, especially in small- to medium-sized calibration settings, while highlighting the need for spectroscopy-specific priors and uncertainty-aware deployment strategies.

</details>


### [66] [CogAdapt: Transferring Clinical ECG Foundation Models to Wearable Cognitive Load Assessment via Lead Adaptation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22774)
*Amir Mousavi,Mohammad Sadegh Sirjani,Erfan Nourbakhsh,Mimi Xie,Rocky Slavin,Leslie Neely,John Davis,John Quarles*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出CogAdapt框架，通过LeadBridge适配器将3导联可穿戴信号转化为12导联表示，结合ProFine渐进微调策略，实现临床ECG基础模型向可穿戴认知负荷评估的迁移，在公开数据集上显著优于从头训练的基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实时认知负荷评估对于自适应人机交互至关重要，但面临标注数据有限和跨主体泛化能力差的挑战。现有临床ECG基础模型虽具备丰富表示能力，但因传感器配置不匹配和任务差异无法直接应用于可穿戴设备。

Method: 提出CogAdapt框架，包含两个核心模块：(1) LeadBridge——可学习适配器，将3导联可穿戴信号转换为解剖学一致的12导联表示；(2) ProFine——渐进微调策略，逐步解冻编码器层同时防止灾难性遗忘。

Result: 在CLARE和CL-Drive两个公开数据集上，采用留一被试交叉验证，CogAdapt的macro-F1分别达到0.626和0.768，显著优于从头训练的基线方法。

Conclusion: 结果表明，基于基础模型适配的方法在可穿戴传感器的跨主体认知负荷评估中具有良好前景，为解决临床模型到可穿戴场景的迁移问题提供了有效方案。

Abstract: Real-time cognitive load assessment is essential for adaptive human-computer interaction but remains challenging due to limited labeled data and poor cross-subject generalization. Recent ECG foundation models pre-trained on millions of clinical recordings offer rich representations, but cannot be directly applied to wearable devices due to sensor configuration mismatch and task differences. In this paper, we propose CogAdapt, a framework that adapts clinical ECG foundation models to wearable cognitive load assessment. CogAdapt introduces LeadBridge, a learnable adapter that transforms 3-lead wearable signals into anatomically consistent 12-lead representations, and ProFine, a progressive fine-tuning strategy that gradually unfreezes encoder layers while preventing catastrophic forgetting. Evaluations on two public datasets (CLARE and CL-Drive) under leave-one-subject-out cross-validation show that CogAdapt substantially outperforms baselines trained from scratch, achieving macro-F1 scores of 0.626 and 0.768. These results demonstrate the promise of foundation model adaptation for subject-independent cognitive load assessment from wearable sensors.

</details>


### [67] [Expectation Consistency Loss: Rethink Confidence Calibration under Covariate Shift](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21552)
*Jinzong Dong,Zhaohui Jiang,Bo Yang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于期望一致性条件（Expectation consistency condition）的协变量偏移下置信度校准方法ECL，该方法无需密度比估计，兼容多种校准范式，并在理论和实验上验证了其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有置信度校准方法大多假设训练和测试数据独立同分布，在协变量偏移下效果受限；而现有的协变量偏移校准方法依赖不稳定的重要性加权，且难以同时处理类别级和标准校准。

Method: 首先推导了协变量偏移下置信度校准的充要条件——期望一致性条件；基于该条件提出无监督域适应损失ECL，兼容标准校准、类别校准和顶层标签校准；证明了ECL的计算复杂度与ECE相同，并设计了基于小批量的可训练方案。

Result: 在模拟和真实协变量偏移数据集上验证了ECL方法的有效性，表明其在各类校准指标上优于现有方法。

Conclusion: 协变量偏移不一定导致置信度失准，期望一致性条件为校准提供了更弱的充分条件；ECL损失避免了密度比估计的不稳定性，具有理论保证和实际可行性。

Abstract: Confidence calibration for classification models is vital in safety-critical decision-making scenarios and has received extensive attention. General confidence calibration methods assume training and test data are independent and identically distributed, limiting their effectiveness under covariate shifts. Previous calibration methods under covariate shift struggle with class-wise or canonical calibrations and often rely on unstable importance weighting when density ratios are large or unbounded. Given the above limitations, this paper rethinks confidence calibration under covariate shifts. First, we derive a necessary and sufficient condition for confidence calibration under covariate shifts, named Expectation consistency condition, which reveals covariate shifts do not necessarily lead to uncalibrated confidence and provides a weaker condition for confidence calibration than global covariate distribution alignment. Then, utilizing Expectation consistency condition, this paper proposes an unsupervised domain adaptation loss to calibrate confidence of the target domain, named Expectation consistency loss (ECL), which is compatible with canonical calibration, class-wise calibration, and top-label calibration. Third, we prove that computing ECL loss has the same sample complexity as Expected Calibration Error (ECE) and provide a theoretically grounded mini-batch trainable scheme for ECL loss. Finally, we validate the effectiveness of our method on both simulated and real-world covariate shift datasets.

</details>


### [68] [MambaGaze: Bidirectional Mamba with Explicit Missing Data Modeling for Cognitive Load Assessment from Eye-Gaze Tracking Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22775)
*Amir Mousavi,Mohammad Sadegh Sirjani,Erfan Nourbakhsh,Mimi Xie,Rocky Slavin,Leslie Neely,John Davis,John Quarles*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出MambaGaze框架，通过XMD编码处理眼动数据缺失，利用双向Mamba-2高效建模长时依赖，在认知负荷评估任务中超越CNN/Transformer等基线4-12个百分点，并在边缘设备上实现实时推理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有眼动信号认知负荷评估面临两个关键挑战：频繁的数据缺失（眨眼、追踪失败）以及高效建模长时时间依赖的需求，亟需一种能同时处理这两个问题的轻量级框架。

Method: 提出MambaGaze框架，包含两个核心组件：1) XMD编码（交叉模态缺失编码），将原始特征与观测掩码和时间增量结合，显式建模数据不确定性；2) 双向Mamba-2模块，以线性计算复杂度捕获时间依赖性。

Result: 在CLARE和CL-Drive数据集上，留一被试交叉验证下分别达到76.8%和73.1%准确率，超越CNN、Transformer、ResNet、VGG基线4-12个百分点；在NVIDIA Jetson边缘平台上实现43-68 FPS实时推理，功耗低于7.5W。

Conclusion: MambaGaze有效解决了眼动信号认知负荷评估中的数据缺失和长时依赖建模问题，并在边缘设备上验证了可穿戴实时监测的可行性。

Abstract: Real-time cognitive load assessment from eye-tracking signals could potentially enable adaptive human-centered-AI such as safety-critical applications such as driver vigilance monitoring or automated flight deck assistance, yet two challenges persist: handling frequent data missingness from blinks and tracking failures, and efficiently modeling long-range temporal dependencies. We propose MambaGaze, a framework that addresses these challenges through 1) XMD encoding, which augments raw features with observation masks and time-deltas to explicitly model data uncertainty, and 2) bidirectional Mamba-2, which captures temporal dependencies with linear computational complexity. Experiments on CLARE and CL-Drive datasets under leave-one-subject-out evaluation show that MambaGaze achieves 76.8% and 73.1% accuracy, respectively, outperforming CNN, Transformer, ResNet, and VGG baselines by 4-12 percentage points. Edge deployment benchmarks on NVIDIA Jetson platforms demonstrate real-time inference at 43-68 FPS with power consumption below 7.5W, confirming feasibility for wearable cognitive load monitoring.

</details>


### [69] [Beyond Single Slot: Joint Optimization for Multi-Slot Guaranteed Display Advertising](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21556)
*Zhaoqi Zhang,Jiaming Deng,Miao Xie,Linyou Cai,Qianlong Xie,Xingxing Wang,Siqiang Luo,Gao Cong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种面向多槽位保证展示广告的联合优化框架，通过合同轮盘机制和页面浏览量约束，将分配问题建模为离线二分图匹配，并在美团广告平台上线后实现用户平均收入提升28.99%，显著提升了商户ROI、平台收入和合同履约稳健性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有保证展示广告方法多基于单槽位假设，无法有效优化多槽位页面浏览场景下的广告分配，存在槽位级冗余、合同不平衡、曝光集中等关键挑战。

Method: 将多槽位GD分配建模为离线二分图匹配问题，引入合同轮盘机制处理槽位独占性，设计页面浏览量约束控制曝光，并开发可扩展的分配优化算法以支持大规模部署。

Result: 在美团广告平台线上A/B测试中，70%流量下用户平均收入（ARPU）提升28.99%；DID分析表明合同稳定性得到改善。

Conclusion: 该联合优化框架在真实广告部署场景中具有强适用性和有效性，同时提升了商户ROI、平台收益和合同履约的稳健性。

Abstract: Guaranteed display advertising is crucial for platform monetization, yet existing methods often operate under a single-slot assumption, limiting their ability to optimize allocation across multi-slot page views. In this paper, we propose a novel joint optimization framework for multi-slot GD allocation, addressing key challenges such as slot-level redundancy, contract imbalance, and exposure concentration. Our approach formulates the allocation as an offline bipartite matching problem with a contract roulette mechanism for slot exclusivity and Page View constraints for impression control, and incorporates a scalable allocation optimization algorithm for efficient large-scale deployment. Extensive online tests on the Meituan advertising platform demonstrate that our method significantly improves merchant ROI, platform revenue efficiency, and contract fulfillment robustness. Specifically, online A/B tests show a 28.99% increase in Average Revenue Per User under 70% traffic, and DID analysis further indicates improved contract stability, demonstrating the strong applicability and effectiveness of our framework in real-world advertising deployments.

</details>


### [70] [Leveraging Self-Paced Curriculum Learning for Enhanced Modality Balance in Multimodal Conversational Emotion Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21565)
*Phuong-Anh Nguyen,The-Son Le,Duc-Trong Le,Cam-Van Thi Nguyen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出基于自定进度课程学习（SPCL）的即插即用框架，通过双层级难度评估器（语句级和对话级）和动态学习调度器，解决多模态对话情感识别中的模态不对齐和失衡学习问题。在IEMOCAP和MELD数据集上，将加权F1分数提升1.2%-10.4%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态对话情感识别（MERC）中，模态不对齐（如语言、表情、语调之间的时序或语义错位）以及模态间的学习不平衡问题严重制约了多模态信息的有效利用，亟需一种能够缓解这些问题的方法。

Method: 提出基于自定进度课程学习（SPCL）的即插即用框架：(1) 双层级难度评估器：语句级难度分数建模细粒度模态特异性难度，对话级难度分数捕捉情感依赖和模态连贯性等对话结构；(2) 学习调度器：根据难度分数动态引导模型从简单到困难样本进行训练，可集成到现有MERC架构中。

Result: 在IEMOCAP上，加权F1分数提升约+1.2%至+6.6%；在MELD上，提升最高达+10.4%。不同架构和模态设置下均表现出一致提升，验证了方法的有效性和泛化性。

Conclusion: SPCL作为一种轻量级即插即用模块，能够有效缓解多模态对话情感识别中的模态不对齐和失衡学习问题，提升模型鲁棒性，并在多个数据集和架构上展现出良好的泛化能力。

Abstract: Multimodal Emotion Recognition in Conversations (MERC) is a crucial task for understanding human interactions, where multimodal approaches integrating language, facial expressions, and vocal tone have achieved significant progress. However, modality misalignment and imbalanced learning remain major challenges, limiting the effective utilization of multimodal information. To address this issue, we propose a plug-and-play framework based on Self-Paced Curriculum Learning (SPCL) for MERC. We introduce a dual-level Difficulty Measurer that captures both utterance-level and conversation-level challenges. The utterance-level score models fine-grained modality-specific difficulty, while the conversation-level score captures broader dialogue structures, including emotional dependencies and modality coherence. Based on these scores, the Learning Scheduler dynamically guides training from easier to more difficult instances. By integrating SPCL into existing MERC architectures, our method alleviates modality imbalance and improves model robustness. Extensive experiments on the IEMOCAP and MELD datasets demonstrate consistent improvements across different architectures and modality settings. On IEMOCAP, SPCL improves weighted F1-score by approximately +1.2% to +6.6% over baseline models, while on MELD, gains reach up to +10.4%. These results highlight the effectiveness and generalizability of SPCL as a lightweight plug-and-play module for multimodal emotion recognition.

</details>


### [71] [Calibration, Uncertainty Communication, and Deployment Readiness in CKD Risk Prediction: A Framework Evaluation Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21566)
*Michael O. Eniolade*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了五种机器学习模型在慢性肾病风险预测中的表现，发现尽管模型在内部测试集上达到近乎完美的区分度（AUROC 1.00），但在外部数据集（MIMIC-IV）上校准质量（ECE升至0.68-0.76）和共形预测覆盖度（从0.80-0.98降至0.21-0.25）急剧恶化，所有模型在部署就绪评估中得分均不超过4/16，表明模型不具备临床部署可靠性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有CKD风险预测模型过度关注区分度（如AUROC），忽视了校准质量和不确定性量化，导致临床医生无法判断模型输出的概率是否可靠。本文旨在系统评估模型在校准、共形预测覆盖和部署就绪性方面的表现，并验证其在外部数据上的泛化能力。

Method: 在UCI CKD数据集（400名患者）上训练逻辑回归、随机森林、XGBoost、带Platt缩放的SVM和高斯朴素贝叶斯五种分类器，通过Platt缩放和保序回归进行重校准，使用期望校准误差（ECE）和Brier评分评估校准质量，采用分割共形预测评估不确定性（目标90%边际覆盖率），并在MIMIC-IV演示队列（97名患者）上进行分布压力测试（患病率偏移和特征缺失）。

Result: 所有模型在UCI测试集上AUROC均为1.00，保序重校准后内部ECE降至0.000-0.022。在MIMIC-IV上AUROC降至0.48-0.58，ECE升至0.68-0.76，共形预测覆盖率从0.80-0.98降至0.21-0.25（目标90%）。所有模型在部署就绪检查表中得分不超过4/16。

Conclusion: 近乎完美的内部性能无法迁移到外部数据。在校准稳定性和共形预测覆盖率通过外部数据验证之前，任何临床预测模型都不应进入部署阶段。

Abstract: Machine learning models for chronic kidney disease (CKD) risk prediction often post strong discrimination scores on internal test sets. Calibration and uncertainty quantification get far less attention, leaving clinicians without reliable information about whether the probability outputs are accurate.
  We trained five classifiers on the UCI CKD dataset (400 patients, 62.5% CKD prevalence): logistic regression, random forest, XGBoost, SVM with Platt scaling, and Gaussian naive Bayes. We evaluated each across calibration quality, conformal prediction coverage, and an eight-criterion deployment readiness framework. A distributional stress-test applied the best-calibrated variant of each model to the open-access MIMIC-IV demo cohort (97 patients, 23.7% CKD) to assess behaviour under prevalence shift and feature missingness. We measured calibration before and after Platt scaling and isotonic regression using Expected Calibration Error and Brier Score, and quantified uncertainty through split conformal prediction targeting 90% marginal coverage.
  All five models reached AUROC 1.00 on the UCI test set. Isotonic recalibration reduced internal ECE to 0.000-0.022. On MIMIC-IV, AUROC fell to 0.48-0.58, ECE rose to 0.68-0.76, and conformal coverage dropped from 0.80-0.98 to 0.21-0.25 against a 90% target. No model scored above 4 out of 16 on the deployment readiness checklist.
  Near-perfect internal performance did not transfer. Calibration stability and conformal coverage should be evaluated on external data before any clinical prediction model moves toward deployment.

</details>


### [72] [VeriScale: Adversarial Test-Suite Scaling for Verifiable Code Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22368)
*Yifan Bai,Xiaoyang Liu,Zihao Mou,Guihong Wang,Jian Yu,Shuhan Xie,Yantao Li,Yangyu Zhang,Jingwei Liang,Tao Luo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出VeriScale框架，通过对抗性实现驱动的测试用例扩展与精炼，构建更高质量的代码生成基准（VerinaPlus/VerinaLite），揭示LLM在软件工程中的形式可验证性缺陷。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准测试在正负测试用例的数量和质量上存在局限，导致对LLM生成规约与实现的能力评估过于乐观，缺乏对代码形式可验证性的有效评估。

Method: 提出VeriScale框架，包含两阶段：测试套件扩展（构建多样且具挑战性的测试用例）和测试套件精炼（蒸馏为紧凑但高区分度的套件），并基于Verina实例化为VerinaPlus（扩展83倍以上）和VerinaLite（14倍轻量变体）。

Result: 在8个SOTA LLM上的实验表明，VerinaPlus暴露了原基准隐藏的模型弱点，SpecGen和CodeGen任务得分显著下降；VerinaLite在极低评估成本下保持了相同的区分能力。

Conclusion: 通过对抗性测试用例扩展与精炼能有效构建更可靠的代码生成基准，更准确地评估LLM在软件工程任务中的真实能力。

Abstract: As large language models (LLMs) are increasingly deployed for software engineering, constructing high-quality benchmarks is crucial for evaluating not just the functional correctness, but also the formal verifiability of generated code. However, existing benchmarks are limited by the quantity and quality of positive and negative test cases, leading to an overestimation of model capabilities in generating specifications and implementations. To address this, we propose VeriScale, a novel framework driven by the adversarial implementations. It consists of two stages: test-suite expansion to construct diverse and challenging test cases, and test-suite reduction to distill them into compact yet discriminative suites. While VeriScale is general, we instantiate it on Verina to construct VerinaPlus, which expands the original test suites by over 83$\times$, and VerinaLite, a lightweight 14$\times$ variant. Our experiments across eight state-of-the-art LLMs demonstrate that VerinaPlus exposes substantial model weaknesses hidden by the original benchmark, evidenced by sharp score drops on both SpecGen and CodeGen tasks, whereas VerinaLite maintains this discriminative power at a fraction of the evaluation cost. The enhanced benchmarks and source code are publicly available at https://github.com/XiaoyangLiu-sjtu/VeriScale.

</details>


### [73] [ConTact: Contact-First Antibody CDR Design via Explicit Interface Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21600)
*Mansoor Ahmed,Spencer VonBank,Nadeem Taj,Sujin Lee,Naila Jan,Murray Patterson*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出ConTact模型，将抗体CDR设计显式分解为三个级联阶段（表面互补性指纹学习→CDR-抗原接触预测→接触门控抗原特征注入），通过距离偏置交叉注意力和接触加权损失函数，在CHIMERA-Bench上实现最佳结构质量和表位感知能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有计算抗体CDR设计方法将"识别哪些CDR位置会接触抗原"和"在这些位置选择氨基酸"两个子问题混为一谈，通过均匀消息传递隐式学习接触推理，导致抗原信号在所有位置上被均匀稀释。

Method: 提出ConTact的"contact-then-act"架构，包含三个级联阶段：(1)学习表面互补性指纹；(2)预测CDR-抗原接触；(3)将接触门控的抗原特征注入序列头。使用距离偏置交叉注意力模块编码偏向空间近邻的几何先验，以及接触加权交叉熵损失将梯度信号集中在结合关键位置。

Result: 在CHIMERA-Bench数据集上，RMSD较次优基线改善7%，表位感知F1分数较GNN基线提升10%，序列恢复AAR达到0.38，在多个CDR-H3设计基线中表现最优。

Conclusion: 显式解耦接触预测和氨基酸选择能有效提升CDR设计性能，接触门控机制和距离偏置注意力是注入抗原结构先验的有效手段。

Abstract: Computational antibody CDR design methods condition on antigen structure to generate binding loops, yet existing architectures conflate two fundamentally distinct sub-problems: identifying which CDR positions will contact the antigen, and selecting amino acids at those positions. This conflation forces models to learn contact reasoning implicitly through uniform message passing, diluting antigen signal across all positions equally. We introduce ConTact, a contact-then-act architecture that explicitly decomposes CDR design into three cascaded stages: learning surface complementarity fingerprints, predicting CDR-antigen contacts, and injecting contact-gated antigen features into the sequence head. A distance-biased cross-attention module encodes geometric priors favoring spatial neighbors, while a contact-weighted cross-entropy loss concentrates gradient signal on binding-critical positions. On CHIMERA-Bench dataset, ConTact achieves the best structural quality (7% RMSD improvement over the next-best baseline), best epitope awareness (10% F1 score over GNN baselines), and competitive sequence recovery (AAR 0.38) among several CDR-H3 design baselines.

</details>


### [74] [When Are Teacher Tokens Reliable? Position-Weighted On-Policy Self-Distillation for Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21606)
*Xiaogeng Liu,Xinyan Wang,Yingzi Ma,Yechao Zhang,Chaowei Xiao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出位置加权自蒸馏（PW-OPSD），通过分析推理过程中教师token的可靠性存在轨迹结构特征（而非局部不确定性），在标准自蒸馏基础上引入递增位置权重，显著提升数学推理任务性能，并在多个模型上验证了泛化性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的基于熵的在线自蒸馏方法假设教师token的可靠性可通过局部不确定性度量，但在推理任务中高熵既可能源自不确定性也可能源于良性多样性，现有方法无法区分。本文旨在识别这一问题并提出更优的加权策略。

Method: 1) 提出分支可行性诊断方法：在教师提示语的每个token位置强制采样替代token，测试其是否能恢复正确答案，从而评估token可靠性。2) 发现序列内位置分数（而非局部熵）是最强预测因子（AUROC=0.83）。3) 提出位置加权在线自蒸馏（PW-OPSD）：保持学生采样轨迹、特权教师前向传播和clip forward-KL目标不变，仅增加递增位置权重。

Result: 在Qwen3-4B上位置分数AUROC达0.83（局部不确定性仅0.57）；AIME 2024/2025 Avg@12分别提升+1.0/+1.1点；在DeepSeek-R1-Distill-Llama-8B和Olmo-3-7B-Think上均取得一致的Avg@12改进。

Conclusion: 推理蒸馏中教师token的可靠性具有轨迹结构化特征，利用位置信息进行加权可以在不增加额外教师计算成本的情况下有效提升蒸馏效果。

Abstract: On-policy self-distillation (OPSD) trains a student on its own rollouts using a privileged teacher, but its standard objective weights all generated tokens equally, implicitly treating the privileged teacher target as equally reliable at every student-visited prefix. Existing entropy-based OPD methods relax this uniformity by modulating token-level supervision with teacher entropy, but high teacher entropy in reasoning has an ambiguous reliability meaning: it can reflect either non-viable uncertainty or benign solution diversity. To identify this phenomenon, we introduce a branch-viability diagnostic. Specifically, we record next-token alternatives from the privileged-answer teacher prompt, force each alternative after the student prompt plus its on-policy spine prefix, and test whether the resulting student-template continuation recovers the correct answer. On Qwen3-4B, we find that an oriented within-sequence position score is the strongest tested predictor of teacher-token reliability, reaching an area-under-ROC-curve (AUROC) of 0.83; local uncertainty scores are at most 0.57. Motivated by this trajectory-level structure, we propose Position-Weighted On-Policy Self-Distillation (PW-OPSD), which applies an increasing position weight while keeping the same student rollout, privileged teacher pass, and clipped forward-KL target as OPSD.
