On a 2{,}232-patient nephrology cohort, the CKD instantiation achieves a dynamic-50\% history rollout test mean absolute error (MAE) of 7.384 and root-mean-square error (RMSE) of 10.256, against 7.964 and 11.069 for a tuned GPT-5.5 structured-prompting baseline ($-7.28\%$ MAE, $-7.35\%$ RMSE), with the gain dominated by the dialogue portion of patient--health-coach communication. The framework is not CKD-specific: its architecture, loss design, and training protocol apply to any chronic condition that can be cast as periodic clinical state interleaved with structured and conversational interventions.

</details>


### [110] [An Improved Adaptive PID Optimizer with Enhanced Convergence and Stability for Deep Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21968)
*Saurabh Saini,Kapil Ahuja,Thomas Wick,Saurav Kumar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 IAdaPID-ADG 优化器，将 AMSGrad 的非递增有效学习率和 DiffGrad 的梯度差调制因子融入 AdaPID，同时解决了收敛性和稳定性问题，在多个数据集上显著优于现有优化器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动量SGD存在噪声梯度与过冲现象；Adam和PID分别解决其一，但AdaPID虽结合两者却继承了Adam的收敛性和稳定性缺陷。本文旨在单一框架内同时解决这两个问题。

Method: 将AMSGrad中非递增有效学习率机制与DiffGrad中基于梯度差的调制因子创新性地集成到AdaPID框架中，形成IAdaPID-ADG优化器。

Result: 在MNIST、CIFAR10、IARC和AnnoCerv数据集上，IAdaPID-ADG显著优于所有竞争优化器；在MNIST上的消融实验验证了各组件贡献。

Conclusion: IAdaPID-ADG通过融合非递增学习率和梯度差调制，有效解决了AdaPID的收敛与稳定性问题，成为更优的深度学习优化器。

Abstract: Optimization is essential in deep learning. The foundational method upon which most optimizers are built is momentum-based stochastic gradient descent. However, it suffers from two key drawbacks. First, it has noisy and varying gradients, and second, it has an overshoot phenomenon. To address noisy gradients, Adam was proposed, which remains the most widely used adaptive optimizer. To address the overshoot phenomenon, a control-theory-based PID optimizer was proposed. To tackle both the limitations within a single framework, several variants of Adaptive PID (AdaPID) have recently been proposed.
  Although AdaPID performs well, it still inherits two critical drawbacks from Adam, namely convergence and stability issues. In this work, we address both these limitations. To fix the convergence issue, we uniquely integrate the idea of using a non-increasing effective learning rate into AdaPID (originally proposed in AMSGrad, an extension of Adam). To fix the stability issue, we innovatively integrate a gradient difference based modulation factor into AdaPID (originally proposed in DiffGrad, another extension of Adam). Combining both these ideas in AdaPID, results in our novel IAdaPID-ADG optimizer.
  We evaluate our proposed optimizer on multiple datasets, including benchmark datasets (MNIST and CIFAR10) and real-world datasets (IARC and AnnoCerv). The IAdaPID-ADG substantially outperforms all competing optimizers. Additionally, we perform an ablation study on the MNIST dataset to demonstrate the contribution of each added component.

</details>


### [111] [Reasoning through Verifiable Forecast Actions: Consistency-Grounded RL for Financial LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21975)
*Jialin Chen,Aosong Feng,Harshit Verma,Siyi Gu,Haiwen Wang,Ali Maatouk,Yixuan He,Yifeng Gao,Leandros Tassiulas,Rex Ying*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: StockR1是一种时序增强的大语言模型，通过可验证的预测动作（forecast action）统一股票预测与金融推理。模型先发出结构化的定性预测动作，再调用时序解码器生成未来分布轨迹，并利用强化学习优化，在金融问答和股票预测任务上显著优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法要么将时序数据抽象为文本，要么将预测与语言推理解耦，导致定性推理与定量结果之间存在根本性不匹配。金融市场的非平稳性、低信噪比以及对外部信息的强依赖性使得这一问题尤为突出。

Method: 基于工具调用设计，模型首先生成结构化的可解释预测动作（forecast action），再基于该动作调用时序解码器生成分布性未来轨迹。采用强化学习优化全流程，奖励函数联合考量答案有效性、预测准确性以及生成动作与观测时序动态的一致性，并通过样本级不确定性标量对奖励进行重加权。

Result: 在覆盖10年的大规模金融问答与股票预测基准上，StockR1-4B提升推理准确率17.7%，StockR1-8B提升25.9%，一致优于时序基线模型和通用大语言模型。

Conclusion: 结构化预测动作能够在语言推理与时间序列预测之间建立有效的协同关系，使LLM能够进行可验证、可解释且基于数值的推理决策。

Abstract: Financial markets are characterized by extreme non-stationarity, low signal-to-noise ratios, and strong dependence on external information such as news, company fundamentals, and macroeconomic signals. Yet, existing approaches either abstract time-series into text or decouple forecasting from language-based reasoning, leading to a fundamental mismatch between qualitative reasoning and quantitative outcomes. To address this, we introduce StockR1, a time-series-enhanced LLM that unifies stock forecasting and financial reasoning through a verifiable forecast action. Based on a tool-call design, the model first emits a forecast action, which is a structured and interpretable representation of its qualitative market outlook. It then invokes a time-series decoder conditioned on this action to generate distributional future trajectories, leading to more informed question answering and financial reasoning. We optimize the full pipeline with reinforcement learning, where rewards jointly reflect answer validity, forecast accuracy, and consistency between generated actions and observed time-series dynamics. In addition, rewards are reweighted by a sample-level uncertainty scalar, encouraging the model to accommodate varying uncertainty in market dynamics. We evaluate StockR1 on financial question answering and stock forecasting over a large-scale 10-year benchmark. Our method consistently outperforms time-series baselines and general-purpose LLMs, improving reasoning accuracy by 17.7% (4B) and 25.9% (8B). These findings demonstrate that structuring the forecast actions establishes a powerful synergy between language reasoning and temporal prediction, enabling LLMs to reason through verifiable, interpretable, and numerically grounded decisions.

</details>


### [112] [Check Your LLM's Secret Dictionary! Five Lines of Code Reveal What Your LLM Learned (Including What It Shouldn't Have)](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22005)
*Hisashi Miyashita*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 通过仅对LLM的lm_head权重矩阵进行SVD分解（仅需5行PyTorch代码，无需模型推理），可直接从模型权重中揭示可解释的语义子空间。分析GPT-OSS-120B、Gemma-2-2B和Qwen2.5-1.5B发现，不同模型的奇异值谱和词汇聚类结构存在系统性差异；引入VCS和WPS两个指标分别量化子空间一致性和检测故障token；发现伦理问题子空间源于预训练且无法被后训练对齐移除；建议将lm_head SVD分析作为标准发布前安全审计步骤。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM安全审计方法依赖模型推理或微调，成本高昂且难以在训练早期发现潜在问题（如故障token、伦理敏感词汇）。作者希望开发一种无需模型推理、仅从权重本身即可检测语义子空间和安全问题的轻量级方法，作为标准化的预发布安全审计工具。

Method: 对transformer LLM的lm_head权重矩阵进行奇异值分解（SVD），提取左奇异向量；每个左奇异向量对应一个语义子空间，通过分析其高值维度对应的词汇聚类，揭示模型训练数据构成和语料偏向。提出Vocabulary Cluster Score (VCS)量化子空间连贯性，Weighted Projection Score (WPS)作为静态故障token检测器。

Result: GPT-OSS-120B呈现梯度分层的功能分化子空间；Gemma-2-2B被19世纪前英语正字法主导，形成阶梯式聚类结构；Qwen2.5-1.5B展现广泛多语言覆盖但包含伦理不适合直接发表的子空间。Base-instruct对比表明伦理问题子空间源自预训练，后训练对齐无法移除。WPS成功检测到CJK语言社区广泛报告的高知名度故障token（shokubutsu-hyakka-tsu, ID 137606），无需任何模型推理。

Conclusion: lm_head SVD分析可作为标准化的预发布安全审计步骤，高效检测伦理敏感子空间和故障token。研究还提出SVD引导的tokenizer优化和更具可控性的LLM设计方向。

Abstract: We show that singular value decomposition of the lm_head} weight matrix of a transformer-based large language model -- requiring only five lines of PyTorch and no model inference -- reveals interpretable semantic subspaces directly from the model weights. Each left singular vector identifies the vocabulary tokens most readily selected when the hidden state aligns with the corresponding singular direction; inspecting these clusters exposes the model's training data composition and curation philosophy.
  Analysing GPT-OSS-120B, Gemma-2-2B, and Qwen2.5-1.5B, we find that singular value spectra and vocabulary cluster structures differ systematically across models: GPT exhibits a graduated hierarchy of functionally differentiated subspaces; Gemma is dominated by pre-nineteenth-century English orthography, forming a stepwise clustering structure that may contribute to high output controllability; and Qwen exhibits broad multilingual coverage alongside subspaces whose vocabulary the authors have determined to be ethically inappropriate for direct publication.
  Base-instruct comparison reveals that ethically concerning subspaces originate in pretraining and are not removed by post-training alignment. We introduce the Vocabulary Cluster Score (VCS) to quantify subspace coherence, and the Weighted Projection Score (WPS) as a static glitch token detector; applying WPS to GPT-OSS-120B recovers shokubutsu-hyakka-tsu (ID 137606), a well-known glitch token widely reported in the CJK language community, without any model inference. We propose a taxonomy of root causes for problematic vocabulary content and call for lm_head} SVD analysis to be adopted as a standard pre-release safety auditing step. Our findings further suggest directions toward SVD-guided tokenizer optimisation and more controllable LLM design.

</details>


### [113] [CASE-NET: Deep Spatio-Temporal Representation Learning via Causal Attention and Channel Recalibration for Multivariate Time Series Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22043)
*Fan Zhang,Yating Cui,Hua Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出CASE-NET架构，通过因果时间编码器（强制时间因果约束）和自适应通道重校准模块（信息瓶颈降噪）解决多变量时间序列分类中的时序非因果性和通道噪声问题，在6个异构数据集上达到SOTA，AWR数据集准确率98.6%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有MTS多尺度范式存在两个关键瓶颈：标准编码器的时间非因果性导致非平稳动态中的时序混杂，以及缺乏显式通道显著性机制使得噪声污染潜在空间。

Method: CASE-NET包含两个核心组件：1) 因果时间编码器（Causal Temporal Encoder），通过掩码自注意力和因果卷积强制实施物理时间箭头约束；2) 自适应通道重校准模块（Adaptive Channel Recalibration），作为信息瓶颈抑制有害噪声。两者协同实现结构流形预处理。

Result: 在6个异构领域数据集上的综合评估显示，CASE-NET在4个任务上建立了新的SOTA基准，AWR数据集峰值准确率达98.6%，在非平稳状态下表现出优异的鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 通过引入因果约束和通道显著性机制，有效提升了MTS分类在非平稳动态下的表征保真度和鲁棒性，验证了结构流形预处理在时序建模中的价值。

Abstract: Multivariate time series (MTS) classification is foundational to pervasive computing and financial analysis, yet existing multi-scale paradigms are often constrained by suboptimal representation fidelity. We identify two critical bottlenecks: temporal non-causality in standard encoders that induces temporal confounding in non-stationary dynamics, and the absence of explicit channel saliency mechanisms that allows noise to contaminate the latent space. To address these challenges, we propose the Causal Attention and Spatio-temporal Encoder Network (CASE-NET), an architecture designed for structural manifold pre-conditioning. CASE-NET synergizes a Causal Temporal Encoder, which enforces physical arrow-of-time constraints via masked self-attention and causal convolutions, with an Adaptive Channel Recalibration module functioning as an information bottleneck to suppress detrimental noise. Comprehensive evaluations across six heterogeneous domains demonstrate that CASE-NET establishes new state-of-the-art benchmarks on four tasks, achieving a peak accuracy of 98.6% on the AWR dataset and superior robustness in non-stationary regimes.

</details>


### [114] [LABO: LLM-Accelerated Bayesian Optimization through Broad Exploration and Selective Experimentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22054)
*Zhuo Chen,Xinzhe Yuan,Jianshu Zhang,Jinzong Dong,Ruichen Zhou,Yingchun Niu,Tianhang Zhou,Yu Yang Fredrik Liu,Yuqiang Li,Nanyang Ye,Qinying Gu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出LABO框架，通过门控机制动态平衡LLM预测与真实实验，利用低成本LLM探索搜索空间，仅对高不确定性区域进行真实实验，从而在科学贝叶斯优化中实现更高样本效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法直接将LLM嵌入贝叶斯优化流程，未能充分利用LLM相较于真实实验的低评估成本优势。

Method: 提出LABO框架，采用门控准则动态平衡LLM预测与真实实验的依赖；利用低成本LLM评估广泛探索搜索空间，仅对高不确定性区域保留昂贵真实实验。

Result: 在多种科学任务中，相同实验预算下LABO一致优于现有方法；提供了含累积遗憾界的理论分析，形式化证明了效率提升。

Conclusion: LABO为将LLM集成到科学发现流程中提供了一种实用且理论上有据可依的方法。

Abstract: The high cost and data scarcity in scientific exploration have motivated the use of large language models (LLMs) as knowledge-driven components in Bayesian optimization (BO). However, existing approaches typically embed LLMs directly into the sampling or surrogate modeling pipeline, without fully leveraging their significantly lower evaluation cost compared to real-world experiments. To address this limitation, we propose LLM-Accelerated Bayesian Optimization (LABO), a framework that combines LLM predictions with experimental observations within a single BO loop. LABO employs a gating criterion to dynamically balance the reliance on LLM predictions versus actual experiments. By leveraging inexpensive LLM evaluations to broadly explore the search space and reserving costly real experiments only for regions with high uncertainty, LABO achieves more sample-efficient optimization. We provide a theoretical analysis with a cumulative regret bound that formalizes this efficiency gain. Empirical results across diverse scientific tasks demonstrate that LABO consistently outperforms existing methods under identical experimental budgets. Our results suggest that LABO offers a practical and theoretically grounded approach for integrating LLMs into scientific discovery workflows.

</details>


### [115] [Prototype-Guided Classification Sub-Task Decoupling Framework: Enhancing Generalization and Interpretability for Multivariate Time Series](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22055)
*Xianhao Song,Yuang Zhang,Yuqi She,Liping Wang,Xuemin Lin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: PDFTime是一个原型引导的框架，将时间序列分类重构为多阶段决策过程，通过解耦的特征到标签映射实现渐进式判别，在UEA和UCR基准上达到SOTA性能，在UCR的128个数据集中有80个取得最高准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有时间序列分类方法采用直接的特征到标签分类范式，通过单一的线性投影将高维时序嵌入压缩为类别logits，这种范式将特征提取和决策逻辑混为一谈，导致模型难以同时保证准确性和可解释性。

Method: 提出PDFTime框架，利用学习到的原型在潜在空间中近似类条件特征分布，通过不同粒度的分类子任务实现渐进式判别，将时间序列分类重构为解耦的、多阶段基于相似性的推理过程。

Result: 在UEA和UCR基准上达到SOTA性能，在UCR的128个数据集中有80个取得最高准确率，在一致性和泛化性上显著优于近期强基线方法。

Conclusion: PDFTime是首个将时间序列分类重构为解耦的多阶段相似性推理过程的框架，打破了长期存在的直接黑箱特征到标签映射范式。

Abstract: Time Series Classification (TSC) is a long-standing research problem that has gained increasing attention in recent years with the rapid growth of large-scale temporal data. Despite substantial progress enabled by deep learning, designing TSC models that are both accurate and interpretable remains a challenging task. Many existing approaches adopt a direct feature-to-label classification paradigm, by collapsing high-dimensional temporal embeddings into class logits via a single linear projection (often after global pooling), the paradigm conflates feature extraction and decision logic into an inseparable mapping.
  To address these limitations, we propose PDFTime, a prototype-guided framework that reformulates time series classification as a multi-stage decision process. Instead of direct feature-to-label mapping, PDFTime leverages learned prototypes to approximate class-conditional feature distributions in the latent space, enabling progressive discrimination through classification sub-tasks of varying granularity. To our knowledge, PDFTime is the first framework to reformulate time series classification as a decoupled, multi-stage similarity-based reasoning process, breaking the long-standing paradigm of direct, black-box feature-to-label mapping. Extensive evaluations demonstrate that PDFTime achieves state-of-the-art (SOTA) performance across UEA and UCR benchmarks. Notably, it secures the top-$1$ accuracy on 80 out of 128 datasets in the UCR archive, significantly outperforming recent strong baselines in both consistency and generalization.

</details>


### [116] [From Reasoning Chains to Verifiable Subproblems: Curriculum Reinforcement Learning Enables Credit Assignment for LLM Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22074)
*Xitai Jiang,Zihan Tang,Wenze Lin,Yang Yue,Shenzhi Wang,Gao Huang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: SCRL是一种子问题课程强化学习框架，通过从参考推理链中提取可验证的子问题，将困难问题的部分进展转化为可验证的学习信号，并采用子问题级归一化实现细粒度信用分配，在多项数学推理基准上显著优于GRPO等基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于结果奖励的RLVR在困难问题上效率低下，因为正确的最终答案 rollout 稀少，且样本级信用分配无法利用失败尝试中的部分进展。

Method: 从参考推理链中提取可验证的子问题，构建子问题课程（最后一个子问题为原问题）；采用子问题级归一化（在每个子问题位置独立归一化奖励），将优势函数分配给对应的答案片段。

Result: 在7个数学推理基准上，SCRL在Qwen3-4B-Base上平均准确率比GRPO高+4.1个百分点，在Qwen3-14B-Base上高+1.9个百分点；在AIME24、AIME25和IMO-Bench上，Qwen3-4B-Base的pass@1提高+3.7个百分点，pass@64提高+4.6个百分点。

Conclusion: 子问题课程能够将困难问题从梯度死区中解救出来，原问题越难，相对收益越大；SCRL在困难推理问题上实现了更好的探索。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning from verifiable rewards (RLVR) has shown strong promise for LLM reasoning, but outcome-based RLVR remains inefficient on hard problems because correct final-answer rollouts are rare and sample-level credit assignment cannot use partial progress in failed attempts. We introduce SCRL (Subproblem Curriculum Reinforcement Learning), a curriculum RL framework that derives verifiable subproblems from reference reasoning chains and fixes the final subproblem as the original problem. This turns partial progress on hard problems into verifiable learning signals. Algorithmically, SCRL uses subproblem-level normalization, which normalizes rewards independently at each subproblem position and assigns the resulting advantages to the corresponding answer spans, enabling finer-grained credit assignment without external rubrics or reward models. Our analysis shows that subproblem curricula lift hard problems out of gradient dead zones, with larger relative gains as the original problem becomes harder. Across seven mathematical reasoning benchmarks, SCRL outperforms strong curriculum-learning baselines, improving average accuracy over GRPO by +4.1 points on Qwen3-4B-Base and +1.9 points on Qwen3-14B-Base. On AIME24, AIME25, and IMO-Bench, SCRL further improves pass@1 by +3.7 points and pass@64 by +4.6 points on Qwen3-4B-Base, indicating better exploration on hard reasoning problems.

</details>


### [117] [Can Breath Biomarkers Causally Influence Blood Glucose? Investigating VOC-Mediated Modulation in Diabetes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22075)
*Varsha Sharma,Prasanta K. Guha,Avik Ghose*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本研究提出了一种基于挥发性有机化合物（VOCs）和生活方式变量的非侵入性数据驱动框架，用于早期糖尿病风险识别，结合因果推断、机器学习和聚类分析，实现了从风险筛查到人群分层的完整流程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 糖尿病是全球健康负担，早期检测对及时干预至关重要，但目前缺乏非侵入性、可解释的早期筛查工具。

Method: 使用因果推断技术评估特定VOCs（丙酮、异丙醇、异戊二烯、乙醇）对血糖水平的影响；设计分类器区分糖尿病患者与非糖尿病患者；构建基于风险的排序系统识别“灰色地带”个体；应用高斯混合模型发现人群中的自然聚类。

Result: 特定VOCs对血糖水平表现出强因果影响；机器学习模型能够可靠地对高风险个体进行分类和分层。

Conclusion: 所提出的集成因果-可解释分析框架可为开发非侵入性糖尿病早期筛查工具提供支持。

Abstract: Diabetes is a global health burden, and early detection is critical for timely intervention. This study explores a non-invasive, data-driven framework to identify individuals at risk of diabetes using Volatile Organic Compounds (VOCs) and lifestyle variables. We use causal inference techniques to estimate the impact of VOCs such as acetone, isopropanol, isoprene, and ethanol on blood glucose levels. Additionally, we designed a classifier to distinguish diabetics from non-diabetics using non-invasive markers. We created a risk-based ranking system for individuals in the "gray zone," and identified natural clusters in the population using Gaussian Mixture Model. Our results suggest that specific VOCs exhibit a strong causal influence on glucose levels and that machine learning models can reliably classify and stratify individuals at high risk. This integrated causal-explainable analysis can support the development of tool for non-invasive early screening of diabetes.

</details>


### [118] [Aerodynamic force reconstruction using physics-informed Gaussian processes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22111)
*Gledson Rodrigo Tondo,Igor Kavrakov,Guido Morgenthal*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种概率物理信息机器学习方法，用于从含噪声的结构动力响应测量中重建气动载荷，避免了过拟合和正则化，支持异构多保真度数据。在Great Belt East Bridge的线性非定常气动载荷重建中表现出色，准确度在均方根误差、幅值、相位角和峰值方面均很高。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 气动载荷建模常依赖简化假设，导致精度受限；在噪声或不完整数据下验证模型尤为困难，亟需一种能从不完美测量数据中准确重建气动载荷的方法。

Method: 采用概率物理信息机器学习方法，结合物理约束与概率建模，利用异构和多保真度数据进行训练，避免过拟合且无需正则化方案，从含噪声的结构动力响应中重建气动载荷。

Result: 以Great Belt East Bridge为案例，在线性非定常假设下进行模拟，重建的气动载荷与真实载荷在均方根误差、幅值、相位角和峰值等多个指标上高度吻合。

Conclusion: 该方法在载荷重建上具有广泛适用性，可用于模型验证、未来载荷估计及结构损伤预测，为气动载荷建模提供了一种可靠且鲁棒的替代方案。

Abstract: Accurate modeling of aerodynamic loads is essential for understanding and predicting the responses of complex structural systems. However, these models often rely on simplifications of the true physical forces, introducing assumptions that can limit their accuracy. Validating such models becomes particularly challenging in the presence of noisy or incomplete data. To address this, we introduce a probabilistic physics-informed machine learning approach designed to reconstruct the underlying aerodynamic loads from noisy measurements of structural dynamic responses. The model avoids overfitting, eliminates the need for regularization schemes, and allows for the use of heterogeneous and multi-fidelity data during the training process. The efficacy of the approach is demonstrated through the reconstruction of aerodynamic loads on the Great Belt East Bridge, simulated under a linear unsteady assumption. Results show a strong agreement between true and predicted loads, particularly related to root mean squared errors, magnitude, phase angle and peak values of the signals. The method for load reconstructing holds broad applicability, such as modeling validation, future load estimation, and structural damage prognosis.

</details>


### [119] [Short-Term-to-Long-Term Memory Transfer for Knowledge Graphs under Partial Observability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22142)
*Taewoon Kim,Vincent François-Lavet,Michael Cochez*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于神经符号的逐项Q学习框架，用于在部分可观测强化学习中显式建模从短期到长期的符号观察记忆转移决策，实验表明该方法在记忆容量受限时优于符号和神经基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于记忆的部分可观测强化学习方法大多没有显式建模从短期观察到长期记忆的符号化转移过程，缺乏可解释的记忆决策机制。

Method: 将记忆转移建模为神经符号值决策问题，对每个观测三元组独立决定保留或丢弃；采用逐项Q学习设计（共享参数），通过时间差分更新匹配连续步骤中的项目。

Result: 在RoomKG基准上，长期记忆容量128时学习到的转移决策优于符号基线（含时间注释）、LSTM/Transformer等历史基线；消融实验显示轻量级短期记忆变体最佳，策略能保留导航和查询相关事实、丢弃低价值候选事实。

Conclusion: 显式建模记忆转移决策在记忆约束下能产生可解释且更优的记忆管理行为。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning under partial observability requires deciding what information to retain, yet most memory-based approaches do not explicitly model short-term-to-long-term transfer of symbolic observations. We study this transfer process in a temporal knowledge-graph memory setting and cast it as a neuro-symbolic value-based decision problem: for each observed triple, the agent chooses whether to keep or drop it before long-term insertion. To handle variable-sized short-term buffers, we use a per-item Q-learning design with shared parameters and a practical temporal-difference update over matched items across consecutive steps. On the RoomKG benchmark at long-term memory capacity 128, learned transfer decisions outperform symbolic and neural baselines, including symbolic baselines with temporal annotations and history-based LSTM/Transformer baselines. Across transfer-policy ablations, a lightweight local short-term-only variant performs best, and step-level behavior shows that the policy keeps navigation- and query-relevant facts while discarding lower-value candidate facts, supporting explicit and interpretable memory decisions under memory constraints.

</details>


### [120] [Algebraic Machine Learning for Small-to-Medium Datasets Is Competitive against Strong Standard Baselines](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22155)
*David Mendez,Fernando Martin-Maroto,Gonzalo G. de Polavieja*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文评估了代数机器学习（AML）框架，该框架通过代数结构的子直分解而非数值优化进行学习。实验发现，在小到中等规模（50-2000训练样本）的图像数据集上，仅使用训练数据且无需验证或交叉验证的AML表现优于包括CNN在内的交叉验证基线方法。在相同规模的表格数据集上，XGBoost整体表现最佳，但AML与LightGBM、随机森林等方法的性能相当。AML使用通用的代数归纳偏置，无需任务相关超参数调优，也不依赖模态特定偏置。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 符号方法通常被认为在现实监督任务上无法与强现代学习方法竞争，本文旨在检验代数机器学习（AML）这一非数值优化框架在这种设定下的实际表现。

Method: AML框架通过子直分解代数结构进行学习，不使用数值优化方法。实验在图像和表格分类任务上，与CNN、XGBoost、LightGBM、随机森林等标准基线方法进行比较，并改变训练集规模（50-2000样本）。

Result: 在小到中等规模图像数据集上，AML（无验证/交叉验证）优于交叉验证的CNN等基线；在表格数据集上，XGBoost最佳，但AML与LightGBM和随机森林性能相当。

Conclusion: AML通过通用的代数归纳偏置，在两个截然不同的数据类型上取得了竞争力强的性能，无需模态特定偏置或任务相关超参数调优，证明了符号方法在特定规模范围内可与强现代学习方法匹敌。

Abstract: Symbolic methods are generally not considered competitive with strong modern learners on realistic supervised tasks. We evaluate Algebraic Machine Learning (AML), a framework that learns through subdirect decomposition of algebraic structure rather than numerical optimization, against standard baselines on image and tabular classification across varying training-set sizes. We find that AML trained only on training data without using validation or cross-validation outperforms a family of cross-validated baseline methods including CNNs on small to medium image datasets (50--2000 training examples). On tabular datasets in the same size range, XGBoost is overall the best performing method, but AML is nonetheless comparable to methods incorporating task-specific biases such as LightGBM and random forests. AML achieves this competitive performance across two very different types of datasets using a generic algebraic inductive bias, rather than the modality-specific biases built into standard baselines like CNNs for images or XGBoost for tabular data, and requires no cross validation because it has no task-dependent hyperparameters to tune.

</details>


### [121] [One-Way Policy Optimization for Self-Evolving LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22156)
*Shuo Yang,Jinda Lu,Kexin Huang,Chiyu Ma,Shaohang Wei,Yuyang Liu,Guoyin Wang,Jingren Zhou,Li Yuan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出OWPO方法，通过解耦优化方向与更新幅度，对策略偏差进行非对称加权（劣偏差加速对齐、优偏差增益锁定），并引入迭代参考更新形成“棘轮效应”，在RLVR框架下实现了超越固定先验的连续自演化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有RLVR方法中，二进制验证器奖励的稀疏性导致训练效率低、优化不稳定；而参考策略的token级约束会无差别惩罚所有偏差，在策略试图超越参考时可能翻转验证器确定的优化方向，从而抑制性能提升。

Method: 提出One-Way Policy Optimization (OWPO)，核心思想是解耦优化方向与更新幅度：验证器决定更新方向，参考策略仅用于调整幅度。具体采用非对称重加权——对劣偏差（策略落后于参考）进行加速对齐，对优偏差（策略超越参考）进行增益锁定；同时引入迭代参考更新机制，形成“棘轮效应”以持续巩固收益。

Result: 实验结果表明OWPO在多个强基线（DAPO、OPD、MOPD）上均取得更优性能，打破了固定先验的瓶颈，实现了无需外部参考模型的连续自演化。

Conclusion: OWPO通过解耦优化方向与幅度、非对称加权以及迭代参考更新，有效解决了RLVR中验证器奖励稀疏和约束冲突的问题，为LLM推理能力的持续提升提供了一种无需外部参考的自演化范式。

Abstract: Reinforcement Learning with Verifiable Rewards (RLVR) has become a promising paradigm for scaling reasoning capabilities of Large Language Models (LLMs). However, the sparsity of binary verifier rewards often leads to low efficiency and optimization instability. To stabilize training, existing methods typically impose token-level constraints relative to a reference policy. We identify that such constraints penalize deviations indiscriminately; this can flip verifier-determined direction when the policy attempts to outperform the reference, thereby suppressing gains. To resolve this, we propose One-Way Policy Optimization (OWPO), a method based on the principle of decoupling optimization direction from update magnitude. In OWPO, the verifier dictates the update direction, while the reference policy serves only to adjust the magnitude. Specifically, OWPO applies asymmetric reweighting: it performs Accelerated Alignment for inferior deviations (where the policy lags behind the reference) and Gain Locking for superior deviations (where the policy surpasses the reference). Furthermore, by incorporating iterative reference updates, OWPO creates a ``Ratchet Effect'' that continuously consolidates gains. Experimental results demonstrate that OWPO outperforms strong baselines, including DAPO, OPD, and MOPD, breaking the bottleneck of fixed priors to enable continuous self-evolution without reliance on external reference models.

</details>


### [122] [IKNO: Infinite-order Kernel Neural Operators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22182)
*Pengyuan Zhu,Ivor W. Tsang,Yueming Lyu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出无限阶核神经算子（IKNO），通过无限阶核积分构造神经算子，并给出优雅的闭式有限近似。IKNO-Vanilla和IKNO-TP两种变体分别通过Kronecker特征分解和逐轴预解式实现全局信息聚合，在多个基准上取得SOTA精度且保持高效计算。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有神经算子主要依赖一阶核积分近似，表达能力严重受限，无法充分捕获复杂全局依赖关系。

Method: 提出无限阶核神经算子（IKNO），基于无限阶核积分构造，导出闭式有限近似。两种变体：IKNO-Vanilla通过Kronecker特征分解在乘积网格上应用全核预解式，IKNO-TP通过逐轴预解式的张量积组合实现。

Result: 在时间和时间无关的基准任务上（包括大规模工业数据集），IKNO几乎在所有数据集上一致取得SOTA精度，且对超大点云具有可扩展性。

Conclusion: IKNO通过无限阶核积分突破了传统一阶近似的表达限制，在保持计算效率的同时显著提升全局信息聚合能力，是神经算子的重要进展。

Abstract: Neural operators have achieved significant success in modern scientific computing due to their flexibility and strong generalization capabilities. Existing models, however, primarily rely on first-order kernel integral approximations, which severely limit their expressivity. To address this, we propose the Infinite-order Kernel Neural Operator (IKNO), which constructs neural operators via infinite-order kernel integrals and admits an elegant closed-form finite approximation. We develop two complementary infinite-order neural operator constructions: IKNO-Vanilla, which applies the full-kernel resolvent on the product grid via Kronecker eigendecomposition, and IKNO-TP, an alternative tensor-product operator that composes per-axis resolvents. Furthermore, we develop fast computation schemes for both variants of IKNO, which achieve outstanding global information aggregation while maintaining high computational efficiency. Empirically, we evaluate our IKNO on both time-dependent and time-independent benchmarks with arbitrary input shapes, including large-scale industrial datasets. Extensive experiments demonstrate that the IKNO method consistently achieves the SOTA accuracy with significant improvements on nearly all benchmark datasets while maintaining scalability to very large point clouds.

</details>


### [123] [From Sequential Nodes to GPU Batches: Parallel Branch and Bound for Optimal $k$-Sparse GLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22188)
*Jiachang Liu,Andrea Lodi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种CPU-GPU协同框架，通过批量处理分支定界节点来加速带有基数约束的广义线性模型的精确求解，实验显示一到两个数量级的加速，并能扩展到收集整个Rashomon集以支持下游统计分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GPU加速的一阶方法在连续优化中表现优异，但未能有效迁移到带有离散变量、组合结构和非线性目标的优化问题（如基数约束广义线性模型的最优解认证）。主要挑战包括分支定界中异构节点的顺序处理以及CPU-GPU间频繁的数据移动。

Method: 提出一个简单、通用、模块化的CPU-GPU框架，核心思路是将多个BnB节点分批在GPU上处理。框架围绕少量GPU高效例程构建，采用填充（padding）技术与轻量级自定义内核来处理不规则的节点数据结构。

Result: 在具有挑战性的实例上实现了一到两个数量级的加速，且最优性间隙为零。框架还可扩展以收集完整的Rashomon集，支持变量重要性分析、基于用户特定指标（如AUC）的模型选择等下游统计任务。

Conclusion: 该框架有效弥合了GPU加速与离散组合优化之间的鸿沟，证明了批量节点处理策略在BnB中的实用性，并展示了在统计可解释性方面的扩展价值。

Abstract: GPUs have significantly accelerated first-order methods for large-scale optimization, especially in continuous optimization. However, this success has not transferred cleanly to problems with discrete variables, combinatorial structure, and nonlinear objectives, such as certifying optimal solutions for cardinality-constrained generalized linear models. Major challenges include the sequential processing of heterogeneous nodes in branch and bound (BnB) and frequent data movement between the CPU and GPU. We propose a simple, generic, and modular CPU--GPU framework that processes multiple BnB nodes in batches on GPUs. The framework is built around a small set of GPU-efficient routines and uses padding together with lightweight custom kernels to handle irregular node data structures. Experiments show one to two orders of magnitude speedups and zero optimality gap on challenging instances. The framework can also be extended to collect the entire Rashomon set, enabling downstream statistical analysis such as variable-importance analysis and model selection under secondary user-specific measures (e.g., AUC in classification).

</details>


### [124] [Bandit Convex Optimization with Gradient Prediction Adaptivity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22191)
*Shuche Wang,Adarsh Barik,Vincent Y. F. Tan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究乐观梯度预测能否提升凸赌博机在线学习的遗憾界。在单点反馈下，即使预测误差$S_T=o(T)$，仍存在$\Omega(\sqrt{T})$的遗憾下界。针对两点反馈，提出TP-VR-OPT算法，利用方差缩减梯度估计器实现$O(\sqrt{d\,\mathbb{E}[S_T]})$的遗憾界，并证明该结果在$\sqrt{d}$因子内最优。进一步提出自适应变体和非平稳场景扩展。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索乐观梯度预测能否以预测自适应方式改进凸赌博机在线学习中最坏情况下的遗憾保证，即遗憾界是否可随累积预测误差$S_T$缩放而非固定为$\sqrt{T}$。

Method: 提出两点方差缩减乐观梯度下降（TP-VR-OPT），核心是构造方差缩减梯度估计器，其方差随预测误差而非梯度范数缩放。在此基础上设计自适应变体以消除对先验知识的需求，并扩展至非平稳环境。

Result: 单点反馈下存在$\Omega(\sqrt{T})$的不可回避下界；两点反馈下TP-VR-OPT达到$O(\sqrt{d\,\mathbb{E}[S_T]})$遗憾界，且匹配信息论下界$\Omega(\sqrt{\mathbb{E}[S_T]})$；自适应变体无需先验知识即可达到相同保证。

Conclusion: 揭示了单点反馈中梯度估计方差阻碍预测增益的根本障碍，在两点反馈下实现了预测自适应的最优遗憾，为凸赌博机优化中利用预测信息建立了理论基础。

Abstract: Bandit convex optimization (BCO) is a fundamental online learning framework with partial feedback, where the learner observes only the loss incurred at the chosen decision point in each round. In this work, we investigate whether optimistic gradient predictions can improve worst-case regret guarantees in a prediction-adaptive manner. Specifically, given gradient predictions $m_t$, we seek regret bounds that scale with the cumulative prediction error $S_T=\sum_{t=1}^T \|\nabla f_t(x_t)-m_t\|^2.$ We first establish a negative result: under the single-point feedback protocol, an unavoidable $Ω(\sqrt{T})$ regret lower bound persists even when $S_T=o(T)$, showing that the variance of gradient estimation fundamentally obscures the benefit of accurate predictions. To overcome this barrier, we propose \emph{Two-Point Variance-Reduced Optimistic Gradient Descent} (TP-VR-OPT) for the two-point feedback setting. The key idea is a novel variance-reduced gradient estimator whose variance scales with the prediction error rather than the gradient norm. This yields a regret bound of $O\big(\sqrt{d\,\mathbb{E}[S_T]}\big),$ where $d$ is the decision dimension. Complementing this result, we establish an information-theoretic lower bound that scales as $Ω(\sqrt{\mathbb{E}[S_T]})$, providing a fundamental characterization of the best achievable prediction-adaptive regret and showing that TP-VR-OPT is optimal up to a factor of $\sqrt d$. We further develop adaptive variants that eliminate the need for prior knowledge of $\mathbb{E}[S_T]$ or the horizon $T$, and extend our framework to non-stationary environments, establishing dynamic regret guarantees that adapt simultaneously to the cumulative prediction error and the comparator path length.

</details>


### [125] [Reinforced Graph of Thoughts: RL-Driven Adaptive Prompting for LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22195)
*Manuel Noah Riesen,Peter Alfred von Niederhäusern*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出强化思维图（RGoT），利用强化学习来自动生成操作图，替代手动定义，提高适应性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 原始思维图（GoT）的操作图需要手动定义，依赖于对问题解决方案的深度理解，且静态缺乏适应性。

Method: 使用强化学习（RL）从人类定义的集合中自适应地生成操作图，实现自动化构建。

Result: 在特定约束条件下，能够根据任务复杂度自动构建适配的操作图。

Conclusion: RGoT通过RL实现了GoT提示范式的自动化，提升了操作图对任务复杂度的适应性。

Abstract: Graph of Thoughts (GoT), a generalized form of recent prompting paradigms for large language models (LLMs), has been shown to be useful for elaborate problem solving. By executing a graph of operations, thoughts of the LLM are structured as an arbitrary graph, forming the actual graph of thoughts. Originally, the graph of operations is defined manually, which requires in-depth knowledge about the solution of the problem to solve. Such a static graph of operations is rigid and therefore lacks adaptability. We propose Reinforced Graph of Thoughts (RGoT), an automated approach to the GoT prompting paradigm that leverages reinforcement learning (RL) to adaptively generate a graph of operations from a human-defined set. Results indicate that, under certain constraints, it is possible to construct graphs of operations adaptively to the task's complexity in an automated way.

</details>


### [126] [Survive or Collapse: The Asymmetric Roles of Data Gating and Reward Grounding in Self-Play RL](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22217)
*Sophia Xiao Pu,Zhaotian Weng,Chengzhi Liu,Jayanth Srinivasa,Gaowen Liu,William Yang Wang,Xin Eric Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过受控实验揭示了自对弈强化学习中，数据级门控（决定哪些生成任务进入训练池）是实现稳定性的关键约束，而奖励信号的影响远小于前者。研究还发现了"接地提议者悖论"：拥有真实标签访问权限的提议者反而会加速崩溃。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自对弈强化学习虽然能提升推理能力，但普遍存在崩溃和不稳定问题，现有研究主要将其归因于奖励设计缺陷。作者认为需要重新审视稳定性背后的真正机制。

Method: 在Python输出预测任务和确定性DSL孪生任务上进行了受控实验，分别操控数据级门控（提议者生成任务的准入机制）和奖励信号（自一致性奖励等），观察两者对稳定性的影响。

Result: 严格的门控机制在所有奖励变体下都足以保证稳定性；一旦移除门控，任何奖励设计都无法维持稳定。门控与奖励存在不对称性。此外发现接地提议者（有真实标签）与自一致性求解器组合时，反而加速崩溃。

Conclusion: 数据级门控是自对弈稳定性的核心约束，而非奖励校准。通过设置连续严格参数ε，观察到训练指标和验证准确率存在两阶段相变，进一步证实了门控的主导作用。

Abstract: Self-play reinforcement learning trains language models on their own generated tasks, co-evolving a proposer and solver without human labels. Recent systems report strong reasoning gains, but collapse and instability are widely observed and poorly understood. The dominant response treats this as a reward-design problem. We argue instead that self-play stability is governed by two distinct levers: a data-level gate that decides which proposer-generated tasks enter the training pool, and the reward signal that updates the policy on tasks already admitted. Through controlled experiments on a Python output-prediction task and a deterministic-DSL twin task that strips pretraining priors, output ambiguity, and executor noise, we find the two levers are asymmetric. A strict gate is sufficient for stability under every reward variant we test, including a self-consistency reward with no access to ground truth; while no reward variant is sufficient once the gate is removed. This asymmetry exposes a counter-intuitive coupling we call the Grounded Proposer Paradox: a proposer with ground-truth access accelerates collapse faster than an ungrounded one when paired with a self-consistency solver, by concentrating training on clean tasks that form the fastest path to a spurious self-consistent attractor. Replacing the binary gate with a continuous strictness parameter $\varepsilon$ further reveals a two-stage phase transition: training-side metrics decouple at low $\varepsilon$, while validation accuracy holds until $\varepsilon$ is much higher. Data-level gating, not reward calibration, is the binding constraint on self-play stability.

</details>


### [127] [Can Transformers Learn to Verify During Backtracking Search?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22221)
*Yin Jun Phua,Tony Ribeiro,Tuan Nguyen,Katsumi Inoue*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文指出，在自回归解码器型Transformer中，基于累积轨迹训练得到的"继续或回溯"预测器存在两个根本性问题：分散检索（scattered retrieval）和历史纠缠（history entanglement）。对此，作者提出了局部化（localization）和选择性状态注意力（SSA）两种结构级修复方法，在3-SAT、图着色、积木世界和回溯解析等任务上验证了其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于Transformer的推理系统在回溯搜索中，模型输入为所有历史决策的累积轨迹，但最优的继续/回溯决策应仅取决于当前搜索状态，而非整个历史路径。研究发现，标准因果解码器在累积轨迹上训练时，无法满足这一要求，存在状态特征被分散到多个位置（分散检索）以及模型会依据轨迹而非状态做决策（历史纠缠）的问题。

Method: 1) 局部化（localization）：在轨迹级别重写每个决策块，使状态特征在局部位置暴露，解决分散检索问题。2) 选择性状态注意力（SSA）：设计固定的注意力掩码，在不修改训练数据、目标函数或参数的前提下，从结构上强制模型基于状态而非历史轨迹做决策。实验聚焦于传播已暴露矛盾后的反应式验证场景。

Result: 在3-SAT、图着色、积木世界和回溯解析任务上，对于仅历史不同而状态相同的样本对，SSA能输出完全一致的决策，而基于累积轨迹训练的因果基线模型则输出不同决策。

Conclusion: 本文诊断了Transformer在序列化轨迹数据上的行为缺陷，并提供了结构性修复方案。分析还指出，对自身推理步骤进行搜索的预训练语言模型可能面临相同问题，而推理时上下文清除（context clearing）是无需重新训练即可实现类似隔离的候选方法。

Abstract: Backtracking search underlies classical constraint solvers, planners, and theorem provers. Recent transformer-based reasoning systems explore search trees over their own intermediate steps. A common training recipe fits an autoregressive next-token loss on offline solver traces. The model's input at each step is a cumulative trace of all prior decisions. The optimal continue-or-backtrack predictor depends only on the current search state, since two trajectories reaching the same state admit the same viable continuations. We show that decoder-only transformers trained on cumulative traces fail this requirement in two ways: the trace can scatter state features across many positions (scattered retrieval), and the predictor can condition on the trajectory rather than the state (history entanglement). We address scattered retrieval with localization, a trace-level fix that rewrites each decision block to expose state features locally. We address history entanglement with Selective State Attention (SSA), a fixed attention mask that enforces state-based decisions structurally without modifying training data, objective, or parameters. We focus on reactive verification, after propagation has exposed a contradiction. We test SSA on 3-SAT, graph coloring, Blocks World, and backtracking parsing. On same-state pairs that differ only in prior history, SSA emits identical decisions while a cumulative-trained causal baseline does not. Our contribution is a diagnostic of transformer behavior on serialized trajectory data, paired with a structural fix. Pretrained language models that search over their own reasoning steps may face the same failure. Our analysis opens up inference-time context clearing as a candidate way to apply the same isolation without retraining.

</details>


### [128] [How Many Different Outputs Can a Transformer Generate?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22223)
*Maxime Meyer,Mario Michelessa,Caroline Chaux,Vincent Y. F. Tan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了仅利用Transformer架构的少量特征即可定性和定量预测其能输出的不同序列数量。理论分析和实验表明，可访问序列的最大长度随提示长度线性增长，超过临界阈值后可访问序列比例呈指数衰减，且线性系数存在理论上界，即使上下文和计算时间无界时这些结论仍成立。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解Transformer表达能力的基本限制，解释其在简单序列任务（如复制和填充）上经验性失败的原因。

Method: 通过理论分析给出上界，并在多种架构和模型规模下进行实证验证，证明上界紧度（误差小于10倍）。

Result: (i) 可访问序列最大长度与提示长度线性增长；(ii) 超临界阈值后可访问序列比例指数衰减；(iii) 线性系数存在理论上界。

Conclusion: 即使模型拥有无限上下文和计算时间，Transformer能生成的序列多样性仍受限于其架构特征，揭示了其表达能力的根本局限。

Abstract: We study how we can leverage only a handful of characteristics of a transformer's architecture to closely predict the number of different sequences it can output, both qualitatively and quantitatively. We provide an upper bound depending on the length of the prompt, which we show empirically to be tight up to a factor less than 10, across architectures and model sizes. Our analysis also provides a theoretical explanation for previously observed empirical failures of transformers on simple sequence tasks, such as copying and cramming. Formally, we prove that (i) the maximal length of accessible sequences (those that the transformer can output for some prompt) grows linearly with the prompt length, (ii) beyond a critical threshold, the proportion of accessible sequences decays exponentially with sequence length, and (iii) the linear coefficient relating prompt length to accessible sequence length admits a theoretical upper bound. Notably, these results hold even with unbounded context and computation time.

</details>


### [129] [Decomposing Ensemble Spread in Lorenz '96 With Learned Stochastic Parameterizations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22242)
*Birgit Kühbacher,Daan Crommelin,Niki Kilbertus*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文使用两尺度Lorenz 1996系统作为受控测试平台，系统研究了集合预报中不同不确定性来源（内在变率、初始条件扰动和随机模型不确定性）的相互作用，发现集合扰动不增加系统长期方差，而是调节轨迹去相关速度；具有时间持久结构的随机参数化方案能更好地提升早期散布增长和散布-误差一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 天气和气候集合预报常存在欠分散问题（散布增长过慢），需要系统理解不同不确定性来源如何相互作用，以改进随机参数化方案的设计。

Method: 以两尺度Lorenz 1996系统为受控测试平台，系统比较了多种集合配置和参数化策略，包括现有确定性方法、自回归方法以及新颖的贝叶斯和基于流的参数化方法。

Result: (1) 集合扰动不增加系统长期方差，而是调节轨迹去相关速度和不变测度的探索；(2) 具有时间持久结构的随机参数化能增强早期散布增长；(3) 不同随机参数化方案在散布-误差一致性上表现不同。

Conclusion: 厘清了混沌系统中不同不确定性来源的相互作用机制，为天气和气候模型中随机参数化的设计和评估提供了指导。

Abstract: Weather and climate forecasts are inherently uncertain due to chaotic dynamics, imperfect initial conditions, and incomplete representation of the underlying physical processes. Operational ensemble forecasts aim to represent these uncertainties through forecast spread, yet many approaches yield underdispersive estimates, with spread that grows too slowly relative to forecast error. Using the two-scale Lorenz 1996 system as a widely used, controlled testbed, we design a systematic approach to disentangle intrinsic variability, initial-condition perturbations, and stochastic model uncertainty. We compare multiple ensemble configurations and parameterization strategies, including existing deterministic and autoregressive as well as novel Bayesian and flow-based approaches. Our results show that ensemble perturbations do not increase the system's long-term variance; rather, they regulate how rapidly trajectories decorrelate and explore the invariant measure. Stochastic parameterizations, particularly those with temporally persistent structure, enhance early spread growth and improve spread-error consistency. Overall, we bring clarity to how different sources of uncertainty interact in a chaotic system and provide guidance for the design and evaluation of stochastic parameterizations in weather and climate models.

</details>


### [130] [Explainable AI for Data-Driven Design of High-Dimensional Predictive Studies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22243)
*Junyu Yan,Damian Machlanski,Kurt Butler,Panagiotis Dimitrakopoulos,Ewen M Harrison,Bruce Guthrie,Sotirios A Tsaftaris*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种探索性AI推荐框架，通过可解释AI技术为可解释统计模型（如Cox比例风险模型）提供特征排除、非线性项和特征交互三种类型的数据驱动建议，以提升高维透明预测模型的性能。在245,614名患者的跌倒风险预测任务中，C-index从0.805提升至0.815，校准度也得到改善，且所有建议均获文献支持。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高维健康数据的预测建模中，手动选择特征、变换和交互项设计困难；复杂机器学习模型虽性能高但缺乏透明性和可解释性，限制了临床信任与决策应用。因此需要一种既能提升统计模型性能、又保持可解释性的数据驱动方法。

Method: 开发了一个探索性AI推荐框架，使用灵活的AI模型捕获复杂数据模式，再通过可解释AI技术将模式转化为三种推荐类型：特征排除、非线性项和特征交互。将这些推荐应用于基础Cox比例风险模型（无交互项或非线性项）构成增强模型，在三个数据集上进行评估。

Result: 在245,614名患者的主要分析中，模型推荐排除23个特征，为2个特征引入非线性项，并包含221个特征交互。C-index从0.805提升至0.815，校准截距从-0.006改善至0.003，斜率从1.063改善至0.950。所有推荐均获现有文献支持，并在两个额外公开数据集上验证了方法的广泛适用性。

Conclusion: 该探索性AI推荐框架展示了可解释AI和数据驱动研究设计在改进高维透明预测模型开发过程和性能方面的潜力，能够在不牺牲可解释性的前提下提升统计模型的预测能力。

Abstract: Predictive modelling is important for health data analysis and data-driven clinical decision-making. However, predictive studies are challenging to design optimally by hand when tens or even hundreds of features require selection, transformation, or interaction modelling. While complex machine learning models offer high performance, their "black-box" nature limits the clinical trust, transparency, and interpretability required for decision-making. We developed and evaluated an Exploratory AI Recommender that provides data-driven recommendations to improve predictive performance of existing interpretable statistical models. The developed framework uses flexible AI modelling to capture complex data patterns and explainable AI techniques to translate the patterns into three recommendation types: feature exclusion, non-linear terms, and feature interactions. We evaluated the framework by comparing predictive performance of a baseline (i.e., no interactions or non-linear terms) Cox Proportional Hazards (CPH) model against an augmented CPH incorporating recommendations suggested by our method. The primary analysis predicts the time to the first occurrence of a fall or related injury in 245,614 patients. Our method recommended excluding 23 features, including non-linear terms for two features, and including 221 suggested feature interactions. The C-index improved from 0.805 (95% CI 0.798-0.812) to 0.815 (95% CI 0.809-0.822), and so did calibration (intercept: -0.006 to 0.003; slope: 1.063 to 0.950). All recommendations were supported by existing literature. The method also proved effective on two additional public datasets, demonstrating wider applicability. The proposed Exploratory AI Recommender demonstrates the potential of explainable AI and data-driven study design to improve the process of developing, and the performance of high-dimensional transparent predictive models.

</details>


### [131] [What are the Right Symmetries for Formal Theorem Proving?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22257)
*Krzysztof Olejniczak,Radoslav Dimitrov,Xingyue Huang,Bernardo Cuenca Grau,Jinwoo Kim,İsmail İlkan Ceylan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出用范畴论中的重写范畴来形式化形式定理证明中的对称性概念，发现当前基于LLM的定理证明器缺乏对称性归纳偏置，并通过测试时聚合等价重写的方法来提升鲁棒性和性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM-based形式定理证明器对问题表示的表面变化高度敏感，语义等价的语句可能表现出截然不同的证明成功率，这反映了模型未能尊重形式数学中固有的结构对称性。因此需要明确形式定理证明中"正确的对称性"是什么。

Method: 1) 引入重写范畴（rewriting categories）这一范畴论框架，形式化刻画证明策略（tactics）诱导的组合性、通常不可逆的变换；2) 定义两种对称性概念：证明等变性（proof equivariance）和成功不变性（success invariance）；3) 提出测试时聚合方法，通过对输入的等价重写进行聚合来恢复成功不变性。

Result: 理论上证明测试时聚合方法在采样极限下能恢复成功不变性；实验上表明该方法在固定推理预算下能提升鲁棒性和性能。同时发现基于状态的下一步策略证明器自然满足证明等变性，而当前SOTA的LLM-based证明器两种对称性均不满足。

Conclusion: 对称性是LLM-based定理证明中缺失的关键归纳偏置，测试时计算是逼近该偏置的实用途径。

Abstract: Formal theorem provers based on large language models (LLMs) are highly sensitive to superficial variations in problem representation: semantically equivalent statements can exhibit drastically different proof success rates, revealing a failure to respect structural symmetries inherent in formal mathematics. This raises a central question: what are the right symmetries for formal theorem proving? We introduce rewriting categories, a category-theoretic framework capturing the compositional, generally non-invertible transformations induced by proof tactics, and use it to formalize two symmetry notions: proof equivariance, governing how proof distributions transform under rewrites, and success invariance (i.e., invariance of success probability), requiring equivalent statements to be solved with the same probability. We observe that state-based next-tactic provers naturally satisfy proof equivariance by operating on proof states. In contrast, state-of-the-art LLM-based provers satisfy neither property, exhibiting large performance variation across equivalent formulations. To mitigate this, we propose test-time methods that aggregate over equivalent rewritings of the input, showing theoretically that they recover success invariance in the sampling limit, and empirically, that they improve robustness and performance under fixed inference budgets. Our results highlight symmetry as a key missing inductive bias in LLM-based theorem proving and suggest test-time computation as a practical route to approximate it.

</details>


### [132] [Tailoring Teaching to Aptitude: Direction-Adaptive Self-Distillation for LLM Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22263)
*Hongbin Zhang,Chaozheng Wang,Kehai Chen,Youcheng Pan,Yang Xiang,Jinpeng Wang,Min Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出方向自适应自蒸馏（DASD），通过根据token熵值自适应调整教师监督方向（高熵推开以保留探索，低熵拉近以稳定执行），解决同策略自蒸馏在复杂推理任务中抑制预测不确定性的问题，在数学推理基准上取得最佳性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 同策略自蒸馏（OPSD）作为新兴的LLM后训练范式，在复杂推理任务中会因抑制预测不确定性而导致性能退化。现有方法对所有token施加统一的教师监督方向，未能区分不同不确定性水平的token，从而同时损害了探索能力和步骤执行准确性。

Method: 提出方向自适应自蒸馏（DASD），将传统的统一教师模仿重构为基于熵值的路由式方向监督：对高熵token推离特权教师以保留探索能力，对低熵token拉近教师以稳定步骤级执行。通过熵值自适应地调整每个token的蒸馏方向。

Result: 在六个数学推理基准上，DASD在宏平均Avg@16指标上超越了强RLVR和自蒸馏基线。Pass@k、推理健康度和泛化分析表明，性能提升源于在不牺牲步骤执行质量的前提下保留了探索能力。

Conclusion: DASD通过熵感知的方向自适应策略，有效解决了同策略自蒸馏中探索与利用的权衡问题，验证了差异化token级监督对复杂推理任务的重要性。

Abstract: On-policy self-distillation (OPSD) is an emerging LLM post-training paradigm in which the model serves as its own teacher: conditioned on privileged information such as a reference trace or hint, the same policy provides dense token-level supervision on its own rollouts. However, recent studies show that OPSD degrades complex reasoning by suppressing predictive uncertainty, which supports exploration and hypothesis revision. Our token-level analysis shows that this failure arises from applying a uniform direction of teacher supervision across tokens with different uncertainty levels: conformity to the privileged self-teacher suppresses exploration at high entropy, while deviation from the teacher degrades step accuracy at low entropy. Accordingly, we propose \textbf{Direction-Adaptive Self-Distillation} (\textbf{DASD}), which reframes privileged self-distillation from uniform teacher imitation into entropy-routed directional supervision: high-entropy tokens are pushed away from the privileged teacher to preserve exploration, while low-entropy tokens are pulled toward the teacher to stabilize step-level execution. Across six mathematical reasoning benchmarks, DASD achieves the best macro Avg@16 over strong RLVR and self-distillation baselines. Pass@$k$, reasoning-health, and generalization analyses show that these average gains come from preserving exploration without sacrificing step-level execution.

</details>


### [133] [Detecting Atypical Clients in Federated Learning via Representation-Level Divergence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22266)
*Cristian Pérez-Corral,Jose I. Mestre,Alberto Fernández-Hernández,Manuel F. Dolz,Enrique S. Quitana-Ortí*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种轻量级几何信号度量方法，通过在共享探针集上评估客户端局部训练如何改变激活诱导的输入空间划分，来量化客户端与全局模型的功能偏差，从而区分稳定异构更新与异常发散更新，实现风险感知的联邦学习聚合。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 联邦学习中客户端数据异构（不仅包括良性非独立同分布，还包括分布偏移或异常输入）导致聚合过程不可靠，现有方法难以区分良性异构与异常发散。

Method: 提出一种置换不变的、可解释的度量指标，通过比较客户端局部训练前后在共享探针集上激活模式对输入空间的划分变化，量化客户端与全局模型的功能偏差，而非直接比较参数或梯度。

Result: 该信号能有效识别引发异常功能变化的客户端，区分稳定异构客户端与显著偏离全局状态的客户端。

Conclusion: 该度量可作为联邦学习系统中监测客户端行为、实现风险感知聚合策略的简单工具。

Abstract: Federated learning enables collaborative training across distributed clients with heterogeneous data, but such heterogeneity often leads to unstable updates and degraded global performance. Moreover, in practical deployments, client updates may deviate from the expected behavior not only due to benign not i.i.d. distributions, but also due to distributional shifts or anomalous inputs, raising concerns about the reliability of the aggregation process. In this work, we propose a lightweight geometric signal to quantify the functional deviation of a client with respect to the global model. Instead of comparing model parameters or gradients, our approach measures how the local training of each client alters the activation-induced partition of the input space, evaluated on a shared probe set. This yields a permutation-invariant, interpretable metric of client--global divergence that captures differences in how data is processed by the model. We show that this signal effectively identifies clients that induce atypical functional changes, distinguishing stable yet heterogeneous clients from those whose updates significantly diverge from the global regime. As a result, the proposed metric provides a simple tool for monitoring client behavior and enabling risk-aware aggregation strategies in federated learning systems.

</details>


### [134] [Adaptive Measurement Allocation for Learning Kernelized SVMs Under Noisy Observations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22275)
*Artur Miroszewski*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种自适应测量分配策略，用于从含噪伯努利观测中学习核化支持向量机。该策略结合几何敏感性与主动集不稳定性，将有限测量预算集中于对决策最关键的核矩阵区域。理论分析表明自适应分配的优势受核重要性结构异质性调控，实验证明其在支持向量恢复、边际估计和决策函数精度上显著优于均匀分配。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有核方法通常假设核矩阵可精确获取，但在量子机器学习等新兴场景中，每个核条目只能通过含噪观测推断，且精度取决于有限测量预算的分配方式。然而现有方法几乎全部采用均匀分配，忽略了核化分类器对核矩阵的高度非均匀依赖性。

Method: 提出一种自适应测量分配策略，结合两种互补原则：(i) 几何敏感性——捕获单个核条目扰动对分类器边际的影响；(ii) 主动集不稳定性——量化测量噪声导致的支持向量隶属离散变化的概率。这些信号定义了任务感知的分配方案，将测量集中于核矩阵中最关键的决策区域。

Result: 理论分析表明自适应分配的优势受核重要性结构异质性调控，存在自适应或均匀策略更优的不同机制。合成数据集实验证明自适应分配显著提升支持向量恢复、边际估计和决策函数精度。双系数稳定性准则实现早停，仅用部分测量成本即可达到近最优性能。量子核实验呈现与核浓缩现象一致的机制依赖性行为。

Conclusion: 自适应测量分配策略能有效提升含噪核化SVM的学习效率，其优势源于对核矩阵非均匀重要性的利用，在固定测量预算下显著优于传统均匀分配方法。

Abstract: Kernel methods are typically formulated under the assumption of exact, noise-free access to the Gram matrix. However, in emerging settings such as quantum machine learning, each kernel entry must be inferred from noisy observations, and its accuracy depends on how a limited measurement budget is allocated. Despite this, existing approaches overwhelmingly rely on uniform allocation, which equalizes estimator variance but ignores the highly non-uniform dependence of kernelized classifiers on the Gram matrix. In this work, we introduce an adaptive measurement-allocation strategy for learning kernelized Support Vector Machines (SVMs) from noisy Bernoulli observations. Our approach combines two complementary principles: (i) geometric sensitivity, capturing how perturbations of individual kernel entries affect the classifier margin, and (ii) active-set instability, quantifying the probability of discrete changes in support-vector membership induced by measurement noise. These signals define a task-aware allocation scheme that concentrates measurements on the most decision-critical regions of the kernel matrix. We provide a theoretical analysis showing that the benefit of adaptive allocation is governed by the heterogeneity of the induced kernel importance structure, leading to distinct regimes in which adaptive or uniform strategies are preferable. Empirical evaluations on synthetic datasets demonstrate that adaptive allocation significantly improves support-vector recovery, margin estimation, and decision-function accuracy under fixed measurement budgets. A dual-coefficient stability criterion further enables early stopping, achieving near-optimal performance while using only a fraction of the measurement cost. Additional experiments on quantum kernels derived from real-world data reveal a regime-dependent behavior aligned with known phenomena such as kernel concentration. Together...

</details>


### [135] [EmoTrack: Robust Depression Tracking from Counseling Transcripts across Session Regimes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22286)
*Zhaomin Wu,Jiayi Li,Bingsheng He*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出LongCounsel多会话咨询数据集和EmoTrack抑郁跟踪框架，通过结合LLM临床信号与冻结语义嵌入，在单会话和多会话场景下实现稳健的PHQ-8预测，在DAIC-WOZ基准上MAE相对降低13.5%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于文本的心理咨询是AI心理健康支持的重要接口，但现有方法在单会话和多会话场景下存在泛化性与数据效率的权衡：微调方法在数据稀缺时泛化差，而基于提示的LLM方法缺乏对纵向上下文的支持。因此需要一种既能利用LLM数据效率又能处理跨会话连续性的稳健抑郁跟踪方法。

Method: 提出EmoTrack框架，包含两个核心组件：(1) 利用LLM提取临床信号并结合冻结的逐轮语义嵌入，构建会话级转录表示；(2) 在该表示上训练症状特定的预测器。对于多会话场景，通过紧凑的跨会话记忆模块整合历史会话信息。同时引入LongCounsel数据集，提供会话级别的PHQ-8标注，支持重复会话跟踪评估。

Result: 在DAIC-WOZ单会话基准上，EmoTrack相比最强基线实现了13.5%的相对MAE降低；在LongCounsel多会话数据上，与最强纵向基线相比具有竞争力。

Conclusion: 结合LLM提取的临床信号与冻结语义嵌入的混合方法，能够有效平衡数据效率与泛化性，为跨会话抑郁跟踪提供实用解决方案。

Abstract: Text-based counseling is an important interface for AI mental-health support, where transcripts may be used to monitor depression severity and flag sessions requiring timely human review. However, robust PHQ-8 prediction across session regimes remains challenging: fine-tuning-based methods can exploit richer supervision but may generalize poorly under data scarcity, while prompt-based LLM methods are data-efficient but usually treat each transcript holistically and provide limited support for longitudinal context. We study robust depression tracking from counseling transcripts across single-session and multi-session regimes. We introduce LongCounsel, a multi-session counseling dataset with session-level PHQ-8 supervision for evaluating repeated-session tracking under partial symptom disclosure and cross-session continuity. We further propose EmoTrack, a PHQ-8 prediction framework that combines LLM-extracted clinical signals with frozen turn-level semantic embeddings and trains symptom-specific predictors over the resulting transcript representation. When prior sessions are available, EmoTrack can further incorporate them through compact cross-session memory. Experiments on LongCounsel and DAIC-WOZ show that EmoTrack achieves a clear gain on the real single-session benchmark, including a 13.5% relative MAE reduction over the strongest DAIC-WOZ baseline, and remains competitive with the strongest longitudinal baseline on LongCounsel.

</details>


### [136] [One LR Doesn't Fit All: Heavy-Tail Guided Layerwise Learning Rates for LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22297)
*Di He,Songjun Tu,Keyu Wang,Lu Yin,Shiwei Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Layerwise Learning Rate (LLR)，一种基于Heavy-Tailed Self-Regularization (HT-SR)理论的自适应逐层学习率分配方法。该方法根据权重相关矩阵的经验谱密度（ESD）量化各层重尾性，对弱重尾层分配较大学习率以加速训练，对强重尾层分配较小学习率，从而实现平衡训练、加速收敛和提升泛化。实验表明，LLR在多种架构、优化器和参数规模下实现最高1.5倍训练加速，并将平均零样本准确率从47.09%提升至49.02%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前深度学习主流实践对所有层使用统一学习率，忽视了Transformer各层之间的结构异质性，这可能限制了大型语言模型（LLMs）的表现力。

Method: 基于HT-SR理论，通过分析权重相关矩阵的ESD量化各层重尾性，据此为每层分配差异化学习率：弱重尾层赋大学习率、强重尾层赋小学习率。

Result: 在LLaMA、GPT-nano等多种架构及AdamW、Muon等优化器下，60M-1B参数规模实验中实现最高1.5倍训练加速，平均零样本准确率从47.09%提升至49.02%，且调参开销低，可从统一基线直接迁移最优学习率设置。

Conclusion: LLR作为一种低开销、高效的自适应逐层学习率策略，能有效促进Transformer各层平衡训练，加速收敛并提升泛化性能。

Abstract: Learning rate configuration is a fundamental aspect of modern deep learning. The prevailing practice of applying a uniform learning rate across all layers overlooks the structural heterogeneity of Transformers, potentially limiting their effectiveness as the backbone of Large Language Models (LLMs). In this paper, we introduce Layerwise Learning Rate (LLR), an adaptive scheme that assigns distinct learning rates to individual Transformer layers. Our method is grounded in Heavy-Tailed Self-Regularization (HT-SR) theory, which characterizes the empirical spectral density (ESD) of weight correlation matrices to quantify heavy-tailedness. Layers with weaker heavy-tailedness are assigned larger learning rates to accelerate their training, while layers with stronger heavy-tailedness receive smaller learning rates. By tailoring learning rates in this manner, LLR promotes balanced training across layers, leading to faster convergence and improved generalization. Extensive experiments across architectures (from LLaMA to GPT-nano), optimizers (AdamW and Muon), and parameter scales (60M-1B) demonstrate that LLR achieves up to 1.5x training speedup and outperforms baselines, notably raising average zero-shot accuracy from 47.09% to 49.02%. A key advantage of LLR is its low tuning overhead: it transfers nearly optimal LR settings directly from the uniform baseline. Code is available at https://github.com/hed-ucas/Layer-wise-Learning-Rate.

</details>


### [137] [SepsisAI Orchestrator: A Containerized and Scalable Platform for Deploying AI Models and Real-Time Monitoring in Early Sepsis Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22331)
*Santiago Ospitia,John Sanabria,John Garcia-Henao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出SepsisAI-Orchestrator，一个开源模块化平台，用于弥合临床机器学习模型（早期脓毒症检测）与床旁部署之间的差距。平台集成HL7 FHIR-inspired CDA预处理、NoSQL存储、容器化LightGBM分类器（REST API）及Streamlit仪表盘，通过Docker/Kubernetes编排。贡献在于基础设施而非模型本身。实验发现，副本数需匹配物理CPU线程数（12线程CPU上，3→12副本使p95延迟从3.3s降至1.41s，消除失败请求；过度扩展至24/48副本因调度竞争导致性能下降），首次量化了临床AI推理工作负载中的U型扩展行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 临床机器学习模型虽有强预测性能，但因异构数据表示、缺乏标准化部署工作流、以及研究原型与医院环境并发/延迟需求不匹配等系统性障碍，模型向床旁应用的转化受限。本文旨在解决这一部署鸿沟。

Method: 构建SepsisAI-Orchestrator平台：HL7 FHIR-inspired CDA预处理模块、NoSQL存储、容器化LightGBM分类器（通过REST API提供服务）、Streamlit临床仪表盘，使用Docker和Kubernetes进行编排。复用已有LightGBM模型（PhysioNet 2019上F1 0.87-0.94），使用k6工具模拟50-1000个并发虚拟用户进行负载测试。

Result: 副本数必须匹配宿主物理CPU线程数：在12线程CPU上，从3个副本扩展到12个副本，p95延迟从3.3s降至1.41s（减少57.3%），所有请求失败被消除；过度扩展到24或48个副本时，由于调度器竞争导致性能下降。首次量化了临床AI推理工作负载中的U型扩展行为。

Conclusion: 平台有效弥合了临床AI模型的部署鸿沟，但未进行前瞻性临床验证。副本数需谨慎配置以避免过度扩展导致的性能退化。代码和部署清单已开源。

Abstract: Despite strong predictive results in the clinical machine learning literature, the translation of these models into bedside use remains limited by systems-level barriers: heterogeneous data representations, the absence of standardized deployment workflows, and a mismatch between research prototypes and the concurrency and latency requirements of hospital environments. We present the SepsisAI-Orchestrator, an open-source modular platform that addresses this deployment gap for early sepsis detection. The platform integrates HL7 FHIR-inspired Clinical Document Architecture (CDA) preprocessing, NoSQL storage, a containerized LightGBM classifier served via REST APIs, and a Streamlit clinical dashboard, orchestrated with Docker and Kubernetes. A previously validated LightGBM model (F1 0.87-0.94 on PhysioNet 2019) is reused without modification; the contribution lies in the surrounding infrastructure and its empirical characterization under load. Using k6 with 50-1000 concurrent virtual users, we find that replica count must be matched to the physical CPU thread count of the host: scaling from 3 to 12 replicas on a 12-thread CPU reduces p95 latency from 3.3s to 1.41s (57.3% reduction) and eliminates all request failures, while over-provisioning to 24 or 48 replicas degrades performance due to scheduler contention. To our knowledge this U-shaped scaling behavior has not been quantified previously for clinical AI inference workloads. We do not claim prospective clinical validation. Source code and deployment manifests are available at https://github.com/nucleusai/sepsisai-orchestrator.

</details>


### [138] [Riemannian geometry meets fMRI: the advantages of modeling correlation manifolds and eigenvector subspaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22334)
*Mario Severino,Manuela Moretto,Robert A. McCutcheon,Mattia Veronese*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种可扩展的几何框架，包含Off-log度量（将相关矩阵平滑映射到对称零对角矩阵，实现闭合形式的距离、Fréchet均值和线性模型）和Grassmannian子空间判别法（通过主角度距离比较特征向量子空间），在帕金森病、精神病和三个老龄化fMRI数据集上验证，提高了统计敏感性和分类性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准的相关矩阵分析忽略其弯曲几何结构，现有几何方法缺乏闭合形式操作或依赖任意区域排序，限制了可扩展性和实用性。

Method: 提出Off-log度量进行相关矩阵的平滑变换，以及Grassmannian子空间判别法解决特征向量的符号和基歧义，两者均可集成到标准机器学习流程中。

Result: Off-log度量在置换检验中提高了敏感性，分类性能匹配或超越黎曼和欧几里得基线；Grassmannian方法始终优于欧几里得基线；脑龄预测性能与基线相当。

Conclusion: 几何感知的表示方法能提高敏感性和预测性能，同时易于大规模部署。

Abstract: Correlation matrices are fundamental summaries of functional brain networks, yet standard analyses often treat entries independently, ignoring the curved geometry of correlation space. Existing geometric methods frequently lack closed-form operations or depend on arbitrary region ordering, limiting scalability. We introduce a scalable geometric framework with two components: (i) the Off-log metric, a smooth transformation mapping correlation matrices to symmetric zero-diagonal matrices. This enables closed-form expressions for distances, Frechet means, and linear models, allowing standard statistical modeling without complex manifold optimization. (ii) Grassmannian subspace discrimination, which compares subjects via principal-angle distances between eigenvector subspaces, resolving inherent sign and basis ambiguities. Both components integrate into standard machine-learning workflows for inference, regression, and classification. Validated across two clinical cohorts (Parkinson's and psychosis) and three ageing fMRI datasets, the Off-log metric increased sensitivity in permutation tests and matched or exceeded Riemannian and Euclidean baselines in classification. Brain-age prediction performance was comparable, with Riemannian metrics excelling in two of three cohorts. The Grassmannian method consistently outperformed Euclidean baselines, highlighting disease-relevant networks. Overall, geometry-aware representations improve sensitivity and predictive performance while remaining straightforward to deploy at scale.

</details>


### [139] [Learning Causal Orderings for In-Context Tabular Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22335)
*Sascha Xu,Sarah Mameche,Jilles Vreeken*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出TabOrder模型，通过因果顺序约束注意力机制，同时推断和执行表格数据中的因果结构（拓扑变量排序），以提升分布偏移和干预情况下的预测鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的表格数据上下文学习主要依赖相关性结构，在分布偏移或干预下不可靠；而因果发现方法虽能识别因果结构，却常与预测架构脱节。因此需要一种能同时进行因果结构推断和预测的方法。

Method: TabOrder采用因果顺序约束的注意力机制，仅基于因果顺序中目标变量之前的特征进行预测；通过无监督的似然目标学习最优变量排序，并研究数据缺失与因果方向识别的相互作用。

Result: 在标准函数模型类别下验证了方法合理性；实验证明TabOrder能恢复准确的变量排序，并有效完成预测和缺失值填充任务；在真实生物数据干预下提供了有意义的洞察。

Conclusion: 将因果结构发现与预测架构结合是可行的，因果顺序约束能提升表格预测在分布偏移下的鲁棒性，且缺失数据与因果方向识别之间存在重要交互。

Abstract: In-context learning for tabular data sets strong predictive standards in observational settings; it however primarily relies on correlational structure, which becomes unreliable under distribution shift or intervention. While established methods to discover causal structure exist, they are often focused on structure identifiability and decoupled from the predictive architectures that could benefit from them.
  To bridge these perspectives, we study how to simultaneously infer and enforce causal structure in the form of topological variable orderings into tabular prediction. Unlike standard architectures, our model TabOrder uses causal order-constrained attention, basing predictions only on features that precede a target under a learned causal order. Similar to causal discovery methods, TabOrder learns the optimal variable ordering in an unsupervised manner through a likelihood-based objective. We justify this choice under standard functional model classes and also study how sample missingness, a common challenge in tabular data, interacts with causal direction identification. Empirically, we confirm that TabOrder recovers accurate variable orderings while addressing prediction and imputation tasks, as well as gives insight into real-world biological data under intervention.

</details>


### [140] [From Snapshots to Trajectories: Learning Single-Cell Gene Expression Dynamics via Conditional Flow Matching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22340)
*Siyu Pu,Qingqing Long,Xiaohan Huang,Haotian Chen,Jiajia Wang,Meng Xiao,Xiao Luo,Hengshu Zhu,Yuanchun Zhou,Xuezhi Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出scFM框架，结合最优传输耦合与流匹配，解决单细胞RNA测序数据中时间点稀疏、快照未配对导致的轨迹推断和长时程预测分布漂移问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于最优传输的方法仅提供分布级匹配，连续时间生成模型面临局部过渡模糊和长期预测误差累积导致的分布漂移问题。

Method: (1) 使用熵正则化OT耦合构建软加权流匹配目标学习速度场；(2) 学习双向速度场并通过一致性约束优化耦合；(3) 引入分布级对齐和潜在动态正则化抑制长程预测漂移。

Result: 在真实时间序列scRNA-seq数据集上，scFM在时间插值和外推的分布预测性能上均有提升，轨迹重建更准确，中间缺失时间点的可视化更连贯。

Conclusion: scFM通过耦合条件流匹配框架有效缓解了稀疏快照下的轨迹推断困难，更忠实地恢复了潜在的时间基因表达动态。

Abstract: Single-cell RNA sequencing (scRNA-seq) provides high-dimensional profiles of cellular states, enabling data-driven modeling of cellular dynamics over time. In practice, time-resolved scRNA-seq is collected at only a few discrete time points as unpaired snapshot populations, leaving substantial temporal gaps. This motivates trajectory inference at unmeasured time points. Existing methods mainly follow two directions, optimal-transport (OT) alignment provides distribution-level matching between observed snapshots, while continuous-time generative models support forecasting via learned dynamics. However, two challenges remain: (i) unpaired snapshots render local transitions between adjacent time points ambiguous, leading to unstable supervision; and (ii) long-horizon prediction relies on repeated integration, where small modeling errors compound and cause distribution drift. To address these challenges, we propose single-cell Flow Matching (scFM), a latent generative framework based on coupling-conditioned flow matching. First, we compute entropically regularized OT couplings between adjacent snapshots and use them to construct soft, weighted flow-matching targets for learning time-dependent velocity fields. Second, we learn bidirectional velocity fields and leverage their consistency to refine couplings and improve temporal coherence under sparse supervision. Third, we introduce distribution-level alignment and latent dynamic regularization to anchor long rollouts and mitigate drift. Experiments on real-world time-series scRNA-seq datasets show that scFM consistently improves distributional prediction performance for both temporal interpolation and extrapolation. Moreover, scFM yields more accurate trajectory reconstruction and temporally coherent visualizations where intermediate time points are absent, indicating a more faithful recovery of underlying temporal gene expression dynamics.

</details>


### [141] [ASAP: Attention Sink Anchored Pruning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22372)
*Jaehyuk Lee,Hanyoung Kim,Yanggee Kim,Donghun Lee*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: ASAP利用注意力汇（attention sink）现象，通过将汇点视为特征并基于扩散距离进行令牌剪枝，在无需训练的情况下加速视觉Transformer，最高提升48%吞吐量且不损失精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有令牌缩减方法依赖局部指标（如单层注意力分数），易受注意力汇现象影响——无信息令牌反而被保留，显著前景对象被剪除，导致性能下降。

Method: 将ViT信息流建模为惰性随机游走，识别注意力汇作为概率质量的累积器；计算令牌到汇点的扩散距离，通过径向扩散聚类划分令牌，再通过转移权重池化压缩背景冗余。

Result: 在图像、视频和视觉-语言任务上，ASAP优于现有方法，吞吐量提升高达48%，同时维持甚至超越基线准确率。

Conclusion: ASAP证明注意力汇可被逆向利用为有效特征进行令牌剪枝，是一种训练免费、即插即用的高效ViT加速方案。

Abstract: Vision Transformers (ViTs) face severe computational bottlenecks due to the quadratic complexity of self-attention at high resolutions. Existing token reduction methods rely on local metrics - such as single-layer attention scores - that are inherently vulnerable to the attention sink phenomenon, where uninformative tokens are paradoxically preserved over salient foreground objects. We propose ASAP (Attention Sink Anchored Pruning), a training-free framework that recasts this sink as a feature. Modeling ViT information flow as a Lazy Random Walk, ASAP identifies the sink as a dominant accumulator of probability mass. By computing the diffusion distance to the sink within the cumulative transition matrix, ASAP partitions tokens via Radial Diffusion Clustering and compresses background redundancy through Transition Weight Pooling in a single shot. Extensive experiments across image, video, and vision-language tasks demonstrate ASAP outperforms state-of-the-art methods, accelerating throughput by up to 48% while maintaining - or even exceeding - baseline accuracy.

</details>


### [142] [Boundary-targeted Membership Inference Attacks on Safety Classifiers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22373)
*Anthony Hughes,Alexander Goldberg,Prince Jha,Adam Perer,Nikolaos Aletras,Niloofar Mireshghallah*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于边界定位的选择策略，通过识别分类器低置信度的样本，显著提升成员推理攻击（MIA）的效果，在情感支持检测任务中以5%假阳性率恢复了19%被标记为高风险的用户对话，比现有最先进MIA方法高出3.5倍，并发现基于内容的过滤无法有效防御，而噪声策略可缓解此类攻击。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 安全分类器虽被广泛用于过滤有害内容或识别高风险用户，但其训练数据包含涉及自残、心理健康等敏感对话，存在严重的隐私泄露风险。现有成员推理攻击方法效率有限，本文旨在探究如何利用分类器决策边界附近的低置信度样本来放大成员推理信号，从而揭示隐私脆弱性。

Method: 提出边界定位选择策略（boundary-targeted selection strategy），识别分类器对哪些训练样本预测置信度最低，假设这些样本因依赖记忆化（memorization）解决歧义而导致泛化失败，从而泄漏成员信息。并在情感支持检测分类器上进行实证评估。

Result: 在5%假阳性率下，攻击者成功恢复19%被标记为用户痛苦的对话，性能是现有最先进MIA方法的3.5倍。基于内容的过滤无法有效防御此类攻击，而噪声策略可有效缓解模型的易感性。

Conclusion: 安全分类器在决策边界附近的低置信度样本是成员推理攻击的主要脆弱点，现有内容过滤防御无效，噪声扰动是有效的缓解手段。该发现对部署隐私安全的AI系统具有重要警示意义。

Abstract: Safety classifiers are essential safeguards within generative AI systems, filtering harmful content or identifying at-risk users when interacting with large language models. Despite their necessity, these models are trained on sensitive datasets including discussions of self-harm and mental health, raising important, yet poorly understood, privacy concerns. Membership inference attacks (MIAs) allow adversaries to infer membership of examples used to train models. In this work, we hypothesize that identifying the examples on which the classifier is least confident are informative for an adversary to infer membership. This reflects a localized failure of generalization, where the model relies on memorization to resolve ambiguity in the training set. To investigate this, we introduce a new boundary-targeted selection strategy that identifies low confidence examples that amplify the signal of an examples membership within a training set. Our experimental results show that an adversary can recover 19\% of the conversations a safety classifier flagged as indicating user distress, at a 5\% false-positive rate, on a classifier fine-tuned for detecting a user who may require emotional support. This is $3.5$ times more than attacking using state-of-the-art MIA methods alone. Finally, we characterize the boundary laying examples and show that content-based filtering is ineffective for protection, and existing noise strategies can effectively mitigate susceptibility of these examples.

</details>


### [143] [Target-Aligned Bellman Backup for Cross-domain Offline Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22376)
*Wei Liu,Ting Long*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Target-Aligned Bellman Backup (TABB)方法，通过基于贝尔曼目标对齐而非表层转移相似性来评估源域数据的可迁移性，从而在跨域离线强化学习中更有效地利用源域数据，在目标域数据极度有限的情况下取得了优异性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有跨域离线强化学习方法基于转移相似性（transition-level similarity）来选择源域数据，但转移层面的相似性并不保证长期回报的一致性，即使视觉或动态上相似的转移也可能在目标域中导致截然不同的结果，从而误导策略学习。

Method: 提出TABB方法，通过衡量源域转移对目标域贝尔曼目标（Bellman target）准确估计的贡献程度来评估其可迁移性，而非依赖表面的转移相似性，从而有选择性地利用源域数据。

Result: 在多种跨域离线RL设置、目标域数据极度有限的条件下，TABB方法持续取得了强性能表现。

Conclusion: 基于贝尔曼目标对齐的源域数据选择策略比基于转移相似性的方法更有效，能更好地促进跨域策略学习。

Abstract: Cross-domain offline reinforcement learning (CDRL) aims to improve policy learning in a target domain by leveraging data collected from a source domain. Existing works typically assess the transferability of source-domain data by measuring its similarity to target-domain transitions, and implicitly perform transition-level selection. Transitions that are considered similar are assigned higher weights or rewards, while dissimilar ones are down-weighted. However, transition-level similarity does not necessarily imply consistency in long-term returns. Even visually or dynamically similar transitions may lead to significantly different outcomes in the target domain, which can mislead policy learning and degrade performance. To address this issue, we revisit the fundamental objective of policy learning. Since policy optimization ultimately relies on Bellman targets to evaluate the quality of decisions, we propose to assess the transferability of source-domain transitions based on their alignment with target-domain Bellman targets, rather than superficial transition similarity. Based on this insight, we propose a method termed Target-Aligned Bellman Backup (TABB), which selectively leverages source-domain data by measuring their contribution to accurate Bellman target estimation in the target domain. We evaluate TABB across a broad range of cross-domain offline RL settings with highly limited target-domain data. Experimental results show that TABB consistently achieves strong performance.

</details>


### [144] [Hybrid Kolmogorov-Arnold Network and XGBoost Framework for Week-Ahead Price Forecasting in Australia's National Electricity Market](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22387)
*Houxuan Zhou,Sriram Prasad,Chenghao Huang,Jiajie Feng,Hao Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种混合KAN+XGBoost框架用于周前电力价格预测，在澳大利亚国家电力市场（NEM）数据上，相比SARIMAX、LSTM、单一KAN和XGBoost等基准方法，MAE降低约12%（对比XGBoost）且超过50%（对比朴素基线），验证了混合学习策略在高动态电力市场中的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 电力价格预测因强波动性、非线性动力学和频繁极端价格尖峰而极具挑战性，尤其是在高可再生能源渗透率增加不确定性的澳大利亚NEM市场中，现有方法难以同时捕捉长期依赖和短期波动。

Method: 提出混合KAN+XGBoost框架，结合Kolmogorov-Arnold Networks（KAN）的全局非线性表示能力与XGBoost的局部鲁棒性，采用扩展窗口评估策略在真实NEM数据上进行周前预测。

Result: 所提模型在MAE指标上优于SARIMAX、LSTM、单一KAN和XGBoost等基准方法，相比XGBoost降低约12%，相比朴素基线降低超过50%。

Conclusion: 混合学习策略为高度动态的电力市场价格预测提供了有效且鲁棒的解决方案。

Abstract: Accurate electricity price forecasting (EPF) is essential for market participants to support operational planning and risk management, yet remains challenging due to strong volatility, nonlinear dynamics, and frequent extreme price spikes. These challenges are particularly pronounced in the Australian National Electricity Market (NEM), where high renewable penetration further increases uncertainty. This paper investigates week-ahead electricity price forecasting and proposes a hybrid KAN+XGBoost framework that integrates Kolmogorov-Arnold Networks (KAN) with tree-based learning. The proposed approach combines the global nonlinear representation capability of KAN with the local robustness of XGBoost to capture both long-term dependencies and short-term price fluctuations. Experiments are conducted on real-world NEM data using an expanding window evaluation strategy. The results demonstrate that the proposed model outperforms benchmark methods, including SARIMAX, Long Short-Term Memory (LSTM), standalone KAN, and XGBoost, reducing MAE by approximately 12% compared to XGBoost and by over 50% compared to a naive baseline. The results suggest that hybrid learning strategies provide an effective and robust solution for electricity price forecasting in highly dynamic electricity markets.

</details>


### [145] [AMUSE: Anytime Muon with Stable Gradient Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22432)
*Jueun Kim,Baekrok Shin,Jihun Yun,Beomhan Baek,Minhak Song,Chulhee Yun*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 现代深度学习常用AdamW配合学习率调度，但近期工作同时挑战这两者：Schedule-Free通过迭代平均消除调度，Muon通过正交化动量改进更新几何。本文研究Muon在河-谷损失景观中的行为，发现其正交化加速河床进度但放大噪声。据此提出AMUSE，结合Muon快速进度和Schedule-Free稳定效果，通过时变插值系数实现无调度且随时可中断的训练，在视觉任务和大语言模型预训练中持续改进性能-迭代帕累托前沿。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Muon优化器虽表现强但机制尚未完全理解，本文旨在剖析其在河-谷损失景观中的行为机理，并基于此设计更好的优化方法。

Method: 通过河-谷损失景观分析Muon的优化行为，发现其正交化加速河床（低曲率子空间）进度但放大主导方向噪声。提出AMUSE，利用时变插值系数在快速Muon序列和稳定平均序列之间动态过渡：初始靠近Muon序列实现快速适应，逐步转向平均序列抑制振荡。

Result: AMUSE无需学习率调度，支持随时中断训练。在视觉任务和大语言模型预训练中，持续改进性能-迭代帕累托前沿，优于(Schedule-Free) AdamW和Muon。

Conclusion: Muon的加速来源于河床子空间进度的提升，但会引入振荡噪声；AMUSE通过结合Muon的快速性与Schedule-Free的稳定性，实现了无调度、随时可中断的高效训练。
