TL;DR: 提出 Patches-to-Trajectories (P2T) 方法，利用开发者编写的参考补丁 $p^\star$ 作为特权信息，通过逆向阶段将 $p^\star$ 蒸馏为潜在过程图 $G^\star$，再通过前向阶段基于 $G^\star$ 对教师轨迹进行逐步骤有效性评分和长度优化，从而在 SWE 智能体微调中生成更高效、更有效的训练轨迹。仅用 1.8k 实例，P2T 在 SWE-bench Verified 上 Pass@1 提升 10.8 点，推理成本降低约 15%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有监督微调方法使用二元终端验证器过滤教师轨迹，无法直接优化每个步骤的 grounding 和效率，且对教师失败的实例缺乏监督信号。

Method: P2T 包含两个阶段：(1) 逆向阶段，将参考补丁 $p^\star$ 蒸馏为潜在过程图 $G^\star$（包含上下文事实和解决方案里程碑）；(2) 前向阶段，在屏蔽未来信息的条件下，基于 $G^\star$ 对教师轨迹的每一步进行 grounding 检查和进度评分，保留最短有效片段。

Result: 在 SWE-bench Verified 上 Pass@1 提升 10.8 点，每个实例推理成本降低约 15%；在 SWE-bench Lite 上也有稳定提升。消融实验和定性分析隔离了轨迹质量与数据规模的影响。

Conclusion: 利用参考补丁作为特权信息进行轨迹级优化，可以在更少数据下获得更好效果和效率，超越基于结果过滤的 SFT 方法。

Abstract: Supervised fine-tuning (SFT) on long teacher trajectories is the dominant way to instill investigation and reasoning in open software-engineering (SWE) agents. Since every retained response becomes an imitation target, the student inherits the final outcome and intermediate flaws, including ungrounded leaps and redundant loops. High-quality training data must be effective(each step is grounded and narrows the agent's epistemic gap to the correct fix) and efficient(each step is information-bearing rather than redundant or looping). Existing recipes filter or relabel teacher rollouts using only a binary terminal verifier, which does not directly target these axes and provides no supervision on instances where the teacher fails.
  Most real issue includes a developer-authored reference patch, $p^\star$, revealing the file paths, runtime behaviors, and coding conventions presupposed by the correct fix, yet standard pipelines discard it. We propose Patches-to-Trajectories (P2T), which uses $p^\star$ as privileged information during curation and formulates trajectory construction as bi-objective optimization over per-step effectiveness and trajectory length. A reverse phase distills $p^\star$ into a latent process graph, $G^\star$, of contextual facts and solution milestones. A forward phase curates trajectories from blinded teacher continuations by scoring per-step progress against $G^\star$ under a leakage-blocking groundedness check and retaining the shortest effective segments.
  Using only 1.8k curated SWE-Gym instances, P2T improves effectiveness and efficiency over outcome-filtered SFT and its tool-error-masking variant. On SWE-bench Verified, it raises Pass@1 by up to 10.8 points while reducing per-instance inference cost by ~15%, with consistent gains on SWE-bench Lite. Size-matched ablations and qualitative analysis further isolate trajectory quality from data scale.

</details>


### [182] [Finding Missing Input Validation in TEEs via LLM-Assisted Symbolic Execution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22058)
*Chengyan Ma,Jieke Shi,Ruidong Han,Ye Liu,Yuqing Niu,David Lo*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: SymTEE提出了一种LLM辅助的符号执行框架，无需真实TEE环境即可检测TEE应用中的输入验证缺失漏洞，在26个漏洞上达到100%精度和92.3%召回率，平均分析成本仅0.05美元。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: TEE应用分析面临两大挑战：配置完整TEE构建和运行环境的高成本与复杂性，以及硬件隔离带来的有限可观测性。现有方法难以高效检测TEE应用中的输入验证缺失问题。

Method: 首先利用AST分析提取可能缺乏输入验证的TEE代码切片，然后使用LLM（GPT-5）自动将提取的代码切片转换为KLEE兼容的harness程序，其中包含轻量级模拟执行环境用于符号分析。

Result: 在26个漏洞（11个真实世界+15个合成）上评估，SymTEE达到100%精度和92.3%召回率，平均分析成本仅0.05美元。

Conclusion: SymTEE开创了LLM辅助符号执行的新范式，通过LLM自主生成模拟环境实现无需复杂配置的自动化安全分析，为可信计算系统提供了更易用、可扩展的漏洞检测框架。

Abstract: Trusted Execution Environments (TEEs) provide hardware-enforced isolation that protects sensitive code and data from untrusted software. Despite their strong security guarantees, analyzing TEE applications remains challenging due to the high cost and complexity of configuring complete TEE build and runtime environments, as well as the limited observability imposed by hardware isolation. This paper presents SymTEE, a novel large language model (LLM)-assisted symbolic execution framework for detecting missing input validation issues in TEE applications without requiring real TEE setups. SymTEE begins by leveraging Abstract Syntax Tree (AST) analysis to extract TEE code slices that may lack sufficient input validation, and then employs an LLM (GPT-5 in our case) to automatically convert the extracted slices into KLEE-compatible harness programs containing lightweight mock execution environments for symbolic analysis. Evaluations on 26 vulnerabilities (11 real-world and 15 synthetic) show that SymTEE achieves 100% precision and 92.3% recall in detecting missing input validation vulnerabilities while incurring an average analysis cost of only $0.05. These results demonstrate the effectiveness and practicality of SymTEE's pioneering paradigm of LLM-assisted symbolic execution, where LLMs autonomously generate mock environments to enable automated security analysis without complex setup, providing a more accessible and scalable framework for trusted computing systems.

</details>


### [183] [Automated Repair of TEE Partitioning Issues via DSL-Guided and LLM-Assisted Patching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22087)
*Chengyan Ma,Jieke Shi,Ruidong Han,Ye Liu,Feng Li,Yuqing Niu,David Lo*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: TEERepair是一个自动化修复TEE应用中不良分区问题的框架，通过领域特定语言编码修复规则、利用大语言模型合成上下文感知补丁并生成测试客户端验证修复，在基准测试中达到87.6%的修复成功率，已有2个补丁被真实项目维护者合并。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: TEE应用通过SDK接口与不可信操作系统交互时，不当分区会导致数据泄露和代码注入等严重漏洞。现有工作主要集中于静态分析检测，自动化修复仍未被充分探索。

Method: 提出TEERepair框架：1) 设计领域特定语言(DSL)编码修复规则，表达常见TEE安全模式并实例化为带有上下文变量占位符的补丁模板；2) 利用大语言模型推理代码语义，合成上下文感知补丁；3) 生成测试客户端验证修复。

Result: 在TEE分区错误基准测试(PartitioningE-Bench)上达到87.6%的修复成功率，显著优于基线方法；应用于真实TEE项目提交5个修复PR，其中2个已被确认并合并。

Conclusion: TEERepair有效解决了TEE应用中不良分区问题的自动修复，通过DSL编码安全模式结合LLM语义推理的方法在修复成功率和实际应用效果上均表现出色。

Abstract: Trusted Execution Environments (TEEs) provide hardware-based isolation to protect sensitive data and computations from potentially compromised operating systems (OS). However, TEE applications inevitably interact with the untrusted OS through SDK interfaces, and improper partitioning can introduce severe vulnerabilities such as data leakage and code injection. While prior work has proposed static analysis tools to detect such issues, automated repair remains largely unexplored. This problem is particularly challenging due to three TEE-specific factors: the lack of standardized secure development guidelines, the difficulty of extracting semantic information from low-level C code, and the absence of mature testing and validation methods. In this work, we present TEERepair, a framework for automatically repairing bad partitioning issues in TEE applications. Our approach tackles the above challenges by introducing a domain-specific language (DSL) to encode repair rules that express and capture common TEE security patterns, which are instantiated as patch templates with placeholders for context-specific variables. We then leverage large language models (LLMs) to reason about code semantics and synthesize context-aware patches, and further generate test clients to validate the repairs. We evaluate TEERepair on the TEE Partitioning Errors Benchmark (PartitioningE-Bench), achieving a significantly higher repair success rate of 87.6% compared to baselines. Furthermore, applying TEERepair to real-world TEE projects, we submitted 5 repair pull requests, 2 of which have been confirmed and merged by project maintainers.

</details>


### [184] [An Architecture for Decentralised Deployment and Operation of Blockchain Applications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22239)
*Fabian Stiehle,Kirill Inozemtsev,Ingo Weber*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种将DevOps实践与去中心化自治组织（DAO）治理架构相集成的智能合约部署框架，通过扩展注册表模式支持确定性部署，并提供了包含集成部署流水线、用户界面和版本控制的开源参考实现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 区块链应用开发者依赖自动化测试和持续集成部署等DevOps实践来确保智能合约的正确性，但这些基础设施通常由单一实体控制；对于大型区块链应用，虽然可以通过DAO实现去中心化治理，但治理架构复杂且需要与合约发现和升级模式集成，现有方案缺乏统一的、与具体治理和升级实现无关的架构。

Method: 1) 将DevOps最佳实践与DAO治理概念集成到一个新型架构中，保持对不同治理和升级实现的不可知性；2) 扩展已知的注册表模式以支持确定性部署；3) 提出去中心化部署框架，包含集成与部署管道、用户界面和版本控制集成；4) 各方在使用新部署的智能合约前实现并验证自己的测试；5) 提供开源参考实现。

Result: 提出了一个完整的去中心化部署架构和开源参考实现，该架构能够作为未来集成的参考，其开源框架旨在降低在实践中采用此类流程的复杂性。

Conclusion: 该架构为去中心化应用开发中的DevOps与DAO治理集成提供了可行的参考方案，通过保持对具体实现的不可知性提高了通用性，有助于降低实际采用门槛。

Abstract: Blockchains and distributed ledger technologies allow the operation of manifold decentralised applications (dApps). Such applications are based on smart contracts, a programmable abstraction that is executed in a decentralised manner. To ensure the correctness of smart contracts, blockchain application developers rely on DevOps practices such as automated testing and continuous integration and deployment. However, such infrastructure is often controlled by single entities. For larger blockchain applications, this issue is resolved by relying on concepts of Decentralised Autonomous Organisations (DAOs), which allow proposals to be autonomously executed once they reach a pre-defined quorum. Such a governance architecture is complex and requires integration with existing patterns for contract discovery and upgradeability. In this paper we integrate these concepts considering DevOps best-practices into a novel architecture that remains agnostic to different governance and upgrade implementations. We extend the known registry pattern to support deterministic deployments and present a decentralised deployment framework, including integration and deployment pipelines, user-interfaces, and version control integration. In our approach, each party implements and verifies their own tests before engaging in the use of a (newly deployed) smart contract. We provide a reference implementation, available as open-source, and evaluate the proposal thoroughly. Our architecture can serve as a reference for future integrations, while our open-source framework is aimed at reducing the complexity of adopting such a process in practice.

</details>


### [185] [At What Cost? Software Developers' Well-Being in the Age of GenAI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22349)
*Mariam Guizani,Maduka Subasinghage,Sherlock A. Licorish,Sofia Ouhbi*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 生成式人工智能（GenAI）正快速重塑软件开发，但过度关注生产力与性能优化可能忽视开发者福祉。本文呼吁将GenAI研究议程重新聚焦于人类经验、社会情境与可持续生产力，并提出了一个理论框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GenAI在软件开发中日益普及，但现有研究过度强调效率与性能指标，忽略了其对开发者认知负荷、监督劳动、工作压力、职业倦怠及工作生活平衡的负面影响，以及由此引发的职业规范转变和机会不平等问题。

Method: 本文采用立场论文（position paper）形式，通过理论分析提出一个重定向GenAI研究议程的理论框架，将关注点从狭隘的性能指标扩展到人类体验、社会背景与可持续生产力。

Result: 识别出GenAI工具加剧认知负荷、引入新型监督劳动、提高产出与节奏期望、导致压力与倦怠、改变职业规范、弱化工作生活平衡、加深获取与支持不平等等多重负面影响。

Conclusion: 软件开发领域的GenAI研究应超越单纯的生产力与性能指标，转向以人为本的研究议程，重视开发者福祉、社会公平与长期可持续的生产力。

Abstract: Generative Artificial Intelligence (GenAI) is rapidly reshaping software development, with growing emphasis on accelerating productivity and optimizing performance. However, excessive focus on such dimensions risks overlooking the critical implications for developer well-being. GenAI tools can amplify cognitive load, introduce new forms of oversight labor, and escalate expectations around output and pace, contributing to stress, burnout, and diminished work-life balance. The GenAI movement is also transforming professional norms, altering career entry points, demanding continuous adaptation, and deepening inequalities in access and support. This position paper calls for a reorientation of the GenAI research agenda in software development and proposes a theoretical framework to move beyond narrow performance metrics toward investigations that also center on human experience, social context, and sustainable productivity.

</details>


### [186] ["Refactoring Runaway": Understanding and Mitigating Tangled Refactorings in Coding Agents for Issue Resolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22526)
*Zhao Tian,Zifan Zhang,Tao Xiao,Dong Wang,Masanari Kondo,Junjie Chen,Yasutaka Kamei*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文研究了代码智能体在软件问题修复中引入的缠结性重构（tangled refactoring）现象。通过对Multi-SWE-bench上3,691个补丁的分析，发现代码智能体比人类开发者引入缠结重构的频率更低（21.43% vs 36.72%）且强度更小（0.66 vs 1.75），但重构类型更多样。逻辑回归分析表明缠结重构与可编译性降低强相关，但与功能正确性无显著关联。据此提出一种重构感知的优化方法，将可编译性从19.34%提升至38.33%，并额外解决了2.79%之前未解决的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实中的软件问题修复常伴随开发者的重构行为，形成"缠结重构"（tangled refactoring）。代码智能体基于LLM训练于开源仓库，可能继承这一行为。然而，缠结重构对补丁质量（如可编译性、功能正确性）的影响尚不明确，且缺乏针对性的优化方法。

Method: 在Multi-SWE-bench数据集上，对3个智能体框架（使用12种LLM）生成的3,691个有效补丁进行实证分析，比较人类与智能体在缠结重构频率、强度和类型多样性上的差异。使用逻辑回归分析缠结重构与可编译性、功能正确性的关联。提出重构感知的优化方法，评估缠结重构的必要性和安全性，选择性移除或修复有问题的重构。

Result: 1）智能体缠结重构频率（21.43%）和强度（0.66）均低于人类（36.72%, 1.75），但重构类型更多样。2）缠结重构与可编译性显著负相关，与功能正确性无显著关联。3）所提方法将可编译性从19.34%提升至38.33%，额外解决2.79%的之前未解决问题。

Conclusion: 该工作首次系统性地研究了代码智能体在问题修复中的缠结重构行为，揭示了其与人类开发者的差异及对补丁质量的影响，并提出有效的优化方法，为未来智能体代码修复的改进提供了方向。

Abstract: Recent advances in coding agents have shown remarkable progress in software issue resolution. In practice, real-world issues are typically bug fixes or feature requests in which human developers naturally incorporate refactoring as part of the resolution process, resulting in tangled refactoring. Since LLMs are trained on large-scale open-source repositories, coding agents may inherit such behaviors. In this paper, we conduct an empirical study on Multi-SWE-bench, analyzing 3,691 valid patches generated by three agent frameworks with 12 LLMs. We find that coding agents introduce tangled refactorings less frequently (21.43% vs. 36.72%) and with lower intensity (0.66 vs. 1.75) than human developers, although they exhibit a broader diversity of refactoring types. Logistic regression analysis further shows that tangled refactorings are strongly associated with reduced compilability, while exhibiting no significant association with functional correctness. Based on these findings, we propose a refactoring-aware refinement approach that assesses the necessity and safety of tangled refactorings and selectively removes or repairs problematic operations. Our approach improves compilability from 19.34% to 38.33%, and additionally resolves 2.79% previously unresolved issues. Overall, this work presents the first step towards understanding tangled refactoring practices in agentic issue resolution and opens up avenues for future work.

</details>


### [187] [Contractual Skills: A GovernSpec Design Framework for Enterprise AI Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22634)
*Ting Liu*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出了"契约技能"（Contractual Skills）框架，将SKILL.md文件组织为可读的任务契约，明确任务目标、边界、权限、质量标准等要素。实验表明，该框架相比无技能和最小技能基线在所有测试模型上表现更好，但相对于信息丰富的纯扩展技能提升有限，主要改进可检查性和可维护性而非生成质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 企业场景中需要技能不仅表达任务指导，还需使目标、输入边界、权限、证据要求、输出契约、质量标准、验证步骤、人工审批点和交接规则可审查。现有技能组织方式无法满足这些可检查性需求。

Method: 提出受GovernSpec启发的契约技能设计框架，将SKILL.md文件组织为可读任务契约，同时保持轻量级技能发现和渐进式加载。通过两个离线实验评估：文本生成研究涵盖3个企业技能、15个合成任务、4种指令条件和8个生成模型（960个输出，1680条交叉评分记录）；工具调用挑战涵盖8个模型和192条模拟工具调用记录。

Result: 契约技能在所有测试模型上优于无技能和最小技能基线。相对于信息丰富的纯扩展技能，提升较小且不稳定。技能通常能减少高风险的工貝调用尝试，但模型差异依然存在，仍需要运行时工貝护栏。

Conclusion: 契约技能最适合被理解为一种治理层（governance layer），使任务意图、边界和验收标准显式化，而非独立的安全机制。

Abstract: Skills are increasingly used to package agent instructions, workflows, scripts, and reference materials. In enterprise settings, however, skills often need to express more than task guidance: they must make goals, input boundaries, permissions, evidence requirements, output contracts, quality criteria, verification steps, human approval points, and handoff rules inspectable. This paper proposes contractual skills, a GovernSpec-inspired design framework for organizing SKILL.md files as readable task contracts while preserving lightweight skill discovery and progressive loading. The framework clarifies the boundary between contractual skills, GovernSpec YAML contracts, Model Context Protocol surfaces, tool adapters, runtime guardrails, tracing, and evaluation systems.
  We evaluate the framework with two offline experiments. A text-generation study covers three enterprise skills, fifteen synthetic tasks, four instruction conditions, and eight generation models, yielding 960 outputs and 1680 cross-judge score records. Contractual skills outperform no-skill and minimal-skill baselines on all tested models. Relative to information-rich plain expanded skills, the gains are small and mixed, suggesting that contractual fields mainly improve checkability and maintainability rather than raw generation quality. A tool-calling challenge covers eight models and 192 simulated tool-call records. Skills usually reduce high-risk tool attempts, but model differences remain and runtime tool guardrails are still required. The results suggest that contractual skills are best understood as a governance layer that makes task intent, boundaries, and acceptance criteria explicit, not as a standalone safety mechanism.

</details>


### [188] [FAME: Failure-Aware Mixture-of-Experts for Message-Level Log Anomaly Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22779)
*Huanchi Wang,Zihang Huang,Yifang Tian,Kristina Dzeparoska,Hans-Arno Jacobsen,Alberto Leon-Garcia*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: FAME是一个标签高效的日志异常检测框架，使用混合专家模型在消息级别进行细粒度检测，仅需离线使用一次LLM，在BGL和Thunderbird数据集上取得了高F1分数，大幅降低了标注成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有日志异常检测方法大多在会话或窗口级别进行检测，只能标记整组日志行，无法精确定位导致异常的特定消息。运维人员需要检查大量常规日志行，效率低下。消息级检测虽理想但面临挑战：同一事件模板可能对应正常和异常消息、故障来自异构子系统、大规模行级标注不现实。直接应用LLM逐行检测成本过高。

Method: FAME包含离线阶段和在线阶段。离线阶段：使用LLM对每个模板最多标注K条样本生成正常/异常指示器和代表性示例，LLM提出模板到故障域的分区方案，通过认证步骤验证分区有效性，然后训练轻量级路由器和领域专家。在线阶段：轻量级路由器和领域专家在本地运行，输出异常预测和故障域标签。

Result: 在BGL数据集上，K=100时F1=98.16，标注量减少76倍；检测到86.3%来自未见过的EventID的异常。在Thunderbird数据集上，F1=99.95，召回率完美。

Conclusion: FAME有效解决了消息级日志异常检测的挑战，通过标签高效的混合专家框架实现了细粒度、低成本的异常定位，且具备对未见事件类型的泛化能力。

Abstract: Production systems generate millions of log lines daily, yet most anomaly detectors operate at the session or window-level, flagging groups of lines rather than identifying the specific message responsible. This coarse granularity forces operators to inspect many routine lines per alert. Message-level detection offers finer granularity, but remains challenging. A single event template may correspond to both normal and anomalous messages, failures arise from heterogeneous subsystems, and line-level labeling at scale is impractical. Although large language models (LLMs) can reason over log semantics, applying them to every line is too costly for continuous monitoring. We present FAME (Failure-Aware Mixture-of-Experts), a label-efficient message-level mixture-of-experts framework that uses an LLM only once offline. We annotate at most K labeled lines per template to derive binary normal/anomaly indicators and representative examples. The LLM proposes a partition of templates into failure domains, and a certification step validates the proposal before training. FAME trains a lightweight router and domain experts that run on-premise and output anomaly predictions and failure-domain labels. On BGL, FAME achieves F1 = 98.16 at K = 100 reducing annotation effort by 76x and detects 86.3% of anomalies from unseen EventIDs. On Thunderbird, FAME reaches F1 = 99.95 with perfect recall.

</details>


<div id='cs.MA'></div>

# cs.MA [[Back]](#toc)

### [189] [Argo: Efficient Importance Labeling for Enterprise Email Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21604)
*Siddhant Ray,Ganesh Ananthanarayanan,Kevin Chian,Yan Guo,Cristina St Hill,Jack W. Stokes,Victor Wang,Junchen Jiang*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 本文提出Argo框架，通过成本-质量权衡搜索和按需资源调配，在保持接近GPT-4.1标注质量的同时，实现148-167倍推理成本降低和20-640000倍配置成本降低，使大规模上下文感知的邮件重要性标注在企业场景中变得可行。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 邮件重要性标注长期依赖关键词匹配、规则和启发式方法，需要大量人工特征工程且难以泛化。虽然LLM（如GPT-4.1）在理解上下文和标注质量上表现优异，但在企业级邮件规模下计算成本过高，阻碍了实际部署。

Method: 提出Argo框架，包含两个核心组件：(1) Profiler，高效搜索标注方案的成本-质量权衡空间，识别成本效益高的替代方案；(2) 按需资源调配方案，根据实时负载智能伸缩，最小化峰值推理时的成本增长。

Result: 在3个开源邮件数据集上，Argo实现了148-167倍的推理成本降低，质量下降可忽略不计，配置成本降低20-640000倍。

Conclusion: Argo通过成本-质量权衡搜索和动态资源调配，使得大规模、上下文感知的邮件重要性标注在企业中变得实用，在保持接近GPT级别标注质量的同时大幅降低成本。

Abstract: Email importance labeling has long been a critical yet challenging problem for businesses and individuals. Traditional approaches; such as keyword matching, user-defined rules, and sender-based heuristics; demand extensive manual feature engineering and fail to scale effectively or generalize. Recent advances in large language models (LLMs) demonstrate strong potential and a natural fit for this task, offering deep contextual understanding and superior labeling quality. However, using LLM models like GPT-4.1 at enterprise email volumes incurs prohibitive computational costs and hinders real-world deployment. We explore the trade-off space of using alternative labeling schemes as opposed to GPT4.1 scale LLMs, with the goal of achieving near GPT level labeling quality with significantly lower cost. We develop Argo, an enterprise email labeling framework, where we construct a profiler to efficiently search the cost quality trade-off space of labeling and identify cost-efficient alternatives to labeling emails. Additionally, we design an on-demand provisioning scheme to intelligently scale Argo with real time load, to minimize cost increases during peak load inference. Over 3 open-source email datasets, Argo achieves 148-167X inference cost reduction with negligible quality degradation and 20-640000X lower profiling costs, making large-scale, context-aware email labeling practical for enterprises.

</details>


### [190] [Sibyl-AutoResearch: Autonomous Research Needs Self-Evolving Trial-and-Error Harnesses, Not Paper Generators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22343)
*Chengcheng Wang,Qinhua Xie,Wei He,Jianyuan Guo,Shiqi Wang,Chang Xu*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 本文提出Sibyl-AutoResearch框架，通过“科学试错机制”将试验信号转化为研究行为和系统改进，解决了当前自主研究系统缺乏研究判断力的问题。SIBYL系统实现了可审计的转换路径，回溯分析确认了8个高置信度转换事件和5类自然故障处理案例。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自主研究系统虽然实现了科学工作流的可执行化（提想法、跑代码、写论文），但缺乏真正的研究判断力：弱证据被转化为看似有力的论述，初步试错信号被夸大为广泛结论，记忆仅停留在文本层面，重复出现的流程错误无法改变后续行为。

Method: 提出Sibyl-AutoResearch框架，核心是“科学试错机制”（Scientific Trial-and-Error Harnesses），让智能体在受限范围内进行试验、保留正负结果、将经验教训路由到后续的规划、验证、主张范围界定、排期、评审、写作和机制修复等环节。形式化给出两个可审计的转换单元：试错到行为转换（trial-to-behavior conversion）和试错到机制行为转换（trial-to-harness-behavior conversion）。基于此实现SIBYL系统，采用文件存储架构，暴露状态、角色、记忆、门控和工件轨迹。

Result: 回溯审计识别出8个高置信度转换事件，中位延迟为1次迭代，最大延迟为3次迭代。故障恢复注册表显示5类自然发生的故障（重复结果、过期数值、无统计支持的统计等）被成功拦截、降级或路由到后续修复流程。

Conclusion: 所提出的转换单元在真实的自主研究工作空间中是可以恢复和追溯的。虽然未建立比较性性能优势，但证明了该框架的可审计性和故障处理能力。SIBYL系统已在GitHub开源。

Abstract: Autonomous research systems increasingly make the scientific workflow executable: agents can propose ideas, run code, inspect results, and draft papers. But executable workflows do not by themselves produce research judgment. We analyze where current systems lose trial experience: weak evidence becomes prose, pilot signals become broad claims, memory remains textual, and recurring process failures do not change later behavior. We introduce Sibyl-AutoResearch, a self-evolving AutoResearch framework built around Scientific Trial-and-Error Harnesses. A harness lets agents run bounded trials, preserve positive and negative outcomes, and route lessons into later planning, validation, claim scope, scheduling, critique, writing, and harness repair. We formalize this through two auditable conversion units: trial-to-behavior conversion, which links trial signals to later research actions, and trial-to-harness-behavior conversion, which links recurring process failures to system updates. We implement the framework in SIBYL, a file-backed autonomous research system that exposes the state, roles, memory, gates, and artifact traces needed to inspect these conversion paths. A retrospective audit identifies eight high-confidence conversion events, with a median latency of one iteration and a maximum latency of three iterations. A recovered-failure registry further shows how five naturally occurring failure classes, including duplicate results, stale numbers, and unsupported statistics, were blocked, downgraded, or routed into later repair. These traces do not establish a comparative performance claim; they show that the proposed conversion units are recoverable from realistic autonomous-research workspaces. The SIBYL framework and system are available at https://github.com/Sibyl-Research-Team/AutoResearch-SibylSystem.

</details>


### [191] [Self-Evolving Multi-Agent Systems via Decentralized Memory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22721)
*Guangya Hao,Yunbo Long,Zhuokai Zhao*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 提出DecentMem，一种去中心化记忆框架，每个LLM智能体维护独立双池记忆（利用池+探索池），通过在线重权重和LLM-as-a-judge反馈，在理论保证O(log T)累积遗憾的同时，在实际多智能体系统和基准测试中提升准确率最高达23.8%，降低token消耗最高达49%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自进化多智能体系统几乎全部采用集中式记忆库，导致通信协调开销大、隐私风险高、损害智能体多样性，亟需去中心化记忆方案。

Method: 每个智能体维护双池记忆：利用池（历史轨迹）和探索池（LLM生成的未见上下文候选）；基于LLM-as-a-judge的阶段性反馈在线重权重两个池；理论上证明可保证解空间全局可达性并达到O(log T)累积遗憾。

Result: 在3种MAS框架（AutoGen, DyLAN, AgentNet）、3种Qwen3骨干（4B/8B/14B）、2种Gemma4骨干（E2B/E4B）及5个基准（数学、代码、QA、具身任务）上，相比最强集中式记忆基线平均准确率提升最高23.8%，相比无记忆基线提升最高52.5%，token使用量降低最高49%。

Conclusion: 去中心化双池记忆机制在保持理论最优性的同时，实际性能显著优于集中式记忆方案，为自进化多智能体系统提供了更高效、更隐私友好的记忆设计范式。

Abstract: Self-evolving multi-agent systems (MAS) have emerged as a promising route to LLM agents that continually improve from experience, with persistent memory at their foundation. However, existing designs almost exclusively adopt a centralized repository shared across agents, incurring communication and coordination overhead, raising privacy concerns, and collapsing agent diversity. We propose DecentMem, a decentralized memory framework in which each agent maintains its own dual-pool memory -- an exploitation pool of consolidated past trajectories and an exploration pool of LLM-generated candidates for unseen contexts. The two pools are reweighted online based on stage-wise feedback from an LLM-as-a-judge. Theoretically, we prove that this design guarantees global reachability of the solution space and achieves $O(\log T)$ cumulative regret, matching the stochastic bandit lower bound up to constants. In practice, across three MAS frameworks (AutoGen, DyLAN, AgentNet), three Qwen3 backbones (4B/8B/14B), two Gemma4 backbones (E2B/E4B) and five benchmarks spanning math, code, QA, and embodied tasks, DecentMem improves average accuracy by up to 23.8% over the strongest centralized memory baseline and by up to 52.5% over the no-memory baseline, while reducing token usage by up to 49%.

</details>
